class AirPressureField {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.time = 0;

        this.spatialScale = options.spatialScale ?? 100000;

        this.gradientStep = options.gradientStep ?? 5000;

        this.windStrength = options.windStrength ?? 0.85;

        this.maxWindSpeed = options.maxWindSpeed ?? 1.1;

        this.prevailingWind = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            0.28,
            0,
            0.12
        );

        this.wind = new BABYLON.Vector3();
    }

    update(deltaTime) {
        this.time += Math.max(0, deltaTime);
    }

    pressureAt(x, z, time = this.time) {
        const scale = this.spatialScale;

        const px = x / scale;
        const pz = z / scale;

        const wave1 = Math.sin(
            px * 0.85 +
            pz * 0.38 +
            time * 0.018
        );

        const wave2 = Math.cos(
            pz * 0.72 -
            px * 0.31 -
            time * 0.012
        );

        const wave3 = Math.sin(
            (px + pz) * 0.42 +
            time * 0.009
        );

        return (
            wave1 * 0.42 +
            wave2 * 0.34 +
            wave3 * 0.24
        );
    }

    sample(position, time = this.time) {
        const x = position.x;
        const z = position.z;
        const h = this.gradientStep;

        const pressure = this.pressureAt(
            x,
            z,
            time
        );

        // Estimate the horizontal pressure gradient.
        const gradientX = (
            this.pressureAt(x + h, z, time) -
            this.pressureAt(x - h, z, time)
        ) / (2 * h);

        const gradientZ = (
            this.pressureAt(x, z + h, time) -
            this.pressureAt(x, z - h, time)
        ) / (2 * h);

        // Air flows approximately from higher pressure
        // toward lower pressure.
        let windX =
            -gradientX *
            this.spatialScale *
            this.windStrength;

        let windZ =
            -gradientZ *
            this.spatialScale *
            this.windStrength;

        windX += this.prevailingWind.x;
        windZ += this.prevailingWind.z;

        const magnitude = Math.hypot(
            windX,
            windZ
        );

        if (magnitude > this.maxWindSpeed) {
            const factor =
                this.maxWindSpeed / magnitude;

            windX *= factor;
            windZ *= factor;
        }

        this.wind.set(
            windX,
            0,
            windZ
        );

        return {
            pressure,
            wind: this.wind.clone()
        };
    }

    getStats(position) {
        const sample = this.sample(position);

        return {
            pressure: sample.pressure,
            windSpeed: sample.wind.length(),
            windDirection: sample.wind.clone().normalize()
        };
    }
}

export { AirPressureField };

class CloudGenerator {
    constructor(config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    createRandom(seed = 91827) {
        let state = seed >>> 0;

        return () => {
            state = (
                Math.imul(state, 1664525) +
                1013904223
            ) >>> 0;

            return state / 4294967296;
        };
    }

    generate(
        position,
        scale,
        maxDepth = 2,
        seed = 91827
    ) {
        const matrices = [];
        const random = this.createRandom(seed);

        this.generateRecursive(
            position.clone(),
            scale,
            maxDepth,
            0,
            random,
            matrices
        );

        return matrices;
    }

    generateRecursive(
        position,
        scale,
        maxDepth,
        currentDepth,
        random,
        matrices
    ) {
        const config = this.config;
        const irregularity = config.irregularity;

        const dimensions = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            scale * config.width *
                (0.80 + random() * irregularity),
            scale * config.height *
                (0.80 + random() * irregularity),
            scale * config.depth *
                (0.80 + random() * irregularity)
        );

        const rotation =
            BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationYawPitchRoll(
                random() * 0.35,
                (random() - 0.5) * 0.22,
                (random() - 0.5) * 0.22
            );

        matrices.push(
            BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
                dimensions,
                rotation,
                position
            )
        );

        if (currentDepth >= maxDepth) {
            return;
        }

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < config.branchCount;
            i++
        ) {
            const angle = random() * Math.PI * 2;

            const horizontal =
                scale *
                config.horizontalSpread *
                (0.45 + random() * 0.75);

            const vertical =
                scale * config.verticalSpread;

            let verticalOffset =
                (random() * 2 - 1) * vertical;

            if (config.height > 2) {
                verticalOffset =
                    verticalOffset * 0.45 +
                    Math.abs(verticalOffset) * 0.75;
            }

            const offset = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                Math.cos(angle) * horizontal,
                verticalOffset,
                Math.sin(angle) * horizontal
            );

            const childPosition =
                position.add(offset);

            const childScale =
                scale *
                config.scaleDecay *
                (0.78 + random() * 0.38);

            this.generateRecursive(
                childPosition,
                childScale,
                maxDepth,
                currentDepth + 1,
                random,
                matrices
            );
        }
    }
}

export { CloudGenerator };

const CloudSpecies = {
    cumulus: {
        scale: 220,
        recursionDepth: 1,
        branchCount: 5,
        scaleDecay: 0.68,
        horizontalSpread: 1.05,
        verticalSpread: 0.24,
        irregularity: 0.30,
        width: 1.65,
        height: 0.75,
        depth: 1.45,
        moistureCapacity: 100,

        rainDuration: 1.5,
        color: new BABYLON.Color3(
            0.98, 0.99, 1.0
        )
    },

    towering: {
        scale: 440,
        recursionDepth: 2,
        branchCount: 4,
        scaleDecay: 0.70,
        horizontalSpread: 0.85,
        verticalSpread: 0.65,
        irregularity: 0.42,

        height: 1.65,

========================================================================
FILE: src/atmosphere/cloud-system.js

import { Cloud } from "./cloud.js";

import { CloudSpecies } from "./cloud-species.js";

import { CloudGenerator } from "./cloud-generator.js";

import { AirPressureField } from "./air-pressure-field.js";

import { CloudRenderer } from "../rendering/cloud-renderer.js";


class CloudSystem {
    constructor(
        scene,
        terrain,
        waterSystem,
        hydrology,
        rainSystem
    ) {
        this.scene = scene;

        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.waterSystem = waterSystem;
        this.hydrology = hydrology;
        this.rainSystem = rainSystem;

        this.clouds = [];

        this.renderer = new CloudRenderer(scene);

        this.maxClouds = 100;

        this.rainingCloud = null;

        // Atmospheric circulation.
        this.pressureField = new AirPressureField();

        this.elapsed = 0;

        // Regional weather classification.
        this.weatherCellSize = 100000;

        this.weatherCells = new Map();

        // Rendering update frequency.
        this.renderAccumulator = 0;
        this.renderInterval = 0.4;

        this.renderDirty = false;

        this.disposed = false;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------
    // REGIONAL WEATHER
    // ------------------------------------------

    getWeatherCellKey(x, z) {
        const cx = Math.floor(
            x / this.weatherCellSize
        );

        const cz = Math.floor(
            z / this.weatherCellSize
        );

        return `${cx},${cz}`;
    }

    getWeatherRegime(position) {
        const key = this.getWeatherCellKey(
            position.x,
            position.z
        );

        if (this.weatherCells.has(key)) {
            return this.weatherCells.get(key);
        }

        const centerX =
            (Math.floor(
                position.x / this.weatherCellSize
            ) + 0.5) * this.weatherCellSize;

        const centerZ =
            (Math.floor(
                position.z / this.weatherCellSize
            ) + 0.5) * this.weatherCellSize;

        const center = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            centerX,
            position.y,
            centerZ
        );

        const atmospheric =
            this.pressureField.sample(center);

        const moisture = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            this.waterSystem.getEvaporationPotential(
                center
            ),
            0,
            1
        );

        // Lower pressure favors rising air.
        // Moisture is required for deep convection.
        const instability = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            0.5 - atmospheric.pressure,
            0,
            1
        );

        let regime = "stable";

        if (
            moisture >= 0.58 &&
            atmospheric.pressure <= -0.12 &&
            instability >= 0.62
        ) {
            regime = "storm";
        } else if (
            moisture >= 0.36 &&
            atmospheric.pressure <= 0.20
        ) {
            regime = "convective";
        }

        this.weatherCells.set(key, regime);

        return regime;
    }

    selectSpecies(regime, random = Math.random) {
        const value = random();

        switch (regime) {
            case "storm":
                if (value < 0.20) {
                    return "cumulus";
                }

                if (value < 0.80) {
                    return "towering";
                }

                return "cumulonimbus";

            case "convective":
                return value < 0.45
                    ? "cumulus"
                    : "towering";

            default:
                return value < 0.85
                    ? "cumulus"
                    : "towering";
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------
    // CLOUD GENERATION
    // ------------------------------------------

    generate(count = 35) {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.clouds = [];

        this.weatherCells.clear();

        this.renderer.clear();

        const actualCount = Math.min(
            count,
            this.maxClouds
        );

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < actualCount;
            i++
        ) {
            const terrainPosition =
                this.terrain.sample();

            if (!terrainPosition) continue;

            const weatherRegime =
                this.getWeatherRegime(
                    terrainPosition
                );

            const speciesName =
                this.selectSpecies(weatherRegime);

            const config =
                CloudSpecies[speciesName];

            const scale =
                config.scale *
                (
                    speciesName === "cumulonimbus"
                        ? 0.78 + Math.random() * 0.35
                        : 0.70 + Math.random() * 0.55
                );

            const position =
                terrainPosition.add(
                    new BABYLON.Vector3(
                        0,
                        90 + Math.random() * 65,
                        0
                    )
                );

            const cloud = new Cloud(
                position,
                speciesName,
                scale,
                config,
                weatherRegime
            );

            // Generate the formation relative to
            // its own origin, not world coordinates.
            const generator = new CloudGenerator(
                config
            );

            cloud.geometryMatrices =
                generator.generate(
                    BABYLON.Vector3.Zero(),
                    scale,
                    config.recursionDepth,
                    i + 91827
                );

            // Start with the prevailing wind.
            const atmospheric =
                this.pressureField.sample(position);

            cloud.velocity.copyFrom(
                atmospheric.wind.scale(0.35)
            );

            this.clouds.push(cloud);
        }

        this.updateRendering(true);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------
    // WIND AND CLOUD MOVEMENT
    // ------------------------------------------

    updateWind(cloud, deltaTime) {
        const atmospheric =
            this.pressureField.sample(
                cloud.position
            );

        const target = atmospheric.wind;

        // Gradually approach the local wind velocity.
        // This avoids abrupt changes between regions.
        const response = 1 - Math.exp(
            -cloud.windResponse * deltaTime
        );

        cloud.velocity.x +=
            (target.x - cloud.velocity.x) *
            response;

        cloud.velocity.z +=
            (target.z - cloud.velocity.z) *
            response;

        // Small, smooth atmospheric turbulence.
        const turbulence =
            cloud.windTurbulence;

        const phase = this.elapsed * 0.12;

        cloud.velocity.x +=
            Math.sin(phase + cloud.position.z * 0.0001) *
            turbulence *
            deltaTime;

        cloud.velocity.z +=
            Math.cos(phase + cloud.position.x * 0.0001) *
            turbulence *
            deltaTime;

        cloud.position.x +=
            cloud.velocity.x * deltaTime * 0.5;

        cloud.position.z +=
            cloud.velocity.z * deltaTime * 0.5;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------
    // CLOUD SIMULATION
    // ------------------------------------------

    update(deltaYears) {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        const deltaTime = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(deltaYears, 0.25)
        );

        this.elapsed += deltaTime;

        this.pressureField.update(deltaTime);

        let activeRainCloud = null;

        for (const cloud of this.clouds) {
            this.updateCloud(cloud, deltaTime);

            if (
                cloud.isRaining &&
                (
                    !activeRainCloud ||
                    cloud.rainIntensity >
                    activeRainCloud.rainIntensity
                )
            ) {
                activeRainCloud = cloud;
            }
        }

        this.rainingCloud = activeRainCloud;

        if (activeRainCloud) {
            const rainPosition =
                activeRainCloud.position.clone();

            rainPosition.y -= 10;

            this.rainSystem.start(
                rainPosition,
                activeRainCloud.rainIntensity
            );
        } else {
            this.rainSystem.stop();
        }

        this.renderAccumulator += deltaTime;

        if (
            this.renderDirty &&
            this.renderAccumulator >=
                this.renderInterval
        ) {
            this.updateRendering();

            this.renderAccumulator = 0;
        }
    }

    updateCloud(cloud, deltaTime) {
        this.updateWind(cloud, deltaTime);

        // Cache environmental sampling.
        cloud.environmentSampleTimer -= deltaTime;

        if (cloud.environmentSampleTimer <= 0) {
            cloud.evaporationPotential =
                BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                    this.waterSystem.getEvaporationPotential(
                        cloud.position
                    ),
                    0,
                    1
                );

            cloud.environmentSampleTimer = 1.0;
        }

        // Moisture accumulation.
        if (!cloud.isRaining) {
            cloud.waterContent +=
                cloud.absorptionRate *
                cloud.evaporationPotential *
                deltaTime;
        }

        cloud.waterContent = Math.min(
            cloud.waterContent,
            cloud.maxWaterCapacity
        );

        // Precipitation threshold.
        if (
            cloud.waterContent >=
            cloud.maxWaterCapacity
        ) {
            this.startRain(cloud);
        }

        // Rainfall duration.
        if (cloud.isRaining) {
            cloud.rainRemaining -= deltaTime;

            if (cloud.rainRemaining <= 0) {
                cloud.isRaining = false;
                cloud.rainIntensity = 0;
                cloud.rainRemaining = 0;
            }
        }

        this.renderDirty = true;
    }

    startRain(cloud) {
        cloud.isRaining = true;

        cloud.rainRemaining =
            cloud.rainDuration;

        cloud.rainIntensity =
            0.5 + cloud.moistureRatio * 1.5;

        const releasedWater =
            cloud.maxWaterCapacity * 0.45;

        cloud.waterContent -= releasedWater;

        const rainfallAmount =
            0.25 * cloud.rainIntensity;

        const rainfallRadius =
            cloud.scale * 4;

        this.hydrology.addRain(
            cloud.position,
            rainfallRadius,
            rainfallAmount
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------
    // RENDERING
    // ------------------------------------------

    updateRendering(force = false) {
        if (
            !force &&
            !this.renderDirty
        ) {
            return;
        }

        this.renderer.clear();

        for (const cloud of this.clouds) {
            const localMatrices =
                cloud.geometryMatrices;

            if (!localMatrices) continue;

            const worldMatrices = [];

            for (const localMatrix of localMatrices) {
                const matrix = localMatrix.clone();

                // Translate the local formation into
                // its current world position.
                matrix.m[12] += cloud.position.x;
                matrix.m[13] += cloud.position.y;
                matrix.m[14] += cloud.position.z;

                worldMatrices.push(matrix);
            }

            this.renderer.addCloud(
                worldMatrices,
                cloud.moistureRatio,
                cloud.speciesName,
                cloud.position
            );
        }

        this.renderer.update();

        this.renderDirty = false;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------
    // DIAGNOSTICS
    // ------------------------------------------

    getStats() {
        const speciesCounts = {
            cumulus: 0,
            towering: 0,
            cumulonimbus: 0
        };

        for (const cloud of this.clouds) {
            speciesCounts[cloud.speciesName]++;
        }

        return {
            totalClouds: this.clouds.length,
            speciesCounts,
            weatherRegions: this.weatherCells.size,
            rainingClouds: this.clouds.filter(
                cloud => cloud.isRaining
            ).length,
            averageWindSpeed:
                this.clouds.length
                    ? this.clouds.reduce(
                        (sum, cloud) =>
                            sum + cloud.velocity.length(),
                        0
                    ) / this.clouds.length
                    : 0
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        this.rainSystem.stop();

        this.renderer.mesh.dispose();

        this.clouds.length = 0;

        this.weatherCells.clear();
    }
}

export { CloudSystem };

FILE: src/atmosphere/cloud.js

export class Cloud {
    constructor(position, speciesName, scale, config) {
        this.position = position.clone();
        this.speciesName = speciesName;
        this.scale = scale;
        this.baseScale = scale;
        this.config = config;

        this.waterContent = 0;
        this.maxWaterCapacity = config.moistureCapacity ?? 100;
        this.absorptionRate = config.absorptionRate ?? 16;

        this.rainIntensity = 0;
        this.rainRemaining = 0;
        this.rainDuration = config.rainDuration ?? 1.5;
        this.isRaining = false;

        // Atmospheric wind state.
        this.velocity = BABYLON.Vector3.Zero();
        this.windTarget = BABYLON.Vector3.Zero();
        this.windResponse = 0.12;

        this.localMoisture = 0;
        this.evaporationPotential = 0;
        this.environmentSampleTimer = 0;

        // Persistent geometry cache.
        this.moistureAtLastRender = -1;
        this.baseGeometryMatrices = null;
        this.baseGeometryPosition = null;

        this.geometrySeed = 1;
    }

    get moistureRatio() {
        return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            this.waterContent / this.maxWaterCapacity,
            0,
            1
        );
    }

    get isStormCloud() {
        return this.speciesName === "cumulonimbus";
    }

    updateWind(target, deltaSeconds) {
        this.windTarget.copyFrom(target);

        const response = 1 - Math.exp(
            -this.windResponse * deltaSeconds
        );

        BABYLON.Vector3.LerpToRef(
            this.velocity,
            this.windTarget,
            response,
            this.velocity
        );

        this.position.addInPlace(
            this.velocity.scale(deltaSeconds*0.01)
        );
    }
}

FILE: src/buildings/building-generator.js

class BuildingGenerator {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.defaultWallHeight = options.wallHeight ?? 3.2;
        this.defaultWallThickness = options.wallThickness ?? 0.18;
    }

    createBox(output, kind, position, dimensions, rotation = 0, material = "wall") {
        if (
            dimensions.x <= 0 ||
            dimensions.y <= 0 ||
            dimensions.z <= 0
        ) return;

        output.push({
            kind,
            position,
            dimensions,
            rotation,
            material
        });
    }

    getRoomEdges(room) {
        const b = room.bounds;

        return [
            {
                side: "north",
                x1: b.minX, z1: b.minZ,
                x2: b.maxX, z2: b.minZ
            },
            {
                side: "south",
                x1: b.minX, z1: b.maxZ,
                x2: b.maxX, z2: b.maxZ
            },
            {
                side: "west",
                x1: b.minX, z1: b.minZ,
                x2: b.minX, z2: b.maxZ
            },
            {
                side: "east",
                x1: b.maxX, z1: b.minZ,
                x2: b.maxX, z2: b.maxZ
            }
        ];
    }

    edgeKey(edge, floor) {
        const a = `${edge.x1},${edge.z1}`;
        const b = `${edge.x2},${edge.z2}`;

        return [
            floor,
            ...[a, b].sort()
        ].join(":");
    }

    generateWall(output, edge, openings, floorY, height, thickness) {
        const horizontal = edge.side === "north" ||
            edge.side === "south";

        const length = horizontal
            ? Math.abs(edge.x2 - edge.x1)
            : Math.abs(edge.z2 - edge.z1);

        const centerX = (edge.x1 + edge.x2) * 0.5;
        const centerZ = (edge.z1 + edge.z2) * 0.5;

        const wallRotation = horizontal ? 0 : Math.PI / 2;

        const centerY = floorY + height * 0.5;

        const validOpenings = openings
            .filter(o => o.side === edge.side)
            .map(o => ({
                ...o,
                center: o.offset * length
            }))
            .filter(o =>
                o.center - o.width * 0.5 >= 0 &&
                o.center + o.width * 0.5 <= length
            )
            .sort((a, b) => a.center - b.center);

        let cursor = 0;

        const addSegment = (start, end) => {
            if (end - start <= 0.01) return;

            const middle = (start + end) * 0.5;
            const along = middle - length * 0.5;

            this.createBox(
                output,
                "walls",
                {
                    x: centerX + (horizontal ? along : 0),
                    y: centerY,
                    z: centerZ + (horizontal ? 0 : along)
                },
                {
                    x: horizontal ? end - start : thickness,
                    y: height,
                    z: horizontal ? thickness : end - start
                },
                0,
                "wall"
            );
        };

        for (const opening of validOpenings) {
            const start = opening.center - opening.width * 0.5;
            const end = opening.center + opening.width * 0.5;

            // Full-height wall before the opening.
            addSegment(cursor, start);

            const along = opening.center - length * 0.5;
            const x = centerX + (horizontal ? along : 0);
            const z = centerZ + (horizontal ? 0 : along);

            // Windows retain their sill and lintel.
            if (opening.type === "window") {
                if (opening.sill > 0) {
                    this.createBox(
                        output,
                        "walls",
                        { x, y: floorY + opening.sill * 0.5, z },
                        {
                            x: horizontal ? opening.width : thickness,
                            y: opening.sill,
                            z: horizontal ? thickness : opening.width
                        }
                    );
                }

                const top = opening.sill + opening.height;
                const remaining = height - top;

                if (remaining > 0) {
                    this.createBox(
                        output,
                        "walls",
                        {
                            x,
                            y: floorY + top + remaining * 0.5,
                            z
                        },
                        {
                            x: horizontal ? opening.width : thickness,
                            y: remaining,
                            z: horizontal ? thickness : opening.width
                        }
                    );
                }

                this.createBox(
                    output,
                    "glazing",
                    {
                        x,
                        y: floorY + opening.sill + opening.height * 0.5,
                        z
                    },
                    {
                        x: horizontal ? opening.width * 0.88 : 0.035,
                        y: opening.height * 0.88,
                        z: horizontal ? 0.035 : opening.width * 0.88
                    },
                    0,
                    "glass"
                );
            } else {
                // Door frame components.
                const frame = 0.075;

                for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
                    this.createBox(
                        output,
                        "trim",
                        {
                            x: x + (horizontal ? side * (opening.width * 0.5 - frame * 0.5) : 0),
                            y: floorY + opening.height * 0.5,
                            z: z + (horizontal ? 0 : side * (opening.width * 0.5 - frame * 0.5))
                        },
                        {
                            x: horizontal ? frame : thickness * 0.75,
                            y: opening.height,
                            z: horizontal ? thickness * 0.75 : frame
                        },
                        0,
                        "wood"
                    );
                }

                this.createBox(
                    output,
                    "trim",
                    { x, y: floorY + opening.height, z },
                    {
                        x: horizontal ? opening.width : thickness * 0.75,
                        y: frame,
                        z: horizontal ? thickness * 0.75 : opening.width
                    },
                    0,
                    "wood"
                );

                // Door leaf is a separate component.
                this.createBox(
                    output,
                    "doors",
                    {
                        x,
                        y: floorY + opening.height * 0.5,
                        z
                    },
                    {
                        x: horizontal ? opening.width * 0.94 : 0.07,
                        y: opening.height * 0.94,
                        z: horizontal ? 0.07 : opening.width * 0.94
                    },
                    0,
                    "wood"
                );
            }

            cursor = end;
        }

        addSegment(cursor, length);
    }

    generate(building) {
        building.validate();

        const output = [];

        const edges = new Map();

        for (const room of building.rooms) {
            const floorY = room.floor * room.wallHeight;
            const b = room.bounds;

            this.createBox(
                output,
                "floors",
                {
                    x: (b.minX + b.maxX) * 0.5,
                    y: floorY - room.floorThickness * 0.5,
                    z: (b.minZ + b.maxZ) * 0.5
                },
                {
                    x: b.maxX - b.minX,
                    y: room.floorThickness,
                    z: b.maxZ - b.minZ
                },
                0,
                "floor"
            );

            this.createBox(
                output,
                "ceilings",
                {
                    x: (b.minX + b.maxX) * 0.5,
                    y: floorY + room.wallHeight,
                    z: (b.minZ + b.maxZ) * 0.5
                },
                {
                    x: b.maxX - b.minX,
                    y: 0.12,
                    z: b.maxZ - b.minZ
                },
                0,
                "ceiling"
            );

            for (const edge of this.getRoomEdges(room)) {
                const key = this.edgeKey(edge, room.floor);

                if (!edges.has(key)) {
                    edges.set(key, {
                        edge,
                        openings: [],
                        floorY,
                        height: room.wallHeight,
                        thickness: room.wallThickness
                    });
                }

		edges.get(key).openings.push(
    ...(room.openings ?? [])
        .filter(o => o.side === edge.side)
        .map(o => ({
            ...o,
            side: edges.get(key).edge.side
        }))
);

            }
        }

        for (const item of edges.values()) {
            this.generateWall(
                output,
                item.edge,
                item.openings,
                item.floorY,
                item.height,
                item.thickness
            );
        }

        // Explicitly placed modular objects.
        const components = [
            ...building.components,
            ...building.furniture,
            ...building.fixtures
        ];

        for (const component of components) {
            const kind = component.kind === "furniture"
                ? "furniture"
                : component.kind === "fixture"
                    ? "fixtures"
                    : component.kind;

            this.createBox(
                output,
                kind,
                component.position,
                component.dimensions,
                component.rotation,
                component.material
            );
        }

        return output;
    }
}

export { BuildingGenerator };


========================================================================
FILE: src/buildings/building-model.js
========================================================================


function createId(prefix = "building") {
    return `${prefix}-${globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.() ??
        `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`}`;
}

function clone(value) {
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
}

class BuildingModel {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.id = options.id ?? createId();

        this.name = options.name ?? "New Building";
        this.type = options.type ?? "residential";

        this.position = {
            x: options.position?.x ?? 0,
            y: options.position?.y ?? 0,
            z: options.position?.z ?? 0
        };

        this.rotation = options.rotation ?? 0;

        this.rooms = clone(options.rooms ?? []);
        this.furniture = clone(options.furniture ?? []);
        this.fixtures = clone(options.fixtures ?? []);

        // Independently placed architectural elements.
        this.components = clone(options.components ?? []);

        this.metadata = {
            version: 1,
            createdAt: options.metadata?.createdAt ??
                new Date().toISOString()
        };
    }

    addRoom(options = {}) {
        const room = {
            id: createId("room"),
            name: options.name ?? "Room",
            floor: options.floor ?? 0,

            bounds: {
                minX: options.minX ?? 0,
                maxX: options.maxX ?? 8,
                minZ: options.minZ ?? 0,
                maxZ: options.maxZ ?? 8
            },

            wallHeight: options.wallHeight ?? 3.2,
            wallThickness: options.wallThickness ?? 0.18,

            floorThickness: options.floorThickness ?? 0.15,

            openings: clone(options.openings ?? [])
        };

        this.rooms.push(room);
        return room;
    }

    addOpening(roomId, options = {}) {
        const room = this.rooms.find(r => r.id === roomId);

        if (!room) {
            throw new Error("Room not found.");
        }

        const opening = {
            id: createId("opening"),
            type: options.type ?? "door",
            side: options.side ?? "north",
            offset: options.offset ?? 0.5,
            width: options.width ?? 0.9,
            height: options.height ?? 2.1,
            sill: options.sill ?? 0
        };

        room.openings.push(opening);
        return opening;
    }

    addComponent(options = {}) {
        const component = {
            id: createId("component"),
            kind: options.kind ?? "furniture",
            type: options.type ?? "custom",

            position: {
                x: options.position?.x ?? 0,
                y: options.position?.y ?? 0,
                z: options.position?.z ?? 0
            },

            dimensions: {
                x: options.dimensions?.x ?? 1,
                y: options.dimensions?.y ?? 1,
                z: options.dimensions?.z ?? 1
            },

            rotation: options.rotation ?? 0,
            material: options.material ?? "wood"
        };

        this.components.push(component);
        return component;
    }

    addFurniture(options = {}) {
        return this.addComponent({
            ...options,
            kind: "furniture"
        });
    }

    validate() {
        if (!this.id || !Array.isArray(this.rooms)) {
            throw new Error("Invalid building.");
        }

        for (const room of this.rooms) {
            const b = room.bounds;

            if (
                !b ||
                ![b.minX, b.maxX, b.minZ, b.maxZ].every(Number.isFinite) ||
                b.maxX <= b.minX ||
                b.maxZ <= b.minZ ||
                !Number.isFinite(room.floor) ||
                !Number.isFinite(room.wallHeight) ||
                room.wallHeight <= 0
            ) {
                throw new Error(`Invalid room: ${room.id}`);
            }

            for (const opening of room.openings ?? []) {
                if (
                    !["north", "south", "east", "west"].includes(opening.side) ||
                    !["door", "window"].includes(opening.type) ||
                    !Number.isFinite(opening.offset) ||
                    opening.offset < 0 ||
                    opening.offset > 1 ||
                    !Number.isFinite(opening.width) ||
                    opening.width <= 0 ||
                    !Number.isFinite(opening.height) ||
                    opening.height <= 0 ||
                    !Number.isFinite(opening.sill) ||
                    opening.sill < 0 ||
                    opening.sill + opening.height > room.wallHeight
                ) {
                    throw new Error(`Invalid opening in room ${room.id}`);
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    serialize() {
        this.validate();

        return {
            id: this.id,
            type: "building",
            version: 1,
            name: this.name,
            buildingType: this.type,
            position: clone(this.position),
            rotation: this.rotation,
            rooms: clone(this.rooms),
            furniture: clone(this.furniture),
            fixtures: clone(this.fixtures),
            components: clone(this.components),
            metadata: clone(this.metadata)
        };
    }

    static deserialize(data) {
        if (!data || data.type !== "building" ||
            ![1].includes(data.version)) {
            throw new Error("Unsupported building record.");
        }

        const building = new BuildingModel({
            id: data.id,
            name: data.name,
            type: data.buildingType,
            position: data.position,
            rotation: data.rotation,
            rooms: data.rooms,
            furniture: data.furniture,
            fixtures: data.fixtures,
            components: data.components,
            metadata: data.metadata
        });

        building.validate();
        return building;
    }
}

export { BuildingModel, createId };


========================================================================
FILE: src/buildings/building-renderer.js
========================================================================

import {
    ThinInstanceRenderer
} from "../rendering/thin-instance-renderer.js";

const MATERIALS = {
    wall: [0.72, 0.69, 0.62],
    floor: [0.48, 0.34, 0.22],
    ceiling: [0.83, 0.81, 0.75],
    glass: [0.35, 0.70, 0.86],
    wood: [0.34, 0.20, 0.11],
    furniture: [0.48, 0.31, 0.20]
};

const BUCKETS = [
    "walls",
    "floors",
    "ceilings",
    "glazing",
    "trim",
    "doors",
    "furniture",
    "fixtures"
];

/**
 * BuildingRenderer
 *
 * Converts authoritative architectural components into
 * thin-instance rendering batches.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * This class never applies SpatialTransform directly.
 * ThinInstanceRenderer delegates projection to the
 * existing GeometryRenderer.
 */
class BuildingRenderer {
    constructor(scene, spatialTransform = null) {
        if (!scene) {
            throw new Error(
                "BuildingRenderer requires a scene."
            );
        }

        this.scene = scene;

        // Retained for compatibility with existing integration.
        // Projection ownership belongs to GeometryRenderer.
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        this.buckets = new Map();
        this.disposed = false;

        this.statistics = {
            submittedComponents: 0,
            rejectedComponents: 0,
            renderedInstances: 0
        };

        for (const kind of BUCKETS) {
            this.createBucket(kind);
        }
    }

    createBucket(kind) {
        const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateBox(
            `building-prototype-${kind}`,
            { size: 1 },
            this.scene
        );

        const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            `building-material-${kind}`,
            this.scene
        );

        const colorName =
            kind === "walls" ? "wall" :
            kind === "floors" ? "floor" :
            kind === "ceilings" ? "ceiling" :
            kind === "glazing" ? "glass" :
            kind === "trim" || kind === "doors" ? "wood" :
            "furniture";

        material.diffuseColor = new BABYLON.Color3(
            ...MATERIALS[colorName]
        );

        if (kind === "glazing") {
            material.alpha = 0.42;

            material.transparencyMode =
                BABYLON.Material.MATERIAL_ALPHABLEND;

            material.backFaceCulling = false;

            material.specularColor =
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.8, 0.9, 1);
        }

        mesh.material = material;

        const renderer = new ThinInstanceRenderer(
            this.scene,
            `building-${kind}`,
            mesh
        );

        this.buckets.set(kind, {
            mesh,
            material,
            renderer
        });
    }

    clear() {
        for (const bucket of this.buckets.values()) {
            bucket.renderer.clear();
        }

        this.statistics.submittedComponents = 0;
        this.statistics.rejectedComponents = 0;
        this.statistics.renderedInstances = 0;
    }

    addComponent(component, building) {
        if (
            !component ||
            !building ||
            !component.position ||
            !component.dimensions
        ) {
            this.statistics.rejectedComponents++;
            return false;
        }

        const bucket = this.buckets.get(component.kind);

        if (!bucket) {
            this.statistics.rejectedComponents++;
            return false;
        }

        const values = [
            component.position.x,
            component.position.y,
            component.position.z,
            component.dimensions.x,
            component.dimensions.y,
            component.dimensions.z,
            building.position.x,
            building.position.y,
            building.position.z
        ];

        if (
            !values.every(Number.isFinite) ||
            values.slice(3, 6).some(value => value <= 0)
        ) {
            this.statistics.rejectedComponents++;
            return false;
        }

        const angle = building.rotation ?? 0;

        if (!Number.isFinite(angle)) {
            this.statistics.rejectedComponents++;
            return false;
        }

        const cos = Math.cos(angle);
        const sin = Math.sin(angle);

        const local = component.position;

        const rotated = {
            x: local.x * cos - local.z * sin,
            y: local.y,
            z: local.x * sin + local.z * cos
        };

        // Authoritative world coordinates.
        // Do not project this position here.
        const world = {
            x: building.position.x + rotated.x,
            y: building.position.y + rotated.y,
            z: building.position.z + rotated.z
        };

        const rotation = BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationAxis(
            BABYLON.Axis.Y,
            angle + (component.rotation ?? 0)
        );

        // Dimensions remain in authoritative units.
        // GeometryRenderer applies the spatial projection.
        const scale = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            component.dimensions.x,
            component.dimensions.y,
            component.dimensions.z
        );

        const matrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
            scale,
            rotation,
            new BABYLON.Vector3(
                world.x,
                world.y,
                world.z
            )
        );

        bucket.renderer.addMatrix(matrix);

        this.statistics.submittedComponents++;

        return true;
    }

    update(buildings) {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.clear();

        for (const building of buildings) {
            if (!Array.isArray(building.generatedGeometry)) {
                continue;
            }

            for (const component of building.generatedGeometry) {
                this.addComponent(component, building);
            }
        }

        for (const bucket of this.buckets.values()) {
            bucket.renderer.update();

            this.statistics.renderedInstances +=
                bucket.renderer.matrices.length;
        }
    }

    getStats() {
        const result = {};

        for (const [kind, bucket] of this.buckets) {
            result[kind] = {
                instances: bucket.renderer.matrices.length
            };
        }

        return {
            buckets: result,
            submittedComponents:
                this.statistics.submittedComponents,
            rejectedComponents:
                this.statistics.rejectedComponents,
            renderedInstances:
                this.statistics.renderedInstances
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        for (const bucket of this.buckets.values()) {
            bucket.renderer.dispose?.();
            bucket.mesh.dispose();
            bucket.material.dispose();
        }

        this.buckets.clear();
    }
}

export { BuildingRenderer };


========================================================================
FILE: src/buildings/building-system.js
========================================================================


import { BuildingModel } from "./building-model.js";
import { BuildingGenerator } from "./building-generator.js";
import { BuildingRenderer } from "./building-renderer.js";

class BuildingSystem {
    constructor(scene, geometryRenderer, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.geometryRenderer = geometryRenderer;

        this.generator = new BuildingGenerator();

        this.renderer = new BuildingRenderer(
            scene,
            options.spatialTransform ?? null
        );

        this.buildings = new Map();
        this.disposed = false;
        this.terrain = options.terrain ?? null;
    }

    createBuilding(options = {}) {
        const building = new BuildingModel(options);

        building.validate();

        this.buildings.set(building.id, building);
        this.rebuild(building);

        return building;
    }

    addBuilding(building) {
        if (!(building instanceof BuildingModel)) {
            building = BuildingModel.deserialize(building);
        }

        building.validate();
        this.buildings.set(building.id, building);
        this.rebuild(building);

        return building;
    }

    removeBuilding(id) {
        const removed = this.buildings.delete(id);

        if (removed) this.refresh();

        return removed;
    }

    getBuilding(id) {
        return this.buildings.get(id) ?? null;
    }

    rebuild(building) {
        building.validate();

        building.generatedGeometry =
            this.generator.generate(building);

        this.refresh();
    }

    refresh() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.renderer.update(
            Array.from(this.buildings.values())
        );
    }

    createRoom(buildingId, options = {}) {
        const building = this.getBuilding(buildingId);

        if (!building) {
            throw new Error("Building not found.");
        }

        const room = building.addRoom(options);

        this.rebuild(building);

        return room;
    }

    addOpening(buildingId, roomId, options = {}) {
        const building = this.getBuilding(buildingId);

        if (!building) {
            throw new Error("Building not found.");
        }

        const opening = building.addOpening(
            roomId,
            options
        );

        this.rebuild(building);

        return opening;
    }

    addFurniture(buildingId, options = {}) {
        const building = this.getBuilding(buildingId);

        if (!building) {
            throw new Error("Building not found.");
        }

        const furniture = building.addFurniture(options);

        this.rebuild(building);

        return furniture;
    }

    captureWorld(world) {
        if (!world?.entities) {
            throw new Error("Invalid world state.");
        }

        const other = world.entities.manual.filter(
            entity => entity.type !== "building"
        );

        const committed = Array.from(
            this.buildings.values()
        ).filter(
            building =>
                building.metadata?.editorPreview !== true
        );

        world.entities.manual = [
            ...other,
            ...committed.map(
                building => building.serialize()
            )
        ];

        return world;
    }

    restoreWorld(world) {
        if (
            !world?.entities ||
            !Array.isArray(world.entities.manual)
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid world entity collection."
            );
        }

        this.buildings.clear();

        for (const entity of world.entities.manual) {
            if (entity.type === "building") {
                this.addBuilding(entity);
            }
        }

        this.refresh();
    }

    getStats() {
        const buildings = Array.from(
            this.buildings.values()
        );

        return {
            buildings: buildings.length,
            provisional: buildings.filter(
                building =>
                    building.metadata?.editorPreview === true
            ).length,
            committed: buildings.filter(
                building =>
                    building.metadata?.editorPreview !== true
            ).length,
            rooms: buildings.reduce(
                (sum, building) =>
                    sum + building.rooms.length,
                0
            ),
            instances: this.renderer.getStats()
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;
        this.buildings.clear();
        this.renderer.dispose();
    }
}

export { BuildingSystem };

const Genetics = {

    createGenome() {

        return {

            growthRate: 1.0,
            maxHeight: 1.0,

            // Structural characteristics
            trunkThickness: 1.0,
            branchDensity: 1.0,
            branchThickness: 1.0,
            branchTaper: 0.75,
            branchAngle: 1.0,

            // Foliage characteristics
            leafDensity: 1.0,
            canopyWidth: 1.0,
            leafSize: 1.0,

            // Environmental characteristics
            lightSensitivity: 1.0,

            seedProduction: 1.0,
            lifespan: 1.0
        };

    },


    inherit(parentGenome, mutationRate = 0.8) {

        const child = {};

        for (const gene in parentGenome) {

            const mutation = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * mutationRate;

            child[gene] = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                parentGenome[gene] * (1 + mutation),
                0.1,
                1.5
            );
        }

        return child;
    }

};
export { Genetics };

const LOG_LEVELS = Object.freeze({
    SILENT: 0,
    ERROR: 1,
    WARN: 2,
    NORMAL: 3,
    DETAIL: 4,
    DEBUG: 5
});

class SomniumLogger {
    static levels = LOG_LEVELS;
    static level = LOG_LEVELS.WARN;

    static setLevel(level) {
        if (
            !Number.isInteger(level) ||
            level < LOG_LEVELS.SILENT ||
            level > LOG_LEVELS.DEBUG
        ) {
            throw new RangeError(
                "Invalid Somnium logging level."
            );
        }

        this.level = level;
    }

    static getLevel() {
        return this.level;
    }

    static enabled(level) {
        return this.level >= level;
    }

    static error(scope, message, details = null) {
        if (!this.enabled(LOG_LEVELS.ERROR)) return;
        console.error(`[${scope}] ${message}`, details ?? "");
    }

    static warn(scope, message, details = null) {
        if (!this.enabled(LOG_LEVELS.WARN)) return;
        console.warn(`[${scope}] ${message}`, details ?? "");
    }

    static info(scope, message, details = null) {
        if (!this.enabled(LOG_LEVELS.NORMAL)) return;
        console.info(`[${scope}] ${message}`, details ?? "");
    }

    static detail(scope, message, details = null) {
        if (!this.enabled(LOG_LEVELS.DETAIL)) return;
        console.info(`[${scope}] ${message}`, details ?? "");
    }

    static debug(scope, message, details = null) {
        if (!this.enabled(LOG_LEVELS.DEBUG)) return;
        console.debug(`[${scope}] ${message}`, details ?? "");
    }
}

export { SomniumLogger, LOG_LEVELS };


========================================================================
FILE: src/core/noise.js

function hashNoise2D(x, z, seed = 1) {

    let n =
        Math.imul(x | 0, 374761393) +
        Math.imul(z | 0, 668265263) +
        Math.imul(seed | 0, 1442695041);

    n = Math.imul(
        n ^ (n >>> 13),
        1274126177
    );

    n ^= n >>> 16;

    return (n >>> 0) / 4294967295;

}


function smoothNoise2D(x, z, seed = 1) {

    const ix = Math.floor(x);
    const iz = Math.floor(z);

    const fx = x - ix;
    const fz = z - iz;

    const u = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx);
    const v = fz * fz * (3 - 2 * fz);

    const a = hashNoise2D(ix, iz, seed);
    const b = hashNoise2D(ix + 1, iz, seed);
    const c = hashNoise2D(ix, iz + 1, seed);
    const d = hashNoise2D(ix + 1, iz + 1, seed);

    const ab = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(a, b, u);
    const cd = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(c, d, u);

    return BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(ab, cd, v);

}


function fractalNoise2D(
    x,
    z,
    seed,
    octaves = 4
) {

    let value = 0;
    let amplitude = 1;
    let frequency = 1;
    let totalAmplitude = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {

        value += smoothNoise2D(
            x * frequency,
            z * frequency,
            seed + i * 1013
        ) * amplitude;

        totalAmplitude += amplitude;

        amplitude *= 0.5;
        frequency *= 2;

    }

    return value / totalAmplitude;

}
export { hashNoise2D, smoothNoise2D, fractalNoise2D };

function CreateSeededRandom(seed) {

    let state = seed >>> 0;

    return function() {

        state += 0x6D2B79F5;

        let t = state;

        t = Math.imul(
            t ^ (t >>> 15),
            t | 1
        );

        t ^= t + Math.imul(
            t ^ (t >>> 7),
            t | 61
        );

        return (
            (t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0
        ) / 4294967296;

    };

}
export { CreateSeededRandom }

const Species = {
    oak: {
        name: "Oak",

        trunkLength: 5.0,
        trunkRadius: 0.52,
        branchLength: 3.0,
        branchRadius: 0.12,
        branchAngle: Math.PI / 3.5,
        branchCount: 5,
        maxDepth: 3,
        lengthDecay: 0.68,
        radiusDecay: 0.65,
        lightSensitivity: 0.35,

        droughtTolerance: 0.12,
        waterloggingTolerance: 0.95,
        optimalMoisture: 0.60,

        soilAffinity: {
            preferred: [
                "loam",
                "silt",
                "organic",
                "fertileSoil"
            ],
            fertility: 0.80,
            moisture: 0.65,
            drainage: 0.55,
            tolerance: 0.65
        },

        branching: "radial",

        canopy: {
            shape: "round",
            width: 1.5,
            height: 0.85,
            startHeight: 0.35,
            density: 1.0,
            layers: 3
        }
    },

    pine: {
        name: "Pine",

        trunkLength: 8.0,
        trunkRadius: 0.18,
        branchLength: 3.0,
        branchRadius: 0.09,
        branchAngle: Math.PI / 3,
        branchCount: 4,
        maxDepth: 2,
        lengthDecay: 0.72,
        radiusDecay: 0.72,
        lightSensitivity: 0.20,

        droughtTolerance: 0.08,
        waterloggingTolerance: 0.85,
        optimalMoisture: 0.40,

        soilAffinity: {
            preferred: [
                "sand",
                "scree",
                "silt",
                "fertileSoil"
            ],
            fertility: 0.30,
            moisture: 0.40,
            drainage: 0.85,
            tolerance: 0.90
        },

        branching: "whorled",

        canopy: {
            shape: "conical",
            width: 0.9,
            height: 1.8,
            startHeight: 0.25,
            density: 0.85,
            layers: 6
        }
    }
};
