import { Environment } from "../environment/environment.js";
import { TerrainSampler } from "../environment/terrain.js";
import { SpatialGrid } from "../environment/spatial-grid.js";
import { GeologicalManager } from "../geology/geological-manager.js";
import { GeologicalField } from "../geology/geological-field.js";
import { TreeRenderer, FoliageRenderer } from "../rendering/tree-renderers.js";
import { RockRenderer } from "../rendering/rock-renderer.js";
import { Tree } from "./tree.js";
import { TreeGenerator } from "./tree-generator.js";
import { Genetics } from "../core/genetics.js";
import { CloudSpecies } from  "../atmosphere/cloud-species.js";
import { CloudGenerator } from "../atmosphere/cloud-generator.js";
import { WaterSystem } from "../water/water-system.js";
import { Hydrology } from "../water/hydrology.js";
import { RainSystem } from "../water/rain-system.js";
import { CloudSystem } from "../atmosphere/cloud-system.js";
import { WorldFieldSystem } from "../world/fields/world-field-system.js";

class Forest {


	constructor(
    scene,
    ground,
    sunDirection,
    shadowGenerator,
    seaLevel = -100
) {
        this.scene = scene;

	this.shadowGenerator = shadowGenerator;

        this.environment = new Environment(sunDirection);

        this.terrain = new TerrainSampler(scene, ground);

	this.lastRenderTime = 0;
	this.renderInterval = 5;
	this.simulationSteps = 0;
	this.renderEverySteps = 3;
	this.renderDirty = true;

        this.minSpacing = 2.5;
	this.spatialGrid = new SpatialGrid(this.minSpacing);
	
        this.trees = [];


	this.geology = new GeologicalManager( scene, this.terrain, shadowGenerator );
	// Initialize centralized environmental fields.

this.worldFields = new WorldFieldSystem({

    terrain: this.terrain,

    geologicalField: this.geology.field

});

        this.renderers = {

oak: new TreeRenderer(
    scene,
    "oakBranches",
    shadowGenerator
),

pine: new TreeRenderer(
    scene,
    "pineBranches",
    shadowGenerator
)
        };
	
this.rockRenderer = new RockRenderer(
    scene,
    shadowGenerator
);



	// Initialize the water system.

this.waterSystem = new WaterSystem(
    scene,
    this.terrain,
    -100
);


// Initialize soil hydrology.

this.hydrology = new Hydrology(
    this.terrain,
    this.waterSystem,
    20
);


// Initialize precipitation rendering.

this.rainSystem = new RainSystem(
    scene
);


// Initialize atmospheric simulation.

this.cloudSystem = new CloudSystem(
    scene,
    this.terrain,
    this.waterSystem,
    this.hydrology,
    this.rainSystem
);

	this.foliageRenderers = {
oak: new FoliageRenderer(
    scene,
    "oakLeaves",
    new BABYLON.Color3(0.18, 0.22, 0.12),
    shadowGenerator
),

pine: new FoliageRenderer(
    scene,
    "pineLeaves",
    new BABYLON.Color3(0.12, 0.32, 0.16),
    shadowGenerator
)

};

        const oakMaterial = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "oakMaterial",
            scene
        );

        oakMaterial.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.30, 0.16, 0.07);

        this.renderers.oak.mesh.material = oakMaterial;

        const pineMaterial = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "pineMaterial",
            scene
        );

        pineMaterial.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.40, 0.27, 0.13);

        this.renderers.pine.mesh.material = pineMaterial;
    }


isPositionAvailable(position) {

    return this.spatialGrid.isAvailable(
        position,
        this.minSpacing
    );

}

addTree(tree) {

    this.trees.push(tree);

    this.spatialGrid.insert(tree);

    return tree;

}

generate(numTrees = 80) {

    let attempts = 0;

    let livingCount = this.trees.reduce(
        (count, tree) => count + (tree.alive ? 1 : 0),
        0
    );

    while (
        livingCount < numTrees &&
        attempts < numTrees * 50
    ) {

        attempts++;

        const position = this.terrain.sample();

        if (!position) continue;
	
	if (this.waterSystem.isUnderwater(position)) {
		continue;
	}

        if (!this.isPositionAvailable(position)) {
            continue;
        }

        const speciesName =
            Math.random() < 0.55 ? "oak" : "pine";

        const tree = new Tree(
            speciesName,
            position,
            Genetics.createGenome(),
            null,
            Math.random() * 20
        );

        this.addTree(tree);

        livingCount++;
    }

// Generate geological formations.
this.geology.generateAll();

// Generate atmospheric objects.
this.generateWorldObjects(35);

// Render the forest.
this.renderAll();
    console.log("Initial forest:", livingCount);
}

    spawnOffspring(parent) {

        const numSeeds = parent.produceSeeds();

        let successful = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < numSeeds; i++) {

// Check available light at the parent location
const parentLight =
    this.environment.sampleLight(parent.position);

// Establish a minimum germination requirement
const minimumGerminationLight = 0.30;

if (parentLight.intensity < minimumGerminationLight) {
    continue;
}

// Germination probability increases with available light
const germinationChance =
    0.65 *
    BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        parentLight.intensity,
        0,
        1
    );

if (Math.random() > germinationChance) {
    continue;
}
// Only successful germination attempts
// need a terrain sample.
const position = this.terrain.sampleNear(
    parent.position,
    10
);

if (!position) continue;

if (this.waterSystem.isUnderwater(position)) {
    continue;
}


// Check actual germination location
const seedLight =
    this.environment.sampleLight(position);

if (seedLight.intensity < minimumGerminationLight) {
    continue;
}

            // Inherit and mutate the parent's genome
            const childGenome = Genetics.inherit(
                parent.genome,
                0.8
            );

            const child = new Tree(
                parent.speciesName,
                position,
                childGenome,
                parent.id,
                0
            );

            this.addTree(child);

            parent.children.push(child.id);

            successful++;
        }

        return successful;
    }


advance(deltaYears) {

	   // Advance water and atmospheric systems.

    this.waterSystem.update(deltaYears);

    this.hydrology.update(deltaYears);

    this.cloudSystem.update(deltaYears);


	// Advance geological processes.
	this.geology.advance(deltaYears);

    const initialTreeCount = this.trees.length;

    // Rebuild environmental canopy information
    this.environment.rebuildCanopyGrid(
        this.trees
    );

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < initialTreeCount;
        i++
    ) {

        const tree = this.trees[i];

        if (!tree.alive) continue;

        const wasAlive = tree.alive;

tree.update(
    deltaYears,
    this.environment,
    this.hydrology
);
        if (!tree.alive && wasAlive) {

            this.renderDirty = true;

        }

        if (tree.alive && tree.isMature) {

            const offspring =
                this.spawnOffspring(tree);

            if (offspring > 0) {
                this.renderDirty = true;
            }

        }

    }

    this.simulationSteps++;

    if (
        this.renderDirty ||
        this.simulationSteps >= this.renderEverySteps
    ) {

        this.renderAll();

        this.renderDirty = false;

        this.simulationSteps = 0;

    }

}


updateRendering() {

    if (!this.renderDirty) return;

    this.renderAll();

    this.renderDirty = false;
}

renderAll() {

    this.renderers.oak.matrices = [];
    this.renderers.pine.matrices = [];

    this.foliageRenderers.oak.matrices = [];
    this.foliageRenderers.pine.matrices = [];

	
const camera = this.scene.activeCamera;

const cameraPosition = camera
    ? camera.globalPosition
    : BABYLON.Vector3.Zero();

const maxRenderDistance = 250;

const nearDistance = 80;
const mediumDistance = 180;

const maxRenderDistanceSquared =
    maxRenderDistance * maxRenderDistance;

const nearDistanceSquared =
    nearDistance * nearDistance;

const mediumDistanceSquared =
    mediumDistance * mediumDistance;

for (const tree of this.trees) {

    if (!tree.alive) continue;

    const dx =
        tree.position.x - cameraPosition.x;

    const dz =
        tree.position.z - cameraPosition.z;

    const distanceSquared =
        dx * dx + dz * dz;

    // Cull distant trees
    if (
        distanceSquared >
        maxRenderDistanceSquared
    ) {
        continue;
    }

    // Determine geometry detail
    let detailLevel;

    if (distanceSquared < nearDistanceSquared) {

        detailLevel = 2;

    } else if (
        distanceSquared < mediumDistanceSquared
    ) {

        detailLevel = 1;

    } else {

        detailLevel = 0;

    }

    const generator = new TreeGenerator(
        tree.species,
        this.environment,
        tree.genome
    );

    const branches = generator.generate(
        tree.position,
        tree.ageFactor,
        tree.thicknessFactor,
        detailLevel
    );

    this.renderers[tree.speciesName]
        .addMatrices(branches);

    const foliage = generator.foliage;

    const renderer =
        this.foliageRenderers[tree.speciesName];

    for (const cluster of foliage) {

        let scale = cluster.scale;

        if (tree.speciesName === "oak") {

            scale *= 1.5;

        } else {

            scale *= 1.15;

        }

        renderer.add(
            cluster.position,
            scale
        );

    }

}


    // Upload branch buffers
    this.renderers.oak.update();
    this.renderers.pine.update();

    // Upload foliage buffers
    this.foliageRenderers.oak.update();
    this.foliageRenderers.pine.update();


	// Update geological formations.
this.rockRenderer.update();
// Update atmospheric rendering.

this.cloudSystem.updateRendering();
}


generateWorldObjects(cloudCount = 35) {

    this.cloudSystem.generate(cloudCount);

}




}
export { Forest }


========================================================================
FILE: src/ecology/tree-generator.js
========================================================================

function generateCanopy(tree, ageFactor, thicknessFactor = ageFactor) {

    const species = tree.species;
    const genome = tree.genome;

    const canopy = species.canopy;

    const height =
        species.trunkLength *
        ageFactor *
        genome.maxHeight;

    const growth = Math.max(0.04, ageFactor);

    const centerY = tree.position.y + height * (canopy.startHeight + canopy.height * 0.5);

    const canopyHeight =
        height * canopy.height;

    const canopyWidth =
        height *
        canopy.width *
        genome.canopyWidth *
        growth;

    const clusters = [];

    const layers = canopy.layers;

    for (let layer = 0; layer < layers; layer++) {

        const t = layers === 1
            ? 0.5
            : layer / (layers - 1);

        const y = centerY +
            (t - 0.5) * canopyHeight;

        let layerRadius;

        if (canopy.shape === "round") {

            // Broad crown: widest around the middle
            layerRadius =
                canopyWidth *
                Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 -
                    Math.pow(2 * t - 1, 2)));

        } else {

            // Conical crown: widest at the bottom
            layerRadius =
                canopyWidth * (1 - t) * 0.95 +
                canopyWidth * 0.05;

        }

        const count = Math.max(
            3,
            Math.round(8 * canopy.density)
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

            const angle =
                2 * Math.PI * i / count +
                layer * 0.35;

            const radius =
                layerRadius *
                (0.65 + Math.random() * 0.35);

            const position = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                tree.position.x +
                    Math.cos(angle) * radius,

                y,

                tree.position.z +
                    Math.sin(angle) * radius
            );

            const leafScale =
                Math.max(0.04, height * 0.055) *
                genome.leafSize *
                growth;

            clusters.push({
                position,
                scale: leafScale
            });
        }
    }

    return clusters;
}

function DirectionRotation(direction) {

    const up = BABYLON.Axis.Y;

    const dir = direction.normalize();

    const dot = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(up, dir),
        -1,
        1
    );

    if (dot > 0.99999) {
        return BABYLON.Quaternion.Identity();
    }

    if (dot < -0.99999) {
        return BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationAxis(
            BABYLON.Axis.X,
            Math.PI
        );
    }

    const axis = BABYLON.Vector3.Cross(
        up,
        dir
    ).normalize();

    return BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationAxis(
        axis,
        Math.acos(dot)
    );
}



function makeBranchMatrix(
    start,
    direction,
    length,
    baseRadius
) {

    const dir = direction.normalize();

    const rotation = DirectionRotation(dir);

    const center = start.add(
        dir.scale(length / 2)
    );

    // Prototype bottom radius is 0.5
    const radialScale = baseRadius / 0.5;

    const scale = new BABYLON.Vector3(
        radialScale,
        length,
        radialScale
    );

    return BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
        scale,
        rotation,
        center
    );
}


// Create a coordinate system perpendicular to a direction
function getRadialDirection(direction, phi) {

    const reference =
        Math.abs(direction.y) < 0.95
            ? BABYLON.Axis.Y
            : BABYLON.Axis.X;

    const tangent = BABYLON.Vector3.Cross(
        direction,
        reference
    ).normalize();

    const bitangent = BABYLON.Vector3.Cross(
        direction,
        tangent
    ).normalize();

    return tangent.scale(Math.cos(phi))
        .add(bitangent.scale(Math.sin(phi)))
        .normalize();
}

class TreeGenerator {

    constructor(species, environment, genome) {

        this.species = species;
        this.environment = environment;
        this.genome = genome;
	this.currentAgeFactor = 0;

        this.matrices = [];
	this.foliage = [];
    }


generate(
    position,
    ageFactor = 1,
    thicknessFactor = 1,
    detailLevel = 2
) {

	this.matrices = [];
	this.foliage = [];
	this.currentAgeFactor = ageFactor;


const growth = ageFactor * ageFactor *
    (3 - 2 * ageFactor);

const s = this.species;
const g = this.genome;

// Height
const trunkLength =
    s.trunkLength * growth * g.maxHeight;

// Thickness
const trunkRadius =
    s.trunkRadius *
    Math.sqrt(Math.max(ageFactor, 0.001)) *
    g.trunkThickness;

// Branch dimensions
const branchLength =
    s.branchLength * growth * g.maxHeight;

const branchRadius =
    s.branchRadius *
    Math.sqrt(Math.max(ageFactor, 0.001)) *
    g.branchThickness;
//generate the trunk
this.matrices.push(
    makeBranchMatrix(
        position,
        BABYLON.Axis.Y,
        trunkLength,
        trunkRadius
    )
);
        const trunkEnd = position.add(
            BABYLON.Axis.Y.scale(trunkLength)
        );
	const canopyScale =
    growth *
    this.genome.canopyWidth *
    this.genome.leafSize;

	// Simplified distant canopy
if (detailLevel === 0 && ageFactor >= 0.2) {

    this.foliage.push({

        position: trunkEnd.clone(),

        scale: Math.max(
            0.2,
            canopyScale * 2.0
        )

    });

}


if (ageFactor < 0.2) {

    this.foliage.push({
        position: trunkEnd.clone(),
        scale: Math.max(0.08, canopyScale * 1.2)
    });

}

        // Branch complexity increases with age
let maxDepth =
    ageFactor < 0.2 ? 0 :
    ageFactor < 0.5 ? 1 :
    s.maxDepth;

// Distance-based geometry reduction
if (detailLevel === 1) {

    maxDepth = Math.min(maxDepth, 1);

} else if (detailLevel === 0) {

    maxDepth = 0;

}
        this.growBranch(
            trunkEnd,
            BABYLON.Axis.Y,
            branchLength,
            branchRadius,
            0,
            maxDepth
        );

        return this.matrices;
    }


    growBranch(
        start,
        parentDirection,
        length,
        radius,
        depth,
        maxDepth
    ) {

        if (depth >= maxDepth) {
            return;
        }

        const s = this.species;
        const g = this.genome;

        const branchCount = Math.max(
            1,
            Math.round(s.branchCount * g.branchDensity)
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < branchCount; i++) {

            const phi =
                2 * Math.PI * i / branchCount;

            const radial = getRadialDirection(
                parentDirection,
                phi
            );

            const angle =
                s.branchAngle * g.branchAngle;

            const intrinsicDirection =
                parentDirection.scale(
                    Math.cos(angle)
                ).add(
                    radial.scale(
                        Math.sin(angle)
                    )
                ).normalize();

            const light =
                this.environment.sampleLight(start);

            const direction = BABYLON.Vector3.Lerp(
                intrinsicDirection,
                light.direction,
                s.lightSensitivity *
                g.lightSensitivity *
                light.intensity
            ).normalize();

this.matrices.push(
    makeBranchMatrix(
        start,
        direction,
        length,
        radius
    )
);
            const end = start.add(
                direction.scale(length)
            );

		const ageFactor = this.currentAgeFactor;

let foliageScale =
    (0.25 + ageFactor * 0.75) *
    this.genome.leafSize *
    this.genome.canopyWidth;

if (s.branching === "radial") {

    // Oak: broad, substantial foliage clusters
    foliageScale *= 1.5;

} else if (s.branching === "whorled") {

    // Pine: smaller, more compact clusters
    foliageScale *= 1.15;

}

if ( depth === maxDepth - 1 ) {

    this.foliage.push({
        position: end.clone(),
        scale: foliageScale
    });

}

if (s.branching === "whorled") {

    const leader = BABYLON.Vector3.Lerp(
        parentDirection,
        light.direction,
        s.lightSensitivity *
        g.lightSensitivity *
        light.intensity
    ).normalize();

    // Keep the visual leader branch
    this.matrices.push(
        makeBranchMatrix(
            end,
            leader,
            length * s.lengthDecay,
            radius * s.radiusDecay
        )
    );

    // Do not recursively grow another leader branch.
}

// Continue normal branch growth
this.growBranch(
    end,
    direction,
    length * s.lengthDecay,
    radius * s.radiusDecay,
    depth + 1,
    maxDepth
);
        }
    }

}
export { TreeGenerator , DirectionRotation }


========================================================================
FILE: src/ecology/tree.js
========================================================================

import { Species } from "../core/species.js";
import { Genetics } from "../core/genetics.js";
import { TreeGenerator } from "./tree-generator.js";
let nextTreeId = 0;

class Tree {

	constructor(
    speciesName,
    position,
    genome = Genetics.createGenome(),
    parentId = null,
    initialAge = 0
) {

    this.id = nextTreeId++;

    this.speciesName = speciesName;

    this.species = Species[speciesName];

    this.position = position.clone();

    this.genome = genome;

    this.parentId = parentId;

    this.age = initialAge;

    this.seedTimer = 0;

this.soilSample = null;
this.soilSampleTimer = 0;
this.soilSuitability = 1.0;
    // Environmental conditions

    this.lightLevel = 1.0;

    this.lightStress = 0;

    this.lowLightYears = 0;

    this.growthMultiplier = 1.0;


    // Hydrological conditions

    this.soilMoisture = 0.35;

    this.moistureMultiplier = 1.0;

    this.droughtYears = 0;

    this.waterloggingYears = 0;


    this.children = [];

    this.alive = true;

}

	get thicknessFactor() {

    return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        this.age / (this.maturityAge * 2),
        0,
        1
    );
}

    get maturityAge() {

        return 10 / this.genome.growthRate;
    }


    get maximumAge() {

        return 100 * this.genome.lifespan;
    }


    get ageFactor() {

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            this.age / this.maturityAge,
            0,
            1
        );
    }


    get isMature() {

        return this.age >= this.maturityAge;
    }


    get isElder() {

        return this.age >= this.maximumAge * 0.85;
    }



update(deltaYears, environment, hydrology) {

    if (!this.alive) {
        return;
    }


    // --------------------------------
    // LIGHT CONDITIONS
    // --------------------------------

    const light = environment.sampleLight(
        this.position
    );

    this.lightLevel = light.intensity;


    const minimumLight =
        this.species.lightSensitivity * 0.20;


    this.growthMultiplier =
        BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(

            0.25 + this.lightLevel * 0.75,

            0.25,

            1.0

        );


    if (this.lightLevel < minimumLight) {

        this.lowLightYears += deltaYears;

    } else {

        this.lowLightYears = Math.max(

            0,

            this.lowLightYears - deltaYears * 0.5

        );

    }


    if (this.lowLightYears >= 15) {

        this.alive = false;

        return;

    }


    // --------------------------------
    // SOIL MOISTURE
    // --------------------------------

	this.soilSampleTimer -= deltaYears;

if (
    hydrology &&
    this.soilSampleTimer <= 0
) {
    this.soilSample = hydrology.sampleSoil(
        this.position
    );

    this.soilSampleTimer = 5.0;
}

	const optimalMoisture = this.species.optimalMoisture;


	const droughtTolerance = this.species.droughtTolerance;


	const waterloggingTolerance = this.species.waterloggingTolerance;

if (hydrology && typeof hydrology.sampleSoil === "function") {
	const soil = this.soilSample;

    if (soil) {
        const affinity = this.species.soilAffinity;

        const textureScore = affinity.preferred.includes(
            soil.surfaceSoil
        ) ? 1.0 : affinity.tolerance;

        const fertilityScore = 1.0 - Math.abs(
            soil.fertility - affinity.fertility
        );

        const drainageScore = 1.0 - Math.abs(
            soil.drainage - affinity.drainage
        );

        const moistureScore = 1.0 - Math.abs(
            this.soilMoisture - affinity.moisture
        );

        this.soilSuitability = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            textureScore * 0.40 +
            fertilityScore * 0.20 +
            drainageScore * 0.20 +
            moistureScore * 0.20,
            0.15,
            1.0
        );
	    }
	}




    // Growth is best near the species'
    // preferred soil moisture level.

    this.moistureMultiplier =
        BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(

            0.45 +
            0.55 * Math.min(
                1,
                this.soilMoisture /
                optimalMoisture
            ),

            0.45,

            1.0

        );


    // Drought stress

    if (
        this.soilMoisture <
        droughtTolerance
    ) {

        this.droughtYears += deltaYears;

    } else {

        this.droughtYears = Math.max(

            0,

            this.droughtYears - deltaYears * 0.5

        );

    }


    // Waterlogging stress

    if (
        this.soilMoisture >
        waterloggingTolerance
    ) {

        this.waterloggingYears += deltaYears;

    } else {

        this.waterloggingYears = Math.max(

            0,

            this.waterloggingYears - deltaYears * 0.5

        );

    }


    // Prolonged environmental stress.

    if (
        this.droughtYears >= 20 ||
        this.waterloggingYears >= 15
    ) {

        this.alive = false;

        return;

    }


    // --------------------------------
    // COMBINED GROWTH
    // --------------------------------

const combinedGrowth =
    this.growthMultiplier *
    this.moistureMultiplier *
    this.soilSuitability;

    this.age += deltaYears * combinedGrowth;


    if (this.age >= this.maximumAge) {

        this.alive = false;

    }


    this.seedTimer += deltaYears;

}


    generateGeometry(environment) {

        const generator = new TreeGenerator(
            this.species,
            environment,
            this.genome
        );

        return generator.generate(
            this.position,
            this.ageFactor
        );
    }

	
produceSeeds() {

    if (!this.alive || !this.isMature || this.isElder) {
        return 0;
    }

    // One reproductive opportunity every five years
    const seedInterval = 5;

    if (this.seedTimer < seedInterval) {
        return 0;
    }

    this.seedTimer -= seedInterval;

    // Genetic influence on reproduction
    const seedProduction = this.genome.seedProduction;

    // Number of seeds produced in this event
    return Math.max(
        1,
        Math.round(2 * seedProduction)
    );
}


}
export { Tree }


========================================================================
FILE: src/editor/building-editor.js
========================================================================


import { EditorCamera } from "./editor-camera.js";
import { BuildingPlacement } from "./building-placement.js";
import { FloorPlanReference } from "./floor-plan-reference.js";
import { FloorPlanBuilder } from "./floor-plan-builder.js";
import {
    FloorPlanHeightControl
} from "./floor-plan-height-control.js";
import {
    BuildingPreviewRenderer
} from "./building-preview-renderer.js";

class BuildingEditor {
    constructor(scene, buildingSystem, spatialTransform) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.system = buildingSystem;
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        // Building geometry must use the authoritative
        // world-to-render coordinate transformation.
        this.system.renderer.transform = spatialTransform;

        this.editorCamera = new EditorCamera(
            scene,
            spatialTransform
        );

        this.placement = new BuildingPlacement(
            buildingSystem,
            this.editorCamera
        );

        this.floorPlan = new FloorPlanReference(
            scene,
            spatialTransform
        );
this.floorPlanBuilder = new FloorPlanBuilder({
    wallHeight: 3.2,
    doorHeight: 2.1,
    windowHeight: 1.2,
    windowSill: 0.9
});

        this.mode = "room";
        this.gridSize = 1;
        this.activeBuilding = null;
        this.selectedRoom = null;
        this.startPoint = null;
        this.enabled = false;

        this.createInterface();
this.previewRenderer = new BuildingPreviewRenderer(
    scene,
    buildingSystem.geometryRenderer
);

this.floorPlanHeightControl =
    new FloorPlanHeightControl(
        this.container,
        this.floorPlanBuilder,
        () => {
            if (
                this.activeBuilding &&
                this.floorPlan.mesh
            ) {
                this.buildFloorFromPlan();
            }
        }
    );

this.installFloorPlanWorkflowControls();

        this.pointerObserver =
            scene.onPointerObservable.add(info => {
                if (!this.enabled) return;

                if (
                    info.type === BABYLON.PointerEventTypes.POINTERDOWN
                ) {
                    this.pointerDown();
                }

                if (
                    info.type === BABYLON.PointerEventTypes.POINTERUP
                ) {
                    this.pointerUp();
                }
            });
    }

	setGameManager(manager) {
    this.gameManager = manager;

    manager.registerEditor(this.editorCamera.camera);

    this.unsubscribeMode = manager.onModeChanged(mode => {
        const active = mode === "editor";

        this.enabled = active;
        this.container.style.display =
            active ? "block" : "none";

        if (active && this.activeBuilding) {
            this.editorCamera.focus(
                this.activeBuilding.position
            );
        }
    });
}

    createInterface() {
        this.container = document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(this.container.style, {
            position: "fixed",
            top: "70px",
            left: "18px",
            width: "280px",
            maxHeight: "85vh",
            overflowY: "auto",
            padding: "16px",
            background: "rgba(19,29,40,0.97)",
            border: "1px solid #536779",
            borderRadius: "8px",
            color: "#fff",
            fontFamily: "Arial,sans-serif",
            zIndex: "1600",
            display: "none"
        });

        this.container.innerHTML = `
            <h3>BUILDING EDITOR</h3>

<div style="display:grid;gap:7px">
    <button data-mode="room">DRAW ROOM</button>
    <button data-mode="door">ADD DOOR</button>
    <button data-mode="window">ADD WINDOW</button>
    <button data-mode="furniture">PLACE FURNITURE</button>
    <button data-mode="floor-plan">POSITION FLOOR PLAN</button>
</div>
            <hr>

            <h4>FLOOR PLAN</h4>
            <input type="file" id="floor-image" accept="image/*">

            <label>Width
                <input id="floor-width" type="number" min="0.1" value="20">
            </label>
	<label>
    Floor Plan Altitude Offset

    <input
        id="floor-altitude"
        type="number"
        step="0.1"
        value="0"
    >
</label>

	<button id="build-floor" style="width:100%">
		BUILD FLOOR FROM PLAN
	</button>

            <label>Depth
                <input id="floor-depth" type="number" min="0.1" value="20">
            </label>

            <label>Opacity
                <input id="floor-opacity" type="range"
                    min="0" max="1" step="0.05" value="0.8">
            </label>

            <label>
                <input id="floor-visible" type="checkbox" checked>
                Show floor plan
            </label>

            <hr>

            <h4>BUILDING POSITION</h4>

            <label>X
                <input id="position-x" type="number" step="0.1" value="0">
            </label>

            <label>Z
                <input id="position-z" type="number" step="0.1" value="0">
            </label>

            <label>Elevation
                <input id="position-y" type="number" step="0.1" value="0">
            </label>

            <label>Rotation (degrees)
                <input id="position-rotation" type="number"
                    step="1" value="0">
            </label>

            <hr>

            <label>Grid size
                <input id="building-grid" type="number"
                    min="0.1" step="0.1" value="1">
            </label>

            <p id="building-status" style="font-size:12px;color:#b8c8d7">
                Select a construction tool.
            </p>

            <button id="building-close" style="width:100%">
                CLOSE EDITOR
            </button>
        `;

        document.body.appendChild(this.container);

        this.status = this.container.querySelector(
            "#building-status"
        );

        this.container.querySelectorAll("[data-mode]")
            .forEach(button => {
                button.onclick = () => this.setMode(
                    button.dataset.mode
                );
            });

        const element = id =>
            this.container.querySelector(`#${id}`);

        element("building-grid").onchange = event => {
            this.gridSize = Math.max(
                0.1,
                Number(event.target.value) || 1
            );
        };

	element("floor-image").onchange = async event => {
    try {
	if (
    this.activeBuilding?.metadata?.editorPreview
) {
    this.setStatus(
        "Please place the current building before importing another plan."
    );

    event.target.value = "";
    return;
}
        await this.floorPlan.load(
            event.target.files[0]
        );
	const width = Number(
    this.container.querySelector("#floor-width").value
);

const depth = width *
    this.floorPlan.image.naturalHeight /
    this.floorPlan.image.naturalWidth;

this.container.querySelector(
    "#floor-depth"
).value = depth.toFixed(2);

this.aspectLock.checked = true;

this.floorPlan.setDimensions(width, depth);

        this.ensureBuildingForFloorPlan();
        this.updateFloorPlanPosition();
	if (
    this.activeBuilding?.metadata?.editorPreview
) {
    this.previewRenderer.show(this.activeBuilding);
}

        this.setStatus(
            "Floor plan imported. Position it, then build the floor."
        );
    } catch (error) {
        this.setStatus(error.message);
    }
};
element("floor-altitude").addEventListener(
    "input",
    event => {
        this.floorPlan.setAltitudeOffset(
            Number(event.target.value) || 0
        );
    }
);

element("build-floor").onclick = () =>
    this.buildFloorFromPlan();

        element("floor-opacity").oninput = event =>
            this.floorPlan.setOpacity(Number(event.target.value));

        element("floor-visible").onchange = event =>
            this.floorPlan.setVisible(event.target.checked);

        for (const id of ["floor-width", "floor-depth"]) {
            element(id).onchange = () => {
                this.updateFloorPlanPosition({
                    width: Number(element("floor-width").value),
                    depth: Number(element("floor-depth").value)
                });
            };
        }

const updatePlacement = () => {
    if (!this.activeBuilding) return;

    this.placement.update({
        x: Number(element("position-x").value),
        z: Number(element("position-z").value),
        y: Number(element("position-y").value),
        rotation:
            Number(element("position-rotation").value) *
            Math.PI / 180
    });

    this.updateFloorPlanPosition();

    if (
        this.activeBuilding.metadata?.editorPreview
    ) {
        this.previewRenderer.show(
            this.activeBuilding
        );
    }
};
        for (const id of [
            "position-x",
            "position-z",
            "position-y",
            "position-rotation"
        ]) {
            element(id).onchange = updatePlacement;
        }

        element("building-close").onclick = () => this.close();

        this.launcher = document.createElement("button");
        this.launcher.textContent = "BUILD";

        Object.assign(this.launcher.style, {
            position: "fixed",
            top: "18px",
            left: "18px",
            zIndex: "1601",
            padding: "12px 20px",
            cursor: "pointer"
        });

        this.launcher.onclick = () => this.open();
        document.body.appendChild(this.launcher);
    }

    setStatus(message) {
        this.status.textContent = message;
    }

    setMode(mode) {
        this.mode = mode;
        this.startPoint = null;
        this.setStatus(`Active tool: ${mode}`);
    }

    open() {
        this.container.style.display = "block";
        this.enabled = true;
        this.gameManager?.startEditor();

        if (this.activeBuilding) {
            this.editorCamera.focus(this.activeBuilding.position);
        }
    }

    close() {
        this.container.style.display = "none";
        this.enabled = false;
        this.startPoint = null;
        this.gameManager?.exitEditor();
    }

    snap(value) {
        return Math.round(value / this.gridSize) *
            this.gridSize;
    }

    pickWorldPosition() {
        const pick = this.scene.pick(
            this.scene.pointerX,
            this.scene.pointerY,
            mesh => mesh.isPickable &&
                mesh.metadata?.terrainChunk === true
        );

        if (!pick?.hit || !pick.pickedPoint) return null;

        return this.transform.renderToWorld({
            x: pick.pickedPoint.x,
            y: pick.pickedPoint.y,
            z: pick.pickedPoint.z
        });
    }

    pointerDown() {
	    if (
    this.mode === "floor-plan" ||
    this.mode !== "room"
) {
    return;
}
        const point = this.pickWorldPosition();

        if (this.mode !== "room") return;

        if (!point) {
            this.setStatus("Cannot pick terrain.");
            return;
        }

        this.startPoint = point;
        this.setStatus("Drag to define the room footprint.");
    }

    pointerUp() {
	if (this.mode === "floor-plan") {
    return;
}
        const point = this.pickWorldPosition();

        if (!point) {
            if (this.startPoint) {
                this.startPoint = null;
                this.setStatus("No surface detected.");
            }
            return;
        }

        if (this.mode === "room") {
            if (!this.startPoint) return;

            const start = this.startPoint;
            this.startPoint = null;

            try {
                this.createRoomFromPoints(start, point);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("[BuildingEditor]", error);
                this.setStatus(error.message);
            }

            return;
        }

        if (this.mode === "door" || this.mode === "window") {
            this.placeOpening(this.mode, point);
        }

        if (this.mode === "furniture") {
            this.placeFurniture(point);
        }
    }


createRoomFromPoints(a, b) {
    const minX = this.snap(Math.min(a.x, b.x));
    const maxX = this.snap(Math.max(a.x, b.x));
    const minZ = this.snap(Math.min(a.z, b.z));
    const maxZ = this.snap(Math.max(a.z, b.z));

    if (
        maxX - minX < this.gridSize ||
        maxZ - minZ < this.gridSize
    ) {
        this.setStatus("Room is too small.");
        return;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // VALIDATE ACTIVE BUILDING REGISTRATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    let building = this.activeBuilding;

    if (building) {
        const registered = this.system.getBuilding(
            building.id
        );

        if (registered) {
            // Adopt the authoritative registered instance.
            building = registered;
            this.activeBuilding = registered;
        } else {
            // The building was removed, possibly by
            // restoring another world.
            console.warn(
                "[BuildingEditor] Active building is no longer registered.",
                building.id
            );

            this.activeBuilding = null;
            this.selectedRoom = null;

            this.placement.select(null);

            building = null;
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CREATE BUILDING IF NECESSARY
    // -----------------------------------------

    if (!building) {
        building = this.system.createBuilding({
            name: "My Building",
            position: {
                x: minX,
                y: a.y,
                z: minZ
            }
        });

        this.activeBuilding = building;

        this.placement.select(building);
        this.syncPositionInputs();
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CREATE ROOM
    // -----------------------------------------

    // Final registration invariant.
    if (
        this.system.getBuilding(building.id) !== building
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Building registration mismatch."
        );
    }

    const room = this.system.createRoom(
        building.id,
        {
            name: `Room ${building.rooms.length + 1}`,

            minX: minX - building.position.x,
            maxX: maxX - building.position.x,

            minZ: minZ - building.position.z,
            maxZ: maxZ - building.position.z
        }
    );

    this.selectedRoom = room;

    this.updateFloorPlanPosition();

    this.setStatus(
        `Created ${room.name}: ` +
        `${maxX - minX} × ${maxZ - minZ}`
    );
}

    placeOpening(type, point) {
        if (!this.activeBuilding || !this.selectedRoom) {
            this.setStatus("Create a room first.");
            return;
        }

        const building = this.activeBuilding;
        const bounds = this.selectedRoom.bounds;

        const x = point.x - building.position.x;
        const z = point.z - building.position.z;

        const distances = [
            { side: "north", distance: Math.abs(z - bounds.minZ),
                offset: (x - bounds.minX) /
                    (bounds.maxX - bounds.minX) },
            { side: "south", distance: Math.abs(z - bounds.maxZ),
                offset: (x - bounds.minX) /
                    (bounds.maxX - bounds.minX) },
            { side: "west", distance: Math.abs(x - bounds.minX),
                offset: (z - bounds.minZ) /
                    (bounds.maxZ - bounds.minZ) },
            { side: "east", distance: Math.abs(x - bounds.maxX),
                offset: (z - bounds.minZ) /
                    (bounds.maxZ - bounds.minZ) }
        ];

        distances.sort((a, b) => a.distance - b.distance);

        const nearest = distances[0];

        if (nearest.offset < 0 || nearest.offset > 1) {
            this.setStatus("Position is outside the selected wall.");
            return;
        }

        const opening = this.system.addOpening(
            building.id,
            this.selectedRoom.id,
            {
                type,
                side: nearest.side,
                offset: nearest.offset,
                width: type === "door" ? 0.9 : 1.4,
                height: type === "door" ? 2.1 : 1.2,
                sill: type === "door" ? 0 : 0.9
            }
        );

        this.setStatus(`Added ${type}: ${opening.id}`);
    }

    placeFurniture(position) {
        if (!this.activeBuilding) {
            this.setStatus("Create a building first.");
            return;
        }

        this.system.addFurniture(
            this.activeBuilding.id,
            {
                type: "table",
                position: {
                    x: position.x - this.activeBuilding.position.x,
                    y: position.y - this.activeBuilding.position.y + 0.5,
                    z: position.z - this.activeBuilding.position.z
                },
                dimensions: { x: 1.6, y: 0.9, z: 1 },
                material: "wood"
            }
        );

        this.setStatus("Furniture placed.");
    }

    syncPositionInputs() {
        if (!this.activeBuilding) return;

        const p = this.activeBuilding.position;
        const set = (id, value) => {
            this.container.querySelector(`#${id}`).value = value;
        };

        set("position-x", p.x);
        set("position-y", p.y);
        set("position-z", p.z);
        set(
            "position-rotation",
            this.activeBuilding.rotation * 180 / Math.PI
        );
    }


updateFloorPlanPosition(dimensions = {}) {
    if (!this.activeBuilding) return;

    this.floorPlan.updateTransform(
        this.activeBuilding.position,
        dimensions,
        this.activeBuilding.rotation
    );
}

    dispose() {
        this.enabled = false;

        if (this.pointerObserver) {
            this.scene.onPointerObservable.remove(
                this.pointerObserver
            );
        }

        this.floorPlan.dispose();
        this.editorCamera.dispose();
	this.floorPlanHeightControl?.dispose();

        this.container?.remove();
        this.launcher?.remove();

        this.container = null;
        this.launcher = null;

	this.unsubscribeMode?.();
	this.previewRenderer?.dispose();
    }

worldToBuildingLocal(position) {
    const building = this.activeBuilding;
    const dx = position.x - building.position.x;
    const dz = position.z - building.position.z;

    const angle = building.rotation;
    const cos = Math.cos(angle);
    const sin = Math.sin(angle);

    return {
        x: dx * cos + dz * sin,
        y: position.y - building.position.y,
        z: -dx * sin + dz * cos
    };
}


ensureBuildingForFloorPlan() {
    if (this.activeBuilding) return;

    const element = id =>
        this.container.querySelector(`#${id}`);

    this.activeBuilding = this.system.createBuilding({
        name: "Floor Plan Building",
        position: {
            x: Number(element("position-x").value) || 0,
            y: Number(element("position-y").value) || 0,
            z: Number(element("position-z").value) || 0
        },
        rotation:
            Number(element("position-rotation").value || 0) *
            Math.PI / 180
    });
	this.activeBuilding.metadata ??= {};

this.activeBuilding.metadata.editorPreview = true;

    this.placement.select(this.activeBuilding);
    this.syncPositionInputs();
}

async buildFloorFromPlan() {
    try {
	if (!this.floorPlan.mesh || !this.floorPlan.image) {
    this.setStatus(
        "Import a floor-plan image before constructing a building."
    );

    return null;
}

        this.ensureBuildingForFloorPlan();

        const element = id =>
            this.container.querySelector(`#${id}`);

        const imageData = await this.floorPlan.readPixels();

        const result = this.floorPlanBuilder.build(
            this.activeBuilding,
            imageData,
            {
                width: Number(element("floor-width").value),
                depth: Number(element("floor-depth").value),
                floor: 0
            }
        );

        this.system.rebuild(this.activeBuilding);
	this.activeBuilding.metadata ??= {};

this.activeBuilding.metadata.editorPreview = true;

const outlineCount =
    this.previewRenderer.show(this.activeBuilding);

this.placeButton.disabled = false;

this.setStatus(
    `Preview ready: ${result.walls} walls, ` +
    `${result.doors} doors, ${result.windows} windows. ` +
    `${outlineCount} highlighted components. ` +
    `Adjust dimensions or place the building.`
);


        return result;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("[BuildingEditor] Floor construction:", error);
        this.setStatus(error.message);
        return null;
    }
}


installFloorPlanWorkflowControls() {
    const buildButton =
        this.container.querySelector("#build-floor");

    const placeButton = document.createElement("button");

    placeButton.id = "place-building";
    placeButton.textContent =
        "PLACE BUILDING & START NEXT";

    placeButton.disabled = true;

    placeButton.style.width = "100%";
    placeButton.style.marginTop = "8px";

    buildButton.after(placeButton);

    placeButton.onclick = () => {
        this.placeCurrentBuilding();
    };

    this.placeButton = placeButton;

    const depthInput =
        this.container.querySelector("#floor-depth");

    const aspectLabel = document.createElement("label");

    aspectLabel.style.display = "block";
    aspectLabel.style.margin = "8px 0";

    this.aspectLock = document.createElement("input");
    this.aspectLock.type = "checkbox";
    this.aspectLock.checked = true;

    aspectLabel.append(
        this.aspectLock,
        document.createTextNode(
            " Maintain image aspect ratio"
        )
    );

    depthInput.parentElement.after(aspectLabel);

    const updateDimensions = source => {
        const width = Number(
            this.container.querySelector("#floor-width").value
        );

        let depth = Number(depthInput.value);

        if (
            this.aspectLock.checked &&
            this.floorPlan.image
        ) {
            if (source === "depth") {
                this.aspectLock.checked = false;
            } else {
                depth = width *
                    this.floorPlan.image.naturalHeight /
                    this.floorPlan.image.naturalWidth;

                depthInput.value = depth.toFixed(2);
            }
        }

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(width) ||
            !Number.isFinite(depth) ||
            width <= 0 ||
            depth <= 0
        ) {
            return;
        }

        this.updateFloorPlanPosition({
            width,
            depth
        });
if (
    this.activeBuilding?.metadata?.editorPreview === true &&
    this.floorPlan.mesh &&
    this.floorPlan.image
) {
    this.buildFloorFromPlan();
}
    };

    this.container.querySelector(
        "#floor-width"
    ).onchange = () => updateDimensions("width");

    depthInput.onchange = () => updateDimensions("depth");

    this.aspectLock.onchange = () => {
        updateDimensions("width");
    };
}

placeCurrentBuilding() {
    const building = this.activeBuilding;

    if (
        !building ||
        building.metadata?.editorPreview !== true
    ) {
        this.setStatus(
            "There is no provisional building to place."
        );
        return false;
    }

    // Commit the preview.
    this.previewRenderer.commit(building);

    building.metadata.editorPreview = false;

    this.system.refresh();

    // Release the current building selection.
    this.activeBuilding = null;
    this.selectedRoom = null;
    this.startPoint = null;

    this.placement.select(null);

    // Reset construction controls.
    this.placeButton.disabled = true;

    // Keep the previous image available as a reference,
    // but require an explicit new build operation.
    this.setStatus(
        "Building placed successfully. " +
        "Import another floor plan to construct the next building."
    );

    return true;
}



resetAfterWorldLoad() {
    this.activeBuilding = null;
    this.selectedRoom = null;
    this.startPoint = null;

    this.placement.select(null);

    if (this.placeButton) {
        this.placeButton.disabled = true;
    }

    this.previewRenderer?.clear?.();

    this.setStatus(
        "World loaded. Select a construction tool to begin."
    );
}


}

export { BuildingEditor };


========================================================================
FILE: src/editor/building-placement.js
========================================================================


class BuildingPlacement {
    constructor(system, editorCamera) {
        this.system = system;
        this.editorCamera = editorCamera;

        this.activeBuilding = null;
    }

    select(building) {
        this.activeBuilding = building;

        if (building) {
            this.editorCamera.focus(
                building.position
            );
        }
    }

    update(patch = {}) {
        const building = this.activeBuilding;

        if (!building) {
            throw new Error("No building selected.");
        }

        const position = building.position;

        if (Number.isFinite(patch.x)) {
            position.x = patch.x;
        }

        if (Number.isFinite(patch.z)) {
            position.z = patch.z;
        }

        if (Number.isFinite(patch.y)) {
            position.y = patch.y;
        }

        if (Number.isFinite(patch.rotation)) {
            building.rotation = patch.rotation;
        }

        this.system.rebuild(building);

        this.editorCamera.focus(position);
    }

    setHorizontal(x, z) {
        this.update({ x, z });
    }

    setElevation(y) {
        this.update({ y });
    }

    setRotation(rotation) {
        this.update({ rotation });
    }

    getState() {
        if (!this.activeBuilding) return null;

        return {
            position: { ...this.activeBuilding.position },
            rotation: this.activeBuilding.rotation
        };
    }
}

export { BuildingPlacement };

FILE: src/editor/building-preview-renderer.js
========================================================================


/**
 * BuildingPreviewRenderer
 *
 * Draws temporary architectural outlines without modifying
 * authoritative building geometry or shared render materials.
 */
class BuildingPreviewRenderer {
    constructor(scene, geometryRenderer) {
        if (!scene || !geometryRenderer) {
            throw new Error(
                "Preview renderer requires scene and GeometryRenderer."
            );
        }

        this.scene = scene;
        this.geometryRenderer = geometryRenderer;
        this.lines = [];
        this.buildingId = null;

        this.color = new BABYLON.Color3(
            0.15, 1.0, 0.75
        );
    }

    createComponentLines(component, building) {
        const p = component.position;
        const d = component.dimensions;

        if (
            !p || !d ||
            ![
                p.x, p.y, p.z,
                d.x, d.y, d.z,
                building.position.x,
                building.position.y,
                building.position.z
            ].every(Number.isFinite) ||
            d.x <= 0 || d.y <= 0 || d.z <= 0
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const angle = building.rotation ?? 0;
        const cos = Math.cos(angle);
        const sin = Math.sin(angle);

        const worldPosition = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            building.position.x + p.x * cos - p.z * sin,
            building.position.y + p.y,
            building.position.z + p.x * sin + p.z * cos
        );

        const rotation = BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationAxis(
            BABYLON.Axis.Y,
            angle + (component.rotation ?? 0)
        );

        const matrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
            new BABYLON.Vector3(d.x, d.y, d.z),
            rotation,
            worldPosition
        );

        const projected =
            this.geometryRenderer.transformInstanceMatrix(matrix);

        if (!projected) return null;

        const corners = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            const local = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                (i & 1) ? 0.5 : -0.5,
                (i & 2) ? 0.5 : -0.5,
                (i & 4) ? 0.5 : -0.5
            );

            corners.push(
                BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(
                    local,
                    projected
                )
            );
        }

        const edges = [
            [0, 1], [0, 2], [0, 4],
            [1, 3], [1, 5],
            [2, 3], [2, 6],
            [3, 7], [4, 5],
            [4, 6], [5, 7], [6, 7]
        ];

        return edges.map(([a, b]) => [
            corners[a],
            corners[b]
        ]);
    }

    show(building) {
        this.clear();

        if (!building?.generatedGeometry) {
            throw new Error(
                "Building has no generated geometry to preview."
            );
        }

        this.buildingId = building.id;

        for (const component of building.generatedGeometry) {
            const edges = this.createComponentLines(
                component,
                building
            );

            if (!edges) continue;

            const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateLineSystem(
                "building-preview-outline",
                { lines: edges },
                this.scene
            );

            mesh.color = this.color;
            mesh.alpha = 1;
            mesh.isPickable = false;
            mesh.renderingGroupId = 2;

            mesh.metadata = {
                type: "building-preview-outline",
                buildingId: building.id
            };

            this.lines.push(mesh);
        }

        return this.lines.length;
    }

    clear() {
        for (const mesh of this.lines) {
            mesh.dispose();
        }

        this.lines.length = 0;
        this.buildingId = null;
    }

    commit(building) {
        if (
            building &&
            building.id !== this.buildingId
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Cannot commit a different preview building."
            );
        }

        this.clear();
    }

    dispose() {
        this.clear();
    }
}

export { BuildingPreviewRenderer };


========================================================================
FILE: src/editor/editor-camera.js
========================================================================


class EditorCamera {
    constructor(scene, spatialTransform, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        this.camera = new BABYLON.ArcRotateCamera(
            "BuildingEditorCamera",
            -Math.PI / 2,
            Math.PI / 3,
            options.radius ?? 80,
            BABYLON.Vector3.Zero(),
            scene
        );

        this.camera.minZ = 0.01;
        this.camera.maxZ = 1e9;

        // Permit extremely close and distant inspection.
        this.camera.lowerRadiusLimit = 0.1;
        this.camera.upperRadiusLimit = Number.MAX_VALUE;

        this.camera.wheelPrecision = 5;
        this.camera.panningSensibility = 40;
        this.camera.angularSensibilityX = 1000;
        this.camera.angularSensibilityY = 1000;

        this.camera.inertia = 0.8;
        this.camera.detachControl();
    }

    focus(position, radius) {
        const rendered = this.transform.worldToRender(
            position
        );

        this.camera.setTarget(
            new BABYLON.Vector3(
                rendered.x,
                rendered.y,
                rendered.z
            )
        );

        if (Number.isFinite(radius)) {
            this.camera.radius = Math.max(0.1, radius);
        }
    }

    attach(canvas) {
        this.camera.attachControl(canvas, true);
    }

    detach() {
        this.camera.detachControl();
    }

    dispose() {
        this.detach();
        this.camera.dispose();
    }
}

export { EditorCamera };


========================================================================
FILE: src/editor/floor-plan-builder.js
========================================================================


class FloorPlanBuilder {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.wallHeight = options.wallHeight ?? 4;
        this.wallThickness = options.wallThickness ?? 0.35;
        this.doorHeight = options.doorHeight ?? 2.1;
        this.windowHeight = options.windowHeight ?? 1.2;
        this.windowSill = options.windowSill ?? 0.9;
        this.minimumSegmentPixels =
            options.minimumSegmentPixels ?? 5;
    }

    setWallHeight(value) {
        if (
            !Number.isFinite(value) ||
            value < 2.4 ||
            value > 1000
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Wall height must be between 2.4 and 1000."
            );
        }

        this.wallHeight = value;
        return value;
    }

    setWallThickness(value) {
        if (
            !Number.isFinite(value) ||
            value < 0.1 ||
            value > 1000
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Wall thickness must be between 0.1 and 1000."
            );
        }

        this.wallThickness = value;
        return value;
    }

    classify(r, g, b, a) {
        if (a < 128) return null;

        if (r < 75 && g < 75 && b < 75) {
            return "wall";
        }

        if (r > 150 && g < 110 && b < 110) {
            return "door";
        }

        if (g > 130 && r < 120 && b < 130) {
            return "window";
        }

        return null;
    }

    extractSegments(imageData) {
        const { data, width, height } = imageData;

        if (
            !data ||
            !Number.isInteger(width) ||
            !Number.isInteger(height) ||
            width <= 0 ||
            height <= 0 ||
            data.length < width * height * 4
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid floor-plan image data.");
        }

        const active = new Map();
        const completed = [];

        for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
            const current = new Set();
            let x = 0;

            while (x < width) {
                const index = (y * width + x) * 4;

                const kind = this.classify(
                    data[index],
                    data[index + 1],
                    data[index + 2],
                    data[index + 3]
                );

                if (!kind) {
                    x++;
                    continue;
                }

                const start = x;

                while (x < width) {
                    const i = (y * width + x) * 4;

                    if (
                        this.classify(
                            data[i],
                            data[i + 1],
                            data[i + 2],
                            data[i + 3]
                        ) !== kind
                    ) {
                        break;
                    }

                    x++;
                }

                const end = x - 1;
                const key = `${kind}:${start}:${end}`;

                current.add(key);

                const previous = active.get(key);

                if (previous) {
                    previous.maxY = y;
                } else {
                    active.set(key, {
                        kind,
                        minX: start,
                        maxX: end,
                        minY: y,
                        maxY: y
                    });
                }
            }

            for (const [key, segment] of active) {
                if (!current.has(key)) {
                    completed.push(segment);
                    active.delete(key);
                }
            }
        }

        completed.push(...active.values());

        return completed.filter(segment => {
            const w = segment.maxX - segment.minX + 1;
            const h = segment.maxY - segment.minY + 1;

            return Math.max(w, h) >= this.minimumSegmentPixels;
        });
    }

    build(building, imageData, options = {}) {
        if (
            !building ||
            typeof building.addComponent !== "function" ||
            !Array.isArray(building.components)
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid building model.");
        }

        const width = options.width ?? 20;
        const depth = options.depth ?? 20;
        const floorIndex = options.floor ?? 0;

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(width) ||
            !Number.isFinite(depth) ||
            width <= 0 ||
            depth <= 0 ||
            !Number.isInteger(floorIndex) ||
            floorIndex < 0
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid floor dimensions.");
        }

        const segments = this.extractSegments(imageData);

        if (!segments.length) {
            throw new Error(
                "No architectural lines detected."
            );
        }

        const scaleX = width / imageData.width;
        const scaleZ = depth / imageData.height;

        const floorY = floorIndex * this.wallHeight;
        const prefix = `floor-plan-${floorIndex}-`;

        const generated = [];

        const counts = {
            walls: 0,
            doors: 0,
            windows: 0,
            floors: 0
        };

        const add = (
            kind,
            type,
            position,
            dimensions,
            material
        ) => {
            generated.push({
                kind,
                type: prefix + type,
                position,
                dimensions,
                material
            });
        };

        add(
            "floors",
            "floor",
            { x: 0, y: floorY - 0.075, z: 0 },
            { x: width, y: 0.15, z: depth },
            "floor"
        );

        counts.floors++;

        for (const segment of segments) {
            const pixelWidth =
                segment.maxX - segment.minX + 1;

            const pixelHeight =
                segment.maxY - segment.minY + 1;

            const horizontal = pixelWidth >= pixelHeight;

            const centerPixelX =
                (segment.minX + segment.maxX + 1) * 0.5;

            const centerPixelY =
                (segment.minY + segment.maxY + 1) * 0.5;

            const x =
                (centerPixelX / imageData.width - 0.5) * width;

            const z =
                (centerPixelY / imageData.height - 0.5) * depth;

            const length = horizontal
                ? pixelWidth * scaleX
                : pixelHeight * scaleZ;

            const detectedThickness = Math.max(
                0.08,
                Math.min(
                    0.35,
                    horizontal
                        ? pixelHeight * scaleZ
                        : pixelWidth * scaleX
                )
            );

            const thickness = segment.kind === "wall"
                ? this.wallThickness
                : detectedThickness;

            const dimensions = horizontal
                ? { x: length, z: thickness }
                : { x: thickness, z: length };

            if (segment.kind === "wall") {
                add(
                    "walls",
                    "wall",
                    {
                        x,
                        y: floorY + this.wallHeight * 0.5,
                        z
                    },
                    {
                        ...dimensions,
                        y: this.wallHeight
                    },
                    "wall"
                );

                counts.walls++;
                continue;
            }

            if (segment.kind === "door") {
                add(
                    "doors",
                    "door",
                    {
                        x,
                        y: floorY + this.doorHeight * 0.5,
                        z
                    },
                    {
                        ...dimensions,
                        y: this.doorHeight
                    },
                    "wood"
                );

                counts.doors++;
                continue;
            }

            if (segment.kind === "window") {
                const windowDepth = Math.max(
                    0.035,
                    detectedThickness * 0.25
                );

                add(
                    "glazing",
                    "window",
                    {
                        x,
                        y: floorY + this.windowSill +
                            this.windowHeight * 0.5,
                        z
                    },
                    horizontal
                        ? {
                            x: length,
                            y: this.windowHeight,
                            z: windowDepth
                        }
                        : {
                            x: windowDepth,
                            y: this.windowHeight,
                            z: length
                        },
                    "glass"
                );

                counts.windows++;
            }
        }

        const legacyTypes = new Set([
            "floor-plan-floor",
            "floor-plan-wall",
            "floor-plan-door",
            "floor-plan-window"
        ]);

        building.components = building.components.filter(
            component =>
                !legacyTypes.has(component.type) &&
                !component.type?.startsWith(prefix)
        );

        for (const component of generated) {
            building.addComponent(component);
        }

        return {
            ...counts,
            floor: floorIndex,
            totalSegments: segments.length,
            wallHeight: this.wallHeight,
            wallThickness: this.wallThickness,
            generatedComponents: generated.length
        };
    }
}

export { FloorPlanBuilder };
