========================================================================


/**
 * FloorPlanHeightControl
 *
 * Controls architectural wall height and thickness.
 * FloorPlanBuilder remains the authoritative owner.
 */
class FloorPlanHeightControl {
    constructor(container, builder, onCommit = null) {
        if (!container || !builder) {
            throw new Error(
                "Wall controls require a container and builder."
            );
        }

        this.container = container;
        this.builder = builder;
        this.onCommit = onCommit;

        this.element = document.createElement("section");

        Object.assign(this.element.style, {
            display: "grid",
            gap: "9px",
            margin: "12px 0",
            padding: "12px",
            background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.07)",
            borderRadius: "6px"
        });

        this.element.innerHTML = `
            <strong>ARCHITECTURAL WALLS</strong>

            <label>
                Wall height
                <output data-value="height"></output>
                <input data-setting="height"
                    type="range"
                    min="2.4"
                    max="1000"
                    step="0.1">
                <input data-number="height"
                    type="number"
                    min="2.4"
                    max="1000"
                    step="0.1">
            </label>

            <label>
                Wall thickness
                <output data-value="thickness"></output>
                <input data-setting="thickness"
                    type="range"
                    min="0.1"
                    max="1000"
                    step="0.1">
                <input data-number="thickness"
                    type="number"
                    min="0.1"
                    max="1000"
                    step="0.1">
            </label>

            <small>
                Thickness controls the horizontal depth
                of generated walls.
            </small>
        `;

        const buildButton =
            container.querySelector("#build-floor");

        if (buildButton) {
            buildButton.after(this.element);
        } else {
            container.appendChild(this.element);
        }

        this.controls = {
            height: {
                slider: this.element.querySelector(
                    '[data-setting="height"]'
                ),
                number: this.element.querySelector(
                    '[data-number="height"]'
                ),
                output: this.element.querySelector(
                    '[data-value="height"]'
                ),
                setter: value =>
                    builder.setWallHeight(value),
                initial: builder.wallHeight
            },
            thickness: {
                slider: this.element.querySelector(
                    '[data-setting="thickness"]'
                ),
                number: this.element.querySelector(
                    '[data-number="thickness"]'
                ),
                output: this.element.querySelector(
                    '[data-value="thickness"]'
                ),
                setter: value =>
                    builder.setWallThickness(value),
                initial: builder.wallThickness
            }
        };

        for (const [name, control] of Object.entries(
            this.controls
        )) {
            control.slider.value = control.initial;
            control.number.value = control.initial;

            control.slider.addEventListener(
                "input",
                () => {
                    control.number.value =
                        control.slider.value;

                    this.updateLabel(name);
                }
            );

            control.number.addEventListener(
                "change",
                () => {
                    control.slider.value =
                        control.number.value;

                    this.commit(name);
                }
            );

            control.slider.addEventListener(
                "change",
                () => this.commit(name)
            );

            this.updateLabel(name);
        }
    }

    updateLabel(name) {
        const control = this.controls[name];

        control.output.textContent =
            `${Number(control.slider.value).toFixed(1)} units`;
    }

    commit(name) {
        const control = this.controls[name];

        try {
            const value = Number(control.number.value);

            if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
                throw new Error("Invalid wall dimension.");
            }

            control.setter(value);

            control.slider.value = value;
            control.number.value = value;

            this.updateLabel(name);
            this.onCommit?.(name, value);

        } catch (error) {
            console.error(
                "[FloorPlanHeightControl]",
                error
            );

            control.slider.value =
                String(control.initial = (
                    name === "height"
                        ? this.builder.wallHeight
                        : this.builder.wallThickness
                ));

            control.number.value = control.slider.value;

            this.updateLabel(name);
        }
    }

    dispose() {
        this.element.remove();
    }
}

export { FloorPlanHeightControl };


========================================================================
FILE: src/editor/floor-plan-reference.js
========================================================================

/**
 * FloorPlanReference
 *
 * Manages an independently positioned architectural
 * reference image in the Somnium building editor.
 *
 * Authoritative coordinates remain in simulation space.
 * SpatialTransform handles rendering coordinates.
 *
 * Babylon CreateGround uses local X-Z dimensions.
 */
class FloorPlanReference {
    constructor(scene, spatialTransform) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        this.mesh = null;
        this.material = null;
        this.texture = null;
        this.objectURL = null;
        this.image = null;

        this.width = 20;
        this.depth = 20;

        this.opacity = 0.65;
        this.visible = true;

        this.position = {
            x: 0,
            y: 0,
            z: 0
        };

        this.altitudeOffset = 0;
        this.rotation = 0;
        this.loaded = false;
    }

    validatePosition(position) {
        if (
            !position ||
            !Number.isFinite(position.x) ||
            !Number.isFinite(position.y) ||
            !Number.isFinite(position.z)
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid floor-plan position."
            );
        }
    }

    async load(file) {
        if (
            !file ||
            typeof file.type !== "string" ||
            !file.type.startsWith("image/")
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Please select a valid image file."
            );
        }

        this.remove();

        try {
            this.objectURL = URL.createObjectURL(file);
            this.image = new Image();

            await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                this.image.onload = resolve;
                this.image.onerror = () => reject(
                    new Error(
                        "Could not load floor-plan image."
                    )
                );

                this.image.src = this.objectURL;
            });

            if (
                !this.image.naturalWidth ||
                !this.image.naturalHeight
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Floor-plan image has invalid dimensions."
                );
            }

            this.texture = new BABYLON.Texture(
                this.objectURL,
                this.scene,
                false,
                false,
                BABYLON.Texture.TRILINEAR_SAMPLINGMODE
            );

            await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                if (this.texture.isReady()) {
                    resolve();
                    return;
                }

                this.texture.onLoadObservable.addOnce(resolve);

                this.texture.onError = message => {
                    reject(
                        new Error(
                            message ||
                            "Floor-plan texture failed."
                        )
                    );
                };
            });

            this.material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
                "building-floor-plan-material",
                this.scene
            );

            this.material.diffuseTexture = this.texture;
            this.material.emissiveTexture = this.texture;
            this.material.disableLighting = true;
            this.material.backFaceCulling = false;
            this.material.alpha = this.opacity;
            this.material.transparencyMode =
                BABYLON.Material.MATERIAL_ALPHABLEND;

            this.mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateGround(
                "building-floor-plan",
                {
                    width: 1,
                    height: 1,
                    subdivisions: 1
                },
                this.scene
            );

            this.mesh.material = this.material;
            this.mesh.isPickable = false;
            this.mesh.receiveShadows = false;
            this.mesh.renderingGroupId = 1;

            this.mesh.metadata = {
                type: "floor-plan-reference",
                reference: true
            };

            this.loaded = true;

            this.setVisible(this.visible);
            this.updateTransform();

            return this;

        } catch (error) {
            this.remove();
            throw error;
        }
    }

    async readPixels() {
        if (!this.image || !this.loaded) {
            throw new Error(
                "Load a floor-plan image first."
            );
        }

        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");

        canvas.width = this.image.naturalWidth;
        canvas.height = this.image.naturalHeight;

        const context = canvas.getContext("2d", {
            willReadFrequently: true
        });

        if (!context) {
            throw new Error(
                "Image processing canvas unavailable."
            );
        }

        context.drawImage(this.image, 0, 0);

        return context.getImageData(
            0,
            0,
            canvas.width,
            canvas.height
        );
    }

    setPosition(position) {
        this.validatePosition(position);

        this.position = {
            x: position.x,
            y: position.y,
            z: position.z
        };

        this.updateTransform();
    }

    setAltitudeOffset(offset) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(offset)) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid altitude offset."
            );
        }

        this.altitudeOffset = offset;
        this.updateTransform();
    }

    setDimensions(width, depth) {
        if (
            !Number.isFinite(width) ||
            !Number.isFinite(depth) ||
            width <= 0 ||
            depth <= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid floor-plan dimensions."
            );
        }

        this.width = width;
        this.depth = depth;

        this.updateTransform();
    }

    setRotation(rotation) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(rotation)) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid floor-plan rotation."
            );
        }

        this.rotation = rotation;
        this.updateTransform();
    }

    updateTransform(
        position = this.position,
        dimensions = {},
        rotation = this.rotation
    ) {
        if (!this.mesh) return;

        if (!this.transform) {
            throw new Error(
                "SpatialTransform is unavailable."
            );
        }

        if (position) {
            this.validatePosition(position);

            this.position = {
                x: position.x,
                y: position.y,
                z: position.z
            };
        }

        const width = dimensions.width ?? this.width;
        const depth = dimensions.depth ?? this.depth;

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(width) ||
            !Number.isFinite(depth) ||
            width <= 0 ||
            depth <= 0 ||
            !Number.isFinite(rotation)
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid floor-plan transformation."
            );
        }

        this.width = width;
        this.depth = depth;
        this.rotation = rotation;

        const worldPosition = {
            x: this.position.x,
            y: this.position.y + this.altitudeOffset,
            z: this.position.z
        };

        const rendered = this.transform.worldToRender(
            worldPosition
        );

        const cos = Math.cos(rotation);
        const sin = Math.sin(rotation);

        const xAxis = this.transform.transformDirection(
            worldPosition,
            {
                x: cos,
                y: 0,
                z: -sin
            }
        );

        const zAxis = this.transform.transformDirection(
            worldPosition,
            {
                x: sin,
                y: 0,
                z: cos
            }
        );

        const xScale = Math.hypot(
            xAxis.x,
            xAxis.y,
            xAxis.z
        );

        const zScale = Math.hypot(
            zAxis.x,
            zAxis.y,
            zAxis.z
        );

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(xScale) ||
            !Number.isFinite(zScale) ||
            xScale <= 0 ||
            zScale <= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid projected floor-plan dimensions."
            );
        }

        this.mesh.position.set(
            rendered.x,
            rendered.y + 0.08,
            rendered.z
        );

        // CreateGround occupies local X-Z, not X-Y.
        this.mesh.scaling.set(
            width * xScale,
            1,
            depth * zScale
        );

        // Align the ground plane to its projected X axis.
        const projectedYaw = Math.atan2(
            -xAxis.z,
            xAxis.x
        );

        this.mesh.rotation.set(
            0,
            projectedYaw,
            0
        );

        this.mesh.isVisible = this.visible;
        this.mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
    }

    setOpacity(value) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid floor-plan opacity."
            );
        }

        this.opacity = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(1, value)
        );

        if (this.material) {
            this.material.alpha = this.opacity;
        }
    }

    setVisible(value) {
        this.visible = Boolean(value);

        if (this.mesh) {
            this.mesh.setEnabled(this.visible);
        }
    }

    toggleVisibility() {
        this.setVisible(!this.visible);
        return this.visible;
    }

    remove() {
        this.mesh?.dispose();
        this.material?.dispose();
        this.texture?.dispose();

        this.mesh = null;
        this.material = null;
        this.texture = null;

        if (this.objectURL) {
            URL.revokeObjectURL(this.objectURL);
            this.objectURL = null;
        }

        this.image = null;
        this.loaded = false;
    }

    dispose() {
        this.remove();
    }
}

export { FloorPlanReference };
export default FloorPlanReference;


========================================================================
FILE: src/environment/celestial-system.js

class CelestialSystem {
    constructor(scene, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;

        this.dayLength = options.dayLength ?? 900;
        this.timeScale = 1;
        this.elapsed = options.initialTime ?? 0;

        this.orbitDistance = options.orbitDistance ?? 45000;
        this.skyRadius = options.skyRadius ?? 90000;
        this.sunSize = options.sunSize ?? 1800;
        this.moonSize = options.moonSize ?? 1300;

        this.sunLight = options.sunLight ??
            new BABYLON.DirectionalLight(
                "celestialSunLight",
                new BABYLON.Vector3(0, -1, 0),
                scene
            );

        this.ambientLight = options.ambientLight ??
            new BABYLON.HemisphericLight(
                "celestialAmbientLight",
                new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 1, 0),
                scene
            );

        this.moonLight = new BABYLON.DirectionalLight(
            "celestialMoonLight",
            new BABYLON.Vector3(0, -1, 0),
            scene
        );

        this.sunLight.diffuse =
            new BABYLON.Color3(1.0, 0.94, 0.78);

        this.sunLight.specular =
            new BABYLON.Color3(1.0, 0.95, 0.85);

        this.moonLight.diffuse =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.48, 0.58, 0.85);

        this.moonLight.specular =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.35, 0.42, 0.65);

        this.sunDirection = new BABYLON.Vector3();
        this.moonDirection = new BABYLON.Vector3();

        this.daylight = 0;
        this.twilight = 0;
        this.starVisibility = 1;

	// Initialize rendering resources before the first update.
this.createSky();

// Initialize the celestial simulation and shader uniforms.
this.update(0);

console.log("[CelestialSystem] Initialized.");

        console.log("[CelestialSystem] Initialized.");
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // ATMOSPHERIC SKY
    // -----------------------------------------

    createSky() {
        BABYLON.Effect.ShadersStore[
            "somniumSkyVertexShader"
        ] = `
            precision highp float;

            attribute vec3 position;

            uniform mat4 world;
            uniform mat4 worldViewProjection;

            varying vec3 vDirection;

            void main(void) {
                vec4 worldDirection =
                    world * vec4(position, 0.0);

                vDirection = normalize(worldDirection.xyz);

                gl_Position =
                    worldViewProjection * vec4(position, 1.0);
            }
        `;

        BABYLON.Effect.ShadersStore[
            "somniumSkyFragmentShader"
        ] = `
            precision highp float;

            varying vec3 vDirection;

            uniform vec3 sunDirection;
            uniform vec3 moonDirection;

            uniform float daylight;
            uniform float twilight;
            uniform float starVisibility;
            uniform float time;

            float hash31(vec3 p) {
                p = fract(p * 0.1031);
                p += dot(p, p.yzx + 33.33);
                return fract(
                    (p.x + p.y) * p.z
                );
            }

            void main(void) {
                vec3 direction = normalize(vDirection);

                float height = clamp(
                    direction.y,
                    -1.0,
                    1.0
                );

                // Daytime atmospheric colors.

                vec3 zenithDay = vec3(
                    0.16, 0.42, 0.78
                );

                vec3 horizonDay = vec3(
                    0.65, 0.79, 0.92
                );

                // Nighttime atmospheric colors.

                vec3 zenithNight = vec3(
                    0.003, 0.006, 0.022
                );

                vec3 horizonNight = vec3(
                    0.018, 0.027, 0.065
                );

                float horizon = pow(
                    1.0 - max(height, 0.0),
                    2.0
                );

                vec3 daySky = mix(
                    zenithDay,
                    horizonDay,
                    horizon
                );

                vec3 nightSky = mix(
                    zenithNight,
                    horizonNight,
                    horizon
                );

                // Blend day and night.

                vec3 sky = mix(
                    nightSky,
                    daySky,
                    daylight
                );

                // Sunrise and sunset palette.

                vec3 sunsetZenith = vec3(
                    0.24, 0.10, 0.25
                );

                vec3 sunsetHorizon = vec3(
                    1.0, 0.24, 0.065
                );

                vec3 sunset = mix(
                    sunsetZenith,
                    sunsetHorizon,
                    pow(horizon, 0.65)
                );

                float sunsetBand = exp(
                    -pow(height * 5.0, 2.0)
                );

                sky = mix(
                    sky,
                    sunset,
                    twilight * sunsetBand
                );

                // Sun disc and atmospheric glow.

                float sunDot = dot(
                    direction,
                    normalize(sunDirection)
                );

                float sunDisc = smoothstep(
                    0.99965,
                    0.99985,
                    sunDot
                );

                float sunGlow = pow(
                    max(sunDot, 0.0),
                    24.0
                );

                vec3 sunColor = mix(
                    vec3(1.0, 0.22, 0.06),
                    vec3(1.0, 0.91, 0.68),
                    daylight
                );

                sky += sunColor *
                    sunGlow *
                    (0.12 + twilight * 0.35);

                sky = mix(
                    sky,
                    sunColor,
                    sunDisc
                );

                // Procedural star field.

                vec3 starCell = floor(
                    direction * 550.0
                );

                float starRandom = hash31(starCell);

                float star = step(
                    0.9985,
                    starRandom
                );

                float starBrightness = mix(
                    0.45,
                    1.0,
                    hash31(starCell + 17.3)
                );

                float twinkle = 0.85 +
                    0.15 * sin(
                        time * 2.0 +
                        starRandom * 40.0
                    );

                float stars = star *
                    starBrightness *
                    twinkle *
                    starVisibility;

                vec3 starColor = mix(
                    vec3(0.55, 0.68, 1.0),
                    vec3(1.0, 0.96, 0.86),
                    starRandom
                );

                sky += starColor * stars;

                // Subtle lunar glow.

float moonDot = dot(
    direction,
    normalize(moonDirection)
);

float moonDisc = smoothstep(
    0.99972,
    0.99988,
    moonDot
);

float moonGlow = pow(
    max(moonDot, 0.0),
    18.0
);

vec3 moonColor = vec3(0.78, 0.83, 0.96);

sky += vec3(0.12, 0.16, 0.30)
    * moonGlow
    * starVisibility
    * 0.3;

sky = mix(
    sky,
    moonColor,
    moonDisc * starVisibility
);
                gl_FragColor = vec4(sky, 1.0);
            }
        `;

        this.skyMesh =
            BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
                "somniumAtmosphere",
                {
                    diameter: this.skyRadius * 2,
                    segments: 32
                },
                this.scene
            );

        this.skyMaterial =
            new BABYLON.ShaderMaterial(
                "somniumSkyMaterial",
                this.scene,
                {
                    vertex: "somniumSky",
                    fragment: "somniumSky"
                },
                {
                    attributes: ["position"],
                    uniforms: [
                        "world",
                        "worldViewProjection",
                        "sunDirection",
                        "moonDirection",
                        "daylight",
                        "twilight",
                        "starVisibility",
                        "time"
                    ]
                }
            );

        this.skyMaterial.backFaceCulling = false;
        this.skyMaterial.disableDepthWrite = true;
        this.skyMaterial.fogEnabled = false;

        this.skyMaterial.setVector3(
            "sunDirection",
            this.sunDirection
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setVector3(
            "moonDirection",
            this.moonDirection
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setFloat("daylight", 0);
        this.skyMaterial.setFloat("twilight", 0);
        this.skyMaterial.setFloat("starVisibility", 1);
        this.skyMaterial.setFloat("time", 0);

        this.skyMesh.material = this.skyMaterial;
        this.skyMesh.isPickable = false;
        this.skyMesh.infiniteDistance = false;
        this.skyMesh.alwaysSelectAsActiveMesh = true;

        this.skyMesh.renderingGroupId = 0;
    }

	
    // -----------------------------------------
    // VISIBLE SUN
    // -----------------------------------------

    createSun() {
        this.sunMesh =
            BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
                "visibleSun",
                {
                    diameter: this.sunSize,
                    segments: 24
                },
                this.scene
            );

        this.sunMaterial =
            new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
                "visibleSunMaterial",
                this.scene
            );

        this.sunMaterial.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(1, 0.85, 0.45);

        this.sunMaterial.emissiveColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(1, 0.72, 0.28);

        this.sunMaterial.specularColor =
            BABYLON.Color3.Black();

        this.sunMaterial.disableLighting = true;

        this.sunMesh.material = this.sunMaterial;
        this.sunMesh.isPickable = false;
        this.sunMesh.renderingGroupId = 1;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // VISIBLE MOON
    // -----------------------------------------

    createMoon() {
        this.moonMesh =
            BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
                "visibleMoon",
                {
                    diameter: this.moonSize,
                    segments: 24
                },
                this.scene
            );

        this.moonMaterial =
            new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
                "visibleMoonMaterial",
                this.scene
            );

        this.moonMaterial.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.72, 0.78, 0.92);

        this.moonMaterial.emissiveColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.16, 0.19, 0.28);

        this.moonMaterial.specularColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.2, 0.2, 0.25);

        this.moonMaterial.disableLighting = true;

        this.moonMesh.material = this.moonMaterial;
        this.moonMesh.isPickable = false;
        this.moonMesh.renderingGroupId = 1;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TRANSITION FUNCTIONS
    // -----------------------------------------

    smoothstep(min, max, value) {
        const t = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            (value - min) / (max - min),
            0,
            1
        );

        return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DAY/NIGHT UPDATE
    // -----------------------------------------

    update(deltaSeconds) {
        if (
            !Number.isFinite(deltaSeconds) ||
            deltaSeconds < 0
        ) {
            return;
        }

        this.elapsed +=
            deltaSeconds * this.timeScale;

        const phase =
            (this.elapsed / this.dayLength) *
            Math.PI * 2 +
            Math.PI / 2;

        const horizontal = Math.cos(phase);
        const vertical = Math.sin(phase);

        this.sunDirection.set(
            horizontal,
            vertical,
            0
        ).normalize();

        this.moonDirection.copyFrom(
            this.sunDirection
        ).scaleInPlace(-1);

        // Directional light directions.

        this.sunLight.direction.copyFrom(
            this.sunDirection
        ).scaleInPlace(-1);

        this.moonLight.direction.copyFrom(
            this.moonDirection
        ).scaleInPlace(-1);

        // -------------------------------------
        // LIGHT INTENSITIES
        // -------------------------------------

        this.daylight = this.smoothstep(
            -0.12,
            0.28,
            vertical
        );

        const moonlight = this.smoothstep(
            -0.12,
            0.28,
            -vertical
        );

        this.sunLight.intensity =
            1.5 * this.daylight;

        this.moonLight.intensity =
            0.22 * moonlight;

        this.ambientLight.intensity =
            BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
                0.10,
                0.52,
                this.daylight
            );

        // -------------------------------------
        // ATMOSPHERIC TRANSITIONS
        // -------------------------------------

        this.twilight =
            1.0 - this.smoothstep(
                0.015,
                0.28,
                Math.abs(vertical)
            );

        this.starVisibility =
            1.0 - this.smoothstep(
                -0.18,
                0.12,
                vertical
            );

        // -------------------------------------
        // UPDATE SKY AND CELESTIAL POSITIONS
        // -------------------------------------

const camera = this.scene.activeCamera;

if (camera) {
    this.skyMesh.position.copyFrom(camera.position);

    // Keep the sky inside the camera far plane.
    const farPlane = camera.maxZ || 100000;

    const effectiveRadius = Math.min(
        this.skyRadius,
        farPlane * 0.45
    );

    const skyScale =
        effectiveRadius / this.skyRadius;

    this.skyMesh.scaling.setAll(skyScale);
}

        // -------------------------------------
        // UPDATE SKY SHADER
        // -------------------------------------

        this.skyMaterial.setVector3(
            "sunDirection",
            this.sunDirection
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setVector3(
            "moonDirection",
            this.moonDirection
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setFloat(
            "daylight",
            this.daylight
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setFloat(
            "twilight",
            this.twilight
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setFloat(
            "starVisibility",
            this.starVisibility
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setFloat(
            "time",
            this.elapsed
        );

        // Nighttime fallback clear color.

        this.scene.clearColor =
            new BABYLON.Color4(
                0.003,
                0.006,
                0.022,
                1
            );
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TIME CONTROLS
    // -----------------------------------------

    setTimeScale(scale) {
        this.timeScale =
            BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(scale, 0, 100);
    }

    setDayLength(seconds) {
        this.dayLength = Math.max(60, seconds);
    }

    setTimeOfDay(normalizedTime) {
        this.elapsed =
            BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                normalizedTime,
                0,
                1
            ) * this.dayLength;

        this.update(0);
    }

    getTimeOfDay() {
        return (this.elapsed / this.dayLength) % 1;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DEBUG INFORMATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    getStats() {
        return {
            timeOfDay: this.getTimeOfDay(),
            dayLength: this.dayLength,
            timeScale: this.timeScale,
            daylight: this.daylight,
            twilight: this.twilight,
            starVisibility: this.starVisibility,
            sunIntensity: this.sunLight.intensity,
            moonIntensity: this.moonLight.intensity,
            sunDirection: this.sunDirection.asArray(),
            moonDirection: this.moonDirection.asArray()
        };
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CLEANUP
    // -----------------------------------------
dispose() {
    if (this.skyMesh) {
        this.skyMesh.dispose();
    }

    if (this.skyMaterial) {
        this.skyMaterial.dispose();
    }

    // Retain compatibility if independent celestial
    // meshes are reintroduced in a future implementation.
    this.sunMesh?.dispose();
    this.moonMesh?.dispose();
    this.sunMaterial?.dispose();
    this.moonMaterial?.dispose();

    this.sunLight?.dispose();
    this.moonLight?.dispose();
    this.ambientLight?.dispose();
}
}

export { CelestialSystem };

class Environment {

    constructor(lightDirection) {

        this.sunDirection = lightDirection
            .negate()
            .normalize();

        // Environmental lighting configuration
        this.baseLight = 1.0;

        this.minimumLight = 0.12;

        // How strongly surrounding trees reduce light
        this.shadingStrength = 0.85;

        // Spatial grid for efficient canopy sampling
        this.canopyCellSize = 10;

        this.canopyGrid = new Map();
    }


    getCellKey(x, z) {

        const cx = Math.floor(x / this.canopyCellSize);
        const cz = Math.floor(z / this.canopyCellSize);

        return `${cx},${cz}`;
    }


    rebuildCanopyGrid(trees) {

        this.canopyGrid.clear();

        for (const tree of trees) {

            if (!tree.alive) continue;

            const age = tree.ageFactor;

            if (age < 0.1) continue;

            const height =
                tree.species.trunkLength *
                age *
                tree.genome.maxHeight;

            const radius =
                height *
                tree.species.canopy.width *
                tree.genome.canopyWidth;

            const key = this.getCellKey(
                tree.position.x,
                tree.position.z
            );

            if (!this.canopyGrid.has(key)) {
                this.canopyGrid.set(key, []);
            }

            this.canopyGrid.get(key).push({
                x: tree.position.x,
                z: tree.position.z,
                radius,
                height
            });

        }

    }

sampleLight(position) {

    const key = this.getCellKey(
        position.x,
        position.z
    );

    const cell = this.canopyGrid.get(key) || [];

    let shade = 0;

    for (const canopy of cell) {

        // Ignore canopies that are below the sample
        if (canopy.height <= position.y) continue;

        const dx = position.x - canopy.x;
        const dz = position.z - canopy.z;

        const distanceSquared =
            dx * dx + dz * dz;

        const radiusSquared =
            canopy.radius * canopy.radius;

        if (distanceSquared < radiusSquared) {

            const influence =
                1 - Math.sqrt(
                    distanceSquared / radiusSquared
                );

            shade += influence *
                this.shadingStrength *
                Math.min(1, canopy.height / 5);

        }

    }

    // Start with generous available light
    const intensity = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        1.0 - shade * 0.35,
        0.15,
        1.0
    );

    return {
        direction: this.sunDirection,
        intensity
    };

}

}
export { Environment }


========================================================================
FILE: src/environment/height-map-terrain.js
========================================================================


export class HeightmapTerrain {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.url = options.url ??
            "./assets/0_somnium_map_topographic.png";

        this.worldSize = options.worldSize ?? 1000000;

        this.minHeight = options.minHeight ?? -2000;
        this.maxHeight = options.maxHeight ?? 1000;

        this.heightChannel = options.heightChannel ?? "average";

        this.min = {
            x: -this.worldSize / 2,
            y: this.minHeight,
            z: -this.worldSize / 2
        };

        this.max = {
            x: this.worldSize / 2,
            y: this.maxHeight,
            z: this.worldSize / 2
        };

        this.width = 0;
        this.height = 0;
        this.pixels = null;
        this.ready = false;

        this.diagnostics = null;
    }

    async load() {
        const image = new Image();

        image.crossOrigin = "anonymous";

        await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            image.onload = resolve;

            image.onerror = () => reject(
                new Error(
                    `Unable to load heightmap: ${this.url}`
                )
            );

            image.src = this.url;
        });

        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");

        canvas.width = image.naturalWidth;
        canvas.height = image.naturalHeight;

        const context = canvas.getContext(
            "2d",
            { willReadFrequently: true }
        );

        if (!context) {
            throw new Error(
                "Unable to initialize heightmap canvas."
            );
        }

        context.drawImage(image, 0, 0);

        this.width = canvas.width;
        this.height = canvas.height;

        this.pixels = context.getImageData(
            0,
            0,
            this.width,
            this.height
        ).data;

        this.ready = true;

        this.diagnostics = this.getDiagnostics(32);

        console.log(
            "[HeightmapTerrain] Loaded:",
            this.getDiagnostics()
        );

        return this;
    }

    getNormalizedCoordinates(x, z) {
        return {
            u: (x - this.min.x) / this.worldSize,
            v: (z - this.min.z) / this.worldSize
        };
    }

    getPixelValue(x, z) {
        x = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(this.width - 1, x)
        );

        z = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(this.height - 1, z)
        );

        const index = (
            Math.floor(z) * this.width +
            Math.floor(x)
        ) * 4;

        const r = this.pixels[index];
        const g = this.pixels[index + 1];
        const b = this.pixels[index + 2];

        switch (this.heightChannel) {
            case "red":
                return r / 255;

            case "green":
                return g / 255;

            case "blue":
                return b / 255;

            case "luminance":
                return (
                    0.2126 * r +
                    0.7152 * g +
                    0.0722 * b
                ) / 255;

            case "average":
            default:
                return (r + g + b) / 765;
        }
    }

    getHeightPixel(x, z) {
        return this.getPixelValue(x, z);
    }

    sampleHeight(x, z) {
        if (!this.ready) {
            throw new Error("Heightmap not loaded.");
        }

        if (
            x < this.min.x ||
            x > this.max.x ||
            z < this.min.z ||
            z > this.max.z
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const { u, v } =
            this.getNormalizedCoordinates(x, z);

        const px = u * (this.width - 1);
        const pz = v * (this.height - 1);

        const x0 = Math.floor(px);
        const z0 = Math.floor(pz);

        const x1 = Math.min(
            x0 + 1,
            this.width - 1
        );

        const z1 = Math.min(
            z0 + 1,
            this.height - 1
        );

        const tx = px - x0;
        const tz = pz - z0;

        const a = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.getPixelValue(x0, z0),
            this.getPixelValue(x1, z0),
            tx
        );

        const b = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.getPixelValue(x0, z1),
            this.getPixelValue(x1, z1),
            tx
        );

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.minHeight,
            this.maxHeight,
            BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(a, b, tz)
        );
    }

    sampleAt(x, z) {
        const y = this.sampleHeight(x, z);

        if (y === null) return null;

        return new BABYLON.Vector3(x, y, z);
    }

    sample() {
        return this.sampleAt(
            BABYLON.Scalar.RandomRange(
                this.min.x,
                this.max.x
            ),
            BABYLON.Scalar.RandomRange(
                this.min.z,
                this.max.z
            )
        );
    }

    sampleNear(center, radius) {
        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;

        const distance =
            radius * Math.sqrt(Math.random());

        return this.sampleAt(
            center.x + Math.cos(angle) * distance,
            center.z + Math.sin(angle) * distance
        );
    }

    sampleSlopeAt(x, z, spacing = 4) {
        const left = this.sampleHeight(x - spacing, z);
        const right = this.sampleHeight(x + spacing, z);

        const back = this.sampleHeight(x, z - spacing);
        const front = this.sampleHeight(x, z + spacing);

        if (
            [left, right, back, front].some(
                value => value === null
            )
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const dx = (right - left) / (2 * spacing);
        const dz = (front - back) / (2 * spacing);

        const magnitude = Math.hypot(dx, dz);

        const angle = Math.atan(magnitude);

        return {
            gradientX: dx,
            gradientZ: dz,
            magnitude,
            angle,
            degrees: angle * 180 / Math.PI,

            normal: new BABYLON.Vector3(
                -dx, 1, -dz
            ).normalize()
        };
    }

    sampleProfileAt(x, z, spacing = 4) {
        const position = this.sampleAt(x, z);

        if (!position) return null;

        const slope = this.sampleSlopeAt(
            x, z, spacing
        );

        if (!slope) return null;

        return {
            position,

            elevation: BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                (position.y - this.minHeight) /
                (this.maxHeight - this.minHeight),
                0, 1
            ),

            slope: slope.magnitude,
            slopeDegrees: slope.degrees,
            normal: slope.normal
        };
    }

    getDiagnostics(gridSize = 32) {
        if (!this.ready) {
            return {
                ready: false
            };
        }

        let minimum = Infinity;
        let maximum = -Infinity;
        let sum = 0;
        let count = 0;

        const samples = [];

        for (let z = 0; z <= gridSize; z++) {
            for (let x = 0; x <= gridSize; x++) {
                const wx = this.min.x +
                    (x / gridSize) * this.worldSize;

                const wz = this.min.z +
                    (z / gridSize) * this.worldSize;

                const height = this.sampleHeight(wx, wz);

                minimum = Math.min(minimum, height);
                maximum = Math.max(maximum, height);

                sum += height;
                count++;

                if (
                    x === 0 ||
                    x === Math.floor(gridSize / 2) ||
                    x === gridSize
                ) {
                    if (
                        z === 0 ||
                        z === Math.floor(gridSize / 2) ||
                        z === gridSize
                    ) {
                        samples.push({
                            x: wx,
                            z: wz,
                            height
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return {
            ready: true,

            imageWidth: this.width,
            imageHeight: this.height,

            worldSize: this.worldSize,

            heightChannel: this.heightChannel,

            configuredHeightRange: {
                min: this.minHeight,
                max: this.maxHeight
            },

            sampledHeightRange: {
                min: minimum,
                max: maximum,
                variation: maximum - minimum
            },

            averageHeight: sum / count,

            sampleCount: count,
            samples
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        this.pixels = null;
        this.ready = false;
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: src/environment/heightmap-terrain.js
========================================================================


export class HeightmapTerrain {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.url = options.url ??
            "./assets/0_somnium_map_topographic.png";

        this.worldSize = options.worldSize ?? 1000000;

        this.minHeight = options.minHeight ?? -2000;
        this.maxHeight = options.maxHeight ?? 1000;

        this.heightChannel = options.heightChannel ?? "average";

        this.min = {
            x: -this.worldSize / 2,
            y: this.minHeight,
            z: -this.worldSize / 2
        };

        this.max = {
            x: this.worldSize / 2,
            y: this.maxHeight,
            z: this.worldSize / 2
        };

        this.width = 0;
        this.height = 0;
        this.pixels = null;

        this.ready = false;
        this.diagnostics = null;
    }

    async load() {
        const image = new Image();

        image.crossOrigin = "anonymous";

        await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            image.onload = resolve;

            image.onerror = () => {
                reject(
                    new Error(
                        `Unable to load heightmap: ${this.url}`
                    )
                );
            };

            image.src = this.url;
        });

        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");

        canvas.width = image.naturalWidth;
        canvas.height = image.naturalHeight;

        const context = canvas.getContext("2d", {
            willReadFrequently: true
        });

        if (!context) {
            throw new Error(
                "Unable to initialize heightmap canvas."
            );
        }

        context.drawImage(image, 0, 0);

        this.width = canvas.width;
        this.height = canvas.height;

        this.pixels = context.getImageData(
            0,
            0,
            this.width,
            this.height
        ).data;

        this.ready = true;

        this.diagnostics = this.getDiagnostics(32);

        console.log(
            "[HeightmapTerrain] Loaded:",
            this.diagnostics
        );

        return this;
    }

    getNormalizedCoordinates(x, z) {
        return {
            u: (x - this.min.x) / this.worldSize,
            v: (z - this.min.z) / this.worldSize
        };
    }

    getPixelValue(x, z) {
        if (!this.pixels) {
            throw new Error("Heightmap pixels are unavailable.");
        }

        x = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(this.width - 1, Math.floor(x))
        );

        z = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(this.height - 1, Math.floor(z))
        );

        const index = (z * this.width + x) * 4;

        const r = this.pixels[index];
        const g = this.pixels[index + 1];
        const b = this.pixels[index + 2];

        switch (this.heightChannel) {
            case "red":
                return r / 255;

            case "green":
                return g / 255;

            case "blue":
                return b / 255;

            case "luminance":
                return (
                    0.2126 * r +
                    0.7152 * g +
                    0.0722 * b
                ) / 255;

            case "average":
            default:
                return (r + g + b) / 765;
        }
    }

    getHeightPixel(x, z) {
        return this.getPixelValue(x, z);
    }

    sampleHeight(x, z) {
        if (!this.ready) {
            throw new Error("Heightmap not loaded.");
        }

        if (
            x < this.min.x ||
            x > this.max.x ||
            z < this.min.z ||
            z > this.max.z
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const { u, v } =
            this.getNormalizedCoordinates(x, z);

        const px = u * (this.width - 1);
        const pz = v * (this.height - 1);

        const x0 = Math.floor(px);
        const z0 = Math.floor(pz);

        const x1 = Math.min(
            x0 + 1,
            this.width - 1
        );

        const z1 = Math.min(
            z0 + 1,
            this.height - 1
        );

        const tx = px - x0;
        const tz = pz - z0;

        const a = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.getPixelValue(x0, z0),
            this.getPixelValue(x1, z0),
            tx
        );

        const b = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.getPixelValue(x0, z1),
            this.getPixelValue(x1, z1),
            tx
        );

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.minHeight,
            this.maxHeight,
            BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(a, b, tz)
        );
    }

    sampleAt(x, z) {
        const y = this.sampleHeight(x, z);

        if (y === null) return null;

        return new BABYLON.Vector3(x, y, z);
    }

    sample() {
        return this.sampleAt(
            BABYLON.Scalar.RandomRange(
                this.min.x,
                this.max.x
            ),
            BABYLON.Scalar.RandomRange(
                this.min.z,
                this.max.z
            )
        );
    }

    sampleNear(center, radius) {
        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;

        const distance =
            radius * Math.sqrt(Math.random());

        return this.sampleAt(
            center.x + Math.cos(angle) * distance,
            center.z + Math.sin(angle) * distance
        );
    }

    sampleSlopeAt(x, z, spacing = 4) {
        const left = this.sampleHeight(x - spacing, z);
        const right = this.sampleHeight(x + spacing, z);

        const back = this.sampleHeight(x, z - spacing);
        const front = this.sampleHeight(x, z + spacing);

        if (
            [left, right, back, front].some(
                value => value === null
            )
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const dx = (right - left) / (2 * spacing);
        const dz = (front - back) / (2 * spacing);

        const magnitude = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
        const angle = Math.atan(magnitude);

        return {
            gradientX: dx,
            gradientZ: dz,
            magnitude,
            angle,
            degrees: angle * 180 / Math.PI,

            normal: new BABYLON.Vector3(
                -dx,
                1,
                -dz
            ).normalize()
        };
    }

    sampleProfileAt(x, z, spacing = 4) {
        const position = this.sampleAt(x, z);

        if (!position) return null;

        const slope = this.sampleSlopeAt(
            x,
            z,
            spacing
        );

        if (!slope) return null;

        return {
            position,

            elevation: BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                (position.y - this.minHeight) /
                (this.maxHeight - this.minHeight),
                0,
                1
            ),

            slope: slope.magnitude,
            slopeDegrees: slope.degrees,
            normal: slope.normal
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // HEIGHTMAP DIAGNOSTICS
    // ------------------------------------------------

    getDiagnostics(gridSize = 32) {
        if (!this.ready || !this.pixels) {
            return {
                ready: false,
                message: "Heightmap has not finished loading."
            };
        }

        gridSize = Math.max(
            1,
            Math.floor(gridSize)
        );

        let minimum = Infinity;
        let maximum = -Infinity;
        let sum = 0;
        let count = 0;

        const samples = [];

        for (let z = 0; z <= gridSize; z++) {
            for (let x = 0; x <= gridSize; x++) {
                const wx = this.min.x +
                    (x / gridSize) * this.worldSize;

                const wz = this.min.z +
                    (z / gridSize) * this.worldSize;

                const height = this.sampleHeight(
                    wx,
                    wz
                );

                minimum = Math.min(minimum, height);
                maximum = Math.max(maximum, height);

                sum += height;
                count++;

                if (
                    (
                        x === 0 ||
                        x === Math.floor(gridSize / 2) ||
                        x === gridSize
                    ) &&
                    (
                        z === 0 ||
                        z === Math.floor(gridSize / 2) ||
                        z === gridSize
                    )
                ) {
                    samples.push({
                        x: wx,
                        z: wz,
                        height
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        return {
            ready: true,

            image: {
                width: this.width,
                height: this.height
            },

            worldSize: this.worldSize,

            heightChannel: this.heightChannel,

            configuredHeightRange: {
                min: this.minHeight,
                max: this.maxHeight
            },

            sampledHeightRange: {
                min: minimum,
                max: maximum,
                variation: maximum - minimum
            },

            averageHeight: sum / count,

            sampleCount: count,
            samples
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        this.pixels = null;
        this.ready = false;
        this.diagnostics = null;
    }

	sampleMapValue(x, z) {
    if (!this.ready || !this.pixels) {
        return null;
    }

    if (
        x < this.min.x ||
        x > this.max.x ||
        z < this.min.z ||
        z > this.max.z
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    const { u, v } = this.getNormalizedCoordinates(x, z);

    const px = u * (this.width - 1);
    const pz = v * (this.height - 1);

    const x0 = Math.floor(px);
    const z0 = Math.floor(pz);

    const x1 = Math.min(x0 + 1, this.width - 1);
    const z1 = Math.min(z0 + 1, this.height - 1);

    const tx = px - x0;
    const tz = pz - z0;

    const a = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
        this.getPixelValue(x0, z0),
        this.getPixelValue(x1, z0),
        tx
    );

    const b = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
        this.getPixelValue(x0, z1),
        this.getPixelValue(x1, z1),
        tx
    );

    return BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(a, b, tz);
}

}

FILE: src/environment/moisture-field.js
========================================================================


class MoistureField {
    constructor(terrain, options = {}) {
        this.terrain = terrain;

        this.seaLevel = options.seaLevel ?? -100;

        this.baseMoisture = options.baseMoisture ?? 0.08;
        this.oceanMoisture = options.oceanMoisture ?? 0.82;

        this.coastalRadius = options.coastalRadius ?? 24000;
        this.atmosphericScale = options.atmosphericScale ?? 7000;

        this.cloudContribution = options.cloudContribution ?? 0.25;
    }

    clamp(value) {
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value));
    }

    getSurfaceHeight(x, z) {
        const height = this.terrain.sampleHeight(x, z);

        if (height === null || !Number.isFinite(height)) {
            return null;
        }

        return height;
    }

    getOceanInfluence(x, z) {
        const centerHeight = this.getSurfaceHeight(x, z);

        if (centerHeight === null) {
            return 0;
        }

        // Directly over the ocean.
        if (centerHeight <= this.seaLevel) {
            return 1;
        }

        // Sample the surrounding terrain to estimate
        // proximity to ocean water.
        const directions = [
            [1, 0],
            [-1, 0],
            [0, 1],
            [0, -1],
            [0.7071, 0.7071],
            [-0.7071, 0.7071],
            [0.7071, -0.7071],
            [-0.7071, -0.7071]
        ];

        let influence = 0;

        const distances = [
            this.coastalRadius * 0.4,
            this.coastalRadius
        ];

        for (let distanceIndex = 0;
             distanceIndex < distances.length;
             distanceIndex++) {

            const distance = distances[distanceIndex];

            const weight = distanceIndex === 0 ? 0.65 : 0.35;

            let oceanSamples = 0;

            for (const direction of directions) {
                const height = this.getSurfaceHeight(
                    x + direction[0] * distance,
                    z + direction[1] * distance
                );

                if (height !== null && height <= this.seaLevel) {
                    oceanSamples++;
                }
            }

            influence += (
                oceanSamples / directions.length
            ) * weight;
        }

        return this.clamp(influence);
    }

    sample(position, cloudMoisture = 0) {
        if (!position) {
            return this.baseMoisture;
        }

        const surfaceHeight = this.getSurfaceHeight(
            position.x,
            position.z
        );

        if (surfaceHeight === null) {
            return this.baseMoisture;
        }

        const referenceHeight = Math.max(
            surfaceHeight,
            this.seaLevel
        );

        const altitude = Math.max(
            0,
            position.y - referenceHeight
        );

        // Moisture availability decreases with altitude.
        const altitudeFactor = Math.exp(
            -altitude / this.atmosphericScale
        );

        const oceanInfluence = this.getOceanInfluence(
            position.x,
            position.z
        );

        const oceanContribution =
            oceanInfluence *
            this.oceanMoisture *
            altitudeFactor;

        const cloudContribution =
            this.clamp(cloudMoisture) *
            this.cloudContribution;

        return this.clamp(
            this.baseMoisture +
            oceanContribution +
            cloudContribution
        );
    }

    sampleSurface(x, z) {
        const position = {
            x,
            y: this.seaLevel,
            z
        };

        return this.sample(position);
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            seaLevel: this.seaLevel,
            baseMoisture: this.baseMoisture,
            oceanMoisture: this.oceanMoisture,
            coastalRadius: this.coastalRadius,
            atmosphericScale: this.atmosphericScale
        };
    }
}

export { MoistureField };

class SpatialGrid {

    constructor(cellSize = 2.5) {

        this.cellSize = cellSize;
        this.cells = new Map();

    }

    getKey(x, z) {

        const cx = Math.floor(x / this.cellSize);
        const cz = Math.floor(z / this.cellSize);

        return `${cx},${cz}`;
    }

    insert(tree) {

        const key = this.getKey(
            tree.position.x,
            tree.position.z
        );

        if (!this.cells.has(key)) {
            this.cells.set(key, []);
        }

        this.cells.get(key).push(tree);

    }

    isAvailable(position, minSpacing) {

        const cx = Math.floor(
            position.x / this.cellSize
        );

        const cz = Math.floor(
            position.z / this.cellSize
        );

        const searchRadius = Math.ceil(
            minSpacing / this.cellSize
        );

        const minSpacingSquared =
            minSpacing * minSpacing;

        for (
            let x = cx - searchRadius;
            x <= cx + searchRadius;
            x++
        ) {

            for (
                let z = cz - searchRadius;
                z <= cz + searchRadius;
                z++
            ) {

                const key = `${x},${z}`;
                const cell = this.cells.get(key);

                if (!cell) continue;

                for (const tree of cell) {

                    if (!tree.alive) continue;

                    const dx =
                        position.x - tree.position.x;

                    const dz =
                        position.z - tree.position.z;

                    if (
                        dx * dx + dz * dz <
                        minSpacingSquared
                    ) {
                        return false;
                    }

                }

            }

        }

        return true;

    }

}
export { SpatialGrid }
