        this.cellSize = configuration.cellSize;

        this.createGrid();
        this.syncAllCharacters();
        this.refreshMovementPreview();

        this.recenterCameraPreservingOrbit(previous);
    }

    restoreGridConfigurationState(previous) {
        this.gridRows = previous.rows;
        this.gridColumns = previous.columns;
        this.cellSize = previous.cellSize;

        this.createGrid();
        this.syncAllCharacters();
        this.refreshMovementPreview();

        this.recenterCameraPreservingOrbit(previous);
    }

    formatGridConfigurationMessage({ rows, columns, cellSize }) {
        return (
            `Grid updated: ${rows} rows × ${columns} columns; ` +
            `${cellSize} world units per cell.`
        );
    }

    reportGridConfigurationError(error) {
        console.warn("[DnD] Grid configuration rejected.", error);
        this.showMessage(error.message);
    }

    setGridDimensions(rows, columns, cellSize) {
        return this.setGridConfiguration(rows, columns, cellSize);
    }

    validateTerrainCoverage(rows, columns, cellSize, origin) {
        for (let row = 0; row <= rows; row++) {
            for (let col = 0; col <= columns; col++) {
                const x = origin.x + col * cellSize;
                const z = origin.z + row * cellSize;

                if (!this.terrain.sampleAt(x, z)) return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    syncAllCharacters() {
        for (const character of this.characters.values()) {
            this.syncCharacter(character);
        }
    }

    restoreCameraOrbit(previous) {
        if (!this.camera || !previous) return;

        if (Number.isFinite(previous.radius)) {
            this.camera.radius = previous.radius;
        }
        if (Number.isFinite(previous.alpha)) {
            this.camera.alpha = previous.alpha;
        }
        if (Number.isFinite(previous.beta)) {
            this.camera.beta = previous.beta;
        }
    }

    isValidCellSize(cellSize) {
        return (
            Number.isFinite(cellSize) &&
            cellSize >= MIN_CELL_SIZE &&
            cellSize <= MAX_CELL_SIZE
        );
    }


    get gridSize() {
        // Legacy read-only alias; rectangular grids deliberately return null.
        return this.gridRows === this.gridColumns
            ? this.gridRows
            : null;
    }

    isValidGridCoordinate(row, col) {
        return (
            Number.isInteger(row) &&
            Number.isInteger(col) &&
            row >= 0 &&
            col >= 0 &&
            row < this.gridRows &&
            col < this.gridColumns
        );
    }

    validateGridDimensions(rows, columns) {
        return (
            Number.isInteger(rows) &&
            Number.isInteger(columns) &&
            rows >= MIN_GRID_DIMENSION &&
            columns >= MIN_GRID_DIMENSION &&
            rows <= this.maxGridRows &&
            columns <= this.maxGridColumns
        );
    }

}

export { DnDMode };


========================================================================
FILE: src/game/dnd-tactical-ui.js
========================================================================


const ACTION_OPTIONS = [
    ["attack", "Attack"],
    ["cast-spell", "Cast a Spell"],
    ["dash", "Dash"],
    ["disengage", "Disengage"],
    ["dodge", "Dodge"],
    ["help", "Help"],
    ["hide", "Hide"],
    ["ready", "Ready"],
    ["search", "Search"],
    ["use-object", "Use an Object"],
    ["grapple", "Grapple"],
    ["shove", "Shove"],
    ["improvise", "Improvise"]
];

const BONUS_OPTIONS = [
    ["bonus-spell", "Cast a Bonus Action Spell"],
    ["two-weapon-fighting", "Two-Weapon Fighting"],
    ["class-feature", "Use a Class Feature"],
    ["bonus-ability", "Use an Ability"]
];

const DEFAULT_COLORS = {
    movement: "#25d9a0",
    dash: "#e9a33c"
};

const ALTITUDE_MIN = 0;
const ALTITUDE_MAX = 1000;

function findOptionByValue(options, value) {
    for (const option of options) {
        if (option[0] === value) return option;
    }
    return null;
}

class DnDTacticalUI {

    constructor(mode) {
        this.mode = mode;
        this.visible = false;
        this.activeTab = "battle";
        this.colors = {
            ...DEFAULT_COLORS,
            ...this.loadColors()
        };

        this.createInterface();
    }
    loadColors() {
        try {
            return JSON.parse(
                localStorage.getItem("somnium-dnd-colors") || "{}"
            );
        } catch {
            return {};
        }
    }

    saveColors() {
        localStorage.setItem(
            "somnium-dnd-colors",
            JSON.stringify(this.colors)
        );

        this.mode.setMovementColors(this.colors);
    }

    createInterface() {
        this.createFloatingButtons();
        this.createPanelShell();
        this.createPanelHeader();
        this.createTabNavigation();
        this.buildTabContents();
        this.mountPanel();
        this.bindKeyboard();
        this.refresh();
    }

    createFloatingButtons() {
        this.createMenuToggleButton();
        this.createFocusButton();
    }

    createMenuToggleButton() {
        this.toggleButton = this.createButton(
            "☰ D&D MENU",
            this.handleToggleMenu.bind(this)
        );
        Object.assign(this.toggleButton.style, {
            position: "fixed",
            top: "15px",
            left: "15px",
            zIndex: "2100",
            width: "125px",
            background: "#193b55"
        });
        document.body.appendChild(this.toggleButton);
    }

    createFocusButton() {
        this.focusButton = this.createButton(
            "◎ FOCUS ACTIVE",
            this.handleFocusActiveCharacter.bind(this)
        );
        Object.assign(this.focusButton.style, {
            position: "fixed",
            top: "15px",
            right: "15px",
            zIndex: "10003",
            width: "auto",
            minWidth: "150px",
            margin: "0",
            background: "#72551b",
            boxShadow: "0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.45)"
        });
        this.focusButton.title = "Center and zoom the camera on the active character";
        this.focusButton.setAttribute("aria-label", "Focus camera on active character");
        document.body.appendChild(this.focusButton);
    }

    createPanelShell() {
        this.panel = document.createElement("div");
        Object.assign(this.panel.style, {
            position: "fixed",
            top: "58px",
            left: "15px",
            zIndex: "10002",
            width: "340px",
            maxWidth: "calc(100vw - 30px)",
            maxHeight: "calc(100vh - 75px)",
            overflowY: "auto",
            boxSizing: "border-box",
            padding: "13px",
            color: "#eaf4fa",
            background: "rgba(8,20,34,0.97)",
            border: "1px solid #54758d",
            borderRadius: "9px",
            fontFamily: "Arial, sans-serif",
            fontSize: "13px",
            display: "none",
            visibility: "visible",
            pointerEvents: "auto"
        });
    }

    createPanelHeader() {
        const heading = this.element("h2", "TACTICAL MODE");
        heading.style.color = "#9dd8ed";
        this.panel.appendChild(heading);
        this.status = this.appendPanelText("div");
        this.battleStatus = this.appendPanelText("p");
        this.message = this.appendPanelText("p");
        this.message.style.color = "#9dd8ed";
    }

    appendPanelText(tag) {
        const element = this.element(tag);
        this.panel.appendChild(element);
        return element;
    }

    createTabNavigation() {
        this.tabBar = document.createElement("div");
        Object.assign(this.tabBar.style, {
            display: "grid",
            gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(4, 1fr)",
            gap: "4px",
            margin: "12px 0"
        });
        this.panel.appendChild(this.tabBar);
        this.tabContents = {};
        this.appendTab("battle", "Battle");
        this.appendTab("characters", "Characters");
        this.appendTab("battlefield", "Grid");
        this.appendTab("settings", "Settings");
    }

    appendTab(id, label) {
        const button = this.createButton(label, this.handleTabButtonClick.bind(this));
        button.dataset.tabId = id;
        button.style.padding = "8px 3px";
        button.style.fontSize = "11px";
        this.tabBar.appendChild(button);
        const content = document.createElement("div");
        this.tabContents[id] = content;
        this.panel.appendChild(content);
    }

    buildTabContents() {
        this.buildBattleTab();
        this.buildCharactersTab();
        this.buildBattlefieldTab();
        this.buildSettingsTab();
    }

    mountPanel() {
        document.body.appendChild(this.panel);
        this.setVisible(false);
        this.selectTab("battle");
    }

    bindKeyboard() {
        this.onKeyDown = this.handleKeyDown.bind(this);
        window.addEventListener("keydown", this.onKeyDown);
    }

    handleToggleMenu() {
        this.toggle();
    }

    handleTabButtonClick(event) {
        this.selectTab(event.currentTarget.dataset.tabId);
    }

    handleKeyDown(event) {
        if (
            event.target instanceof HTMLElement &&
            ["INPUT", "TEXTAREA", "SELECT"].includes(event.target.tagName)
        ) return;

        if (event.code === "Escape") this.setVisible(false);
        if (event.code === "KeyM") this.toggle();
    }

    element(tag, content = "") {
        const element = document.createElement(tag);
        element.textContent = content;
        return element;
    }

    createButton(label, callback) {
        const button = this.element("button", label);

        Object.assign(button.style, {
            display: "block",
            width: "100%",
            margin: "5px 0",
            padding: "9px",
            color: "white",
            background: "#193b55",
            border: "1px solid #54758d",
            borderRadius: "4px",
            cursor: "pointer"
        });

        button.addEventListener("click", callback);

        return button;
    }

    addSection(parent, label) {
        const heading = this.element("h3", label);

        Object.assign(heading.style, {
            marginTop: "15px",
            paddingBottom: "5px",
            color: "#9dd8ed",
            borderBottom: "1px solid #54758d"
        });

        parent.appendChild(heading);
    }

    selectTab(id) {
        this.activeTab = id;

        for (const [key, content] of
            Object.entries(this.tabContents)) {
            content.style.display =
                key === id ? "block" : "none";
        }

        for (const button of this.tabBar.children) {
            const selected =
                button.textContent.toLowerCase() ===
                (id === "battlefield" ? "grid" : id);

            button.style.background = selected
                ? "#72551b"
                : "#193b55";
        }
    }

    createInput(parent, label, type = "text") {
        const wrapper = document.createElement("div");

        wrapper.style.margin = "8px 0";

        const caption = this.element("label", label);
        caption.style.display = "block";
        caption.style.marginBottom = "4px";

        const input = document.createElement("input");
        input.type = type;

        Object.assign(input.style, {
            width: "100%",
            boxSizing: "border-box",
            padding: "8px",
            color: "white",
            background: "#193b55",
            border: "1px solid #54758d",
            borderRadius: "4px"
        });

        wrapper.appendChild(caption);
        wrapper.appendChild(input);
        parent.appendChild(wrapper);

        return input;
    }

    createSelect(parent, label, options) {
        const wrapper = document.createElement("div");

        wrapper.style.margin = "8px 0";

        const caption = this.element("label", label);
        caption.style.display = "block";
        caption.style.marginBottom = "4px";

        const select = document.createElement("select");

        Object.assign(select.style, {
            width: "100%",
            padding: "9px",
            color: "white",
            background: "#193b55",
            border: "1px solid #54758d",
            borderRadius: "4px"
        });

        for (const [value, labelText] of options) {
            const option = this.element("option", labelText);
            option.value = value;
            select.appendChild(option);
        }

        wrapper.appendChild(caption);
        wrapper.appendChild(select);
        parent.appendChild(wrapper);

        return select;
    }

    createTextarea(parent, placeholder, rows = 3) {
        const textarea = document.createElement("textarea");

        textarea.placeholder = placeholder;
        textarea.rows = rows;

        Object.assign(textarea.style, {
            width: "100%",
            boxSizing: "border-box",
            padding: "9px",
            margin: "7px 0",
            color: "white",
            background: "#193b55",
            border: "1px solid #54758d",
            borderRadius: "4px",
            fontFamily: "monospace",
            resize: "vertical"
        });

        parent.appendChild(textarea);

        return textarea;
    }

    buildBattlefieldTab() {
        const parent = this.tabContents.battlefield;
        this.buildGridOriginControls(parent);
        this.buildGridDimensionControls(parent);
        this.buildCameraControls(parent);
    }

    buildGridOriginControls(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "BATTLEFIELD GRID");
        this.gridX = this.createInput(parent, "World X origin", "number");
        this.gridZ = this.createInput(parent, "World Z origin", "number");
        this.gridX.value = String(this.mode.gridOrigin.x);
        this.gridZ.value = String(this.mode.gridOrigin.z);
        parent.appendChild(this.createButton(
            "APPLY GRID ORIGIN",
            this.handleApplyGridOrigin.bind(this)
        ));
    }

    buildGridDimensionControls(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "GRID DIMENSIONS");
        this.gridRowsInput = this.createGridDimensionInput(parent, "Rows (2–300)", this.mode.gridRows);
        this.gridColumnsInput = this.createGridDimensionInput(parent, "Columns (2–300)", this.mode.gridColumns);
        this.cellSizeInput = this.createInput(parent, "Cell size (world units; square cells)", "number");
        this.configureNumericInput(this.cellSizeInput, 0.5, 1000, 0.5, this.mode.cellSize);
        parent.appendChild(this.createButton(
            "APPLY GRID SETTINGS",
            this.handleApplyGridConfiguration.bind(this)
        ));
        this.gridInfo = this.element("div");
        Object.assign(this.gridInfo.style, {
            marginTop: "10px",
            padding: "9px",
            background: "rgba(40,80,110,0.3)",
            borderRadius: "5px",
            lineHeight: "1.7",
            whiteSpace: "pre-line"
        });
        parent.appendChild(this.gridInfo);
    }

    createGridDimensionInput(parent, label, value) {
        const input = this.createInput(parent, label, "number");
        this.configureNumericInput(input, 2, 300, 1, value);
        return input;
    }

    configureNumericInput(input, min, max, step, value) {
        input.min = String(min);
        input.max = String(max);
        input.step = String(step);
        input.value = String(value);
    }

    buildCameraControls(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "CAMERA");
        parent.appendChild(this.createButton(
            "ZOOM TO ACTIVE CHARACTER",
            this.handleFocusActiveCharacter.bind(this)
        ));
        parent.appendChild(this.createButton(
            "CENTER ON SELECTED CHARACTER",
            this.handleCenterSelectedCharacter.bind(this)
        ));
    }

    handleApplyGridOrigin() {
        this.run(this.applyGridOrigin.bind(this));
    }

    applyGridOrigin() {
        const x = Number(this.gridX.value);
        const z = Number(this.gridZ.value);
        if (!Number.isSafeInteger(x) || !Number.isSafeInteger(z)) {
            throw new Error("World coordinates must be safe integers.");
        }
        if (!this.mode.setGridOrigin(x, z)) {
            throw new Error("Unable to reposition battlefield.");
        }
    }

    handleApplyGridConfiguration() {
        this.run(this.applyGridConfiguration.bind(this));
    }

    handleCenterSelectedCharacter() {
        this.mode.centerCameraOnSelectedCharacter();
    }

    buildBattleTab() {
        const parent = this.tabContents.battle;
        this.buildEncounterControls(parent);
        this.buildInitiativeControls(parent);
        this.buildMovementControls(parent);
        this.buildActionControls(parent);
        this.buildBonusActionControls(parent);
        this.buildSpeechControls(parent);
        this.buildBattleHistoryControls(parent);
    }

    buildEncounterControls(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "ENCOUNTER MANAGEMENT");
        this.startBattleButton = this.createButton("START BATTLE", this.handleStartBattle.bind(this));
        this.advanceButton = this.createButton("ADVANCE INITIATIVE", this.handleAdvanceTurn.bind(this));
        this.endBattleButton = this.createButton("END BATTLE", this.handleEndBattle.bind(this));
        this.endBattleButton.style.background = "#713b40";
        parent.append(this.startBattleButton, this.advanceButton, this.endBattleButton);
    }

    buildInitiativeControls(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "INITIATIVE ORDER");
        this.initiativeList = this.element("div");
        parent.appendChild(this.initiativeList);
    }

    buildMovementControls(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "MOVEMENT (SEPARATE FROM ACTIONS)");
        this.previewButton = this.createButton(
            "ENABLE MOVEMENT PREVIEW",
            this.handleToggleMovementPreview.bind(this)
        );
        parent.appendChild(this.previewButton);
        this.movementHelp = this.element(
            "p",
            "Green: remaining movement. Amber: additional movement available by using Dash (your action)."
        );
        this.movementHelp.style.color = "#b7cbd8";
        parent.appendChild(this.movementHelp);
    }

    buildActionControls(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "ACTION");
        this.actionSelect = this.createSelect(parent, "Available actions", ACTION_OPTIONS);
        this.actionButton = this.createButton("USE ACTION", this.handleUseAction.bind(this));
        parent.appendChild(this.actionButton);
    }

    buildBonusActionControls(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "BONUS ACTION");
        this.bonusSelect = this.createSelect(parent, "Available bonus actions", BONUS_OPTIONS);
        this.bonusButton = this.createButton("USE BONUS ACTION", this.handleUseBonusAction.bind(this));
        parent.appendChild(this.bonusButton);
    }

    buildSpeechControls(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "CHARACTER SPEECH");
        this.speechInput = this.createTextarea(parent, "Enter character dialogue...");
        parent.appendChild(this.createButton("RECORD SPEECH", this.handleRecordSpeech.bind(this)));
    }

    buildBattleHistoryControls(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "BATTLE HISTORY");
        this.historyList = this.element("div");
        parent.appendChild(this.historyList);
    }

    handleStartBattle() {
        this.run(this.mode.startBattle.bind(this.mode));
    }

    handleAdvanceTurn() {
        this.run(this.mode.advanceTurn.bind(this.mode));
    }

    handleEndBattle() {
        this.run(this.mode.endBattle.bind(this.mode));
    }

    handleToggleMovementPreview() {
        this.mode.setMovementPreview(!this.mode.movementPreview);
        this.refresh();
    }

    handleUseAction() {
        this.run(this.executeSelectedAction.bind(this));
    }

    executeSelectedAction() {
        const character = this.requireActiveCharacter();
        const selected = findOptionByValue(ACTION_OPTIONS, this.actionSelect.value);
        if (!selected) throw new Error("Select a valid action.");
        this.mode.performAction(character, selected[1]);
    }

    handleUseBonusAction() {
        this.run(this.executeSelectedBonusAction.bind(this));
    }

    executeSelectedBonusAction() {
        const character = this.requireActiveCharacter();
        const selected = findOptionByValue(BONUS_OPTIONS, this.bonusSelect.value);
        if (!selected) throw new Error("Select a valid bonus action.");
        this.mode.performBonusAction(character, selected[1]);
    }

    requireActiveCharacter() {
        const character = this.mode.getActiveCharacter();
        if (!character) throw new Error("There is no active character.");
        return character;
    }

    handleRecordSpeech() {
        this.run(this.recordCharacterSpeech.bind(this));
    }

    recordCharacterSpeech() {
        const character = this.requireActiveCharacter();
        const speech = this.speechInput.value.trim();
        if (!speech) throw new Error("Enter dialogue before recording.");
        this.mode.speakAsCharacter(character, speech);
        this.speechInput.value = "";
    }

    buildCharactersTab() {
        const parent = this.tabContents.characters;
        this.buildCharacterCreationForm(parent);
        this.buildAltitudeControls(parent);
        this.buildCharacterRoster(parent);
        this.buildSelectedCharacterDetails(parent);
    }

    buildCharacterCreationForm(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "CREATE CHARACTER");
        this.characterName = this.createInput(parent, "Character name");
        this.characterRace = this.createInput(parent, "Race");
        this.characterClass = this.createInput(parent, "Class");
        this.characterLevel = this.createInput(parent, "Level", "number");
        this.characterHP = this.createInput(parent, "Hit points", "number");
        this.characterAC = this.createInput(parent, "Armor class", "number");
        this.characterInitiative = this.createInput(parent, "Initiative bonus", "number");
        this.characterMovement = this.createInput(parent, "Movement allowance", "number");
        this.characterAltitude = this.createInput(parent, "Starting altitude above ground", "number");
        this.configureCharacterDefaults();
        parent.appendChild(this.createButton(
            "CREATE CHARACTER",
            this.handleCreateCharacter.bind(this)
        ));
    }

    configureCharacterDefaults() {
        this.characterLevel.value = "1";
        this.characterHP.value = "12";
        this.characterAC.value = "10";
        this.characterInitiative.value = "0";
        this.characterMovement.value = "30";
        this.configureNumericInput(this.characterAltitude, ALTITUDE_MIN, ALTITUDE_MAX, 0.5, 0);
    }

    handleCreateCharacter() {
        this.run(this.createCharacterFromForm.bind(this));
    }

    createCharacterFromForm() {
        const values = this.readCharacterForm();
        const row = Math.floor(this.mode.gridRows / 2);
        const col = Math.floor(this.mode.gridColumns / 2);
        const character = this.mode.addCharacter(values.name, row, col);
        this.applyCharacterForm(character, values);
        this.mode.setCharacterAltitude(character, values.altitude);
        this.mode.saveCharacters();
        this.mode.refreshUI();
        this.characterName.value = "";
    }

    readCharacterForm() {
        const values = {
            name: this.characterName.value.trim(),
            level: Number(this.characterLevel.value),
            hitPoints: Number(this.characterHP.value),
            armorClass: Number(this.characterAC.value),
            initiative: Number(this.characterInitiative.value),
            movement: Number(this.characterMovement.value),
            altitude: Number(this.characterAltitude.value)
        };
        this.validateCharacterForm(values);
        return values;
    }

    validateCharacterForm(values) {
        if (!values.name) throw new Error("Enter a character name.");
        if (
            !Number.isInteger(values.level) || values.level < 1 ||
            !Number.isFinite(values.hitPoints) || values.hitPoints < 1 ||
            !Number.isFinite(values.armorClass) || values.armorClass < 0 ||
            !Number.isFinite(values.initiative) ||
            !Number.isFinite(values.movement) || values.movement < 0 ||
            !Number.isFinite(values.altitude) ||
            values.altitude < ALTITUDE_MIN || values.altitude > ALTITUDE_MAX
        ) throw new Error("Check the character statistics and altitude.");
    }

    applyCharacterForm(character, values) {
        character.race = this.characterRace.value.trim() || "Unknown";
        character.className = this.characterClass.value.trim() || "Adventurer";
        character.level = values.level;
        character.hitPoints = values.hitPoints;
        character.maxHitPoints = values.hitPoints;
        character.armorClass = values.armorClass;
        character.initiative = values.initiative;
        character.movement = values.movement;
    }

    buildAltitudeControls(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "FLIGHT & ALTITUDE");
        this.selectedAltitudeInput = this.createInput(
            parent,
            "Selected character altitude above ground (world units)",
            "number"
        );
        this.configureNumericInput(this.selectedAltitudeInput, ALTITUDE_MIN, ALTITUDE_MAX, 0.5, 0);
        parent.appendChild(this.createButton(
            "APPLY ALTITUDE TO SELECTED CHARACTER",
            this.handleApplySelectedAltitude.bind(this)
        ));
        this.altitudeHelp = this.element(
            "p",
            "Altitude is measured vertically above the sampled terrain surface. Set 0 to place a character on the ground."
        );
        this.altitudeHelp.style.color = "#b7cbd8";
        parent.appendChild(this.altitudeHelp);
    }

    handleApplySelectedAltitude() {
        this.run(this.applySelectedAltitude.bind(this));
    }

    applySelectedAltitude() {
        const character = this.mode.selectedCharacter;
        if (!character) throw new Error("Select a character first.");
        this.mode.setCharacterAltitude(
            character,
            Number(this.selectedAltitudeInput.value)
        );
    }

    buildCharacterRoster(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "CHARACTER ROSTER");
        this.characterList = this.element("div");
        parent.appendChild(this.characterList);
    }

    buildSelectedCharacterDetails(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "SELECTED CHARACTER");
        this.characterDetails = this.element("div");
        parent.appendChild(this.characterDetails);
    }

    buildSettingsTab() {
        const parent = this.tabContents.settings;
        this.buildMovementColorSettings(parent);
        this.buildInterfaceSettings(parent);
        this.buildControlHelp(parent);
    }

    buildMovementColorSettings(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "MOVEMENT COLORS");
        this.movementColor = this.createInput(parent, "Normal movement", "color");
        this.dashColor = this.createInput(parent, "Extended / Dash movement", "color");
        this.movementColor.value = this.colors.movement;
        this.dashColor.value = this.colors.dash;
        parent.appendChild(this.createButton(
            "SAVE MOVEMENT COLORS",
            this.handleSaveMovementColors.bind(this)
        ));
    }

    buildInterfaceSettings(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "INTERFACE");
        parent.appendChild(this.createButton("HIDE TACTICAL MENU", this.handleHideMenu.bind(this)));
        parent.appendChild(this.createButton("SHOW TACTICAL MENU", this.handleShowMenu.bind(this)));
    }

    buildControlHelp(parent) {
        this.addSection(parent, "CONTROLS");
        const controls = [
            "M — Toggle tactical menu",
            "ESC — Hide tactical menu",
            "Arrow Left — Previous character",
            "Arrow Right — Next character",
            "Mouse wheel — Zoom camera",
            "Left mouse drag — Camera interaction"
        ];
        for (const description of controls) {
            const item = this.element("p", description);
            item.style.color = "#b7cbd8";
            parent.appendChild(item);
        }
    }

    handleSaveMovementColors() {
        this.colors.movement = this.movementColor.value;
        this.colors.dash = this.dashColor.value;
        this.saveColors();
        this.showMessage("Movement colors saved.");
    }

    handleHideMenu() {
        this.setVisible(false);
    }

    handleShowMenu() {
        this.setVisible(true);
    }

    handleFocusActiveCharacter() {
        this.run(this.zoomToActiveCharacter.bind(this));
    }

    zoomToActiveCharacter() {
        return this.mode.zoomToActiveCharacter();
    }

    applyGridConfiguration() {
        const rows = Number(this.gridRowsInput.value);
        const columns = Number(this.gridColumnsInput.value);
        const cellSize = Number(this.cellSizeInput.value);

        this.mode.setGridConfiguration(rows, columns, cellSize);
    }

    run(callback) {
        try {
            const result = callback();

            if (result && typeof result.then === "function") {
                result.then(
                    this.handleRunSuccess.bind(this),
                    this.handleRunFailure.bind(this)
                );
            } else {
                this.refresh();
            }

            return result;
        } catch (error) {
            this.showMessage(error.message);
            console.error("[DnD UI]", error);
            return null;
        }
    }

    handleRunSuccess() {
        this.refresh();
    }

    handleRunFailure(error) {
        this.showMessage(error.message);
        console.error("[DnD UI]", error);
    }

    createTextRow(parent, label, value) {
        const row = this.element("div");

        Object.assign(row.style, {
            display: "flex",
            justifyContent: "space-between",
            gap: "8px",
            padding: "5px 2px",
            borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(120,160,185,0.2)"
        });

        const left = this.element("span", label);
        const right = this.element(
            "span",
            String(value ?? "—")
        );

        right.style.color = "#9dd8ed";

        row.appendChild(left);
        row.appendChild(right);
        parent.appendChild(row);

        return row;
    }

    refreshInitiative() {
        const list = this.initiativeList;
        list.replaceChildren();

        const battle = this.mode.activeBattle;

        if (!battle?.active) {
            list.textContent = "No active encounter.";
            return;
        }

        for (let index = 0; index < battle.turnOrder.length; index++) {
            this.appendInitiativeEntry(list, battle.turnOrder[index], index, battle);
        }
    }

    appendInitiativeEntry(list, characterId, index, battle) {
        const character = this.mode.characters.get(characterId);
        if (!character) return;
        const active = index === battle.currentTurn;
        const item = this.createButton(
            `${active ? "▶ " : ""}${character.name}`,
            this.handleCharacterSelect.bind(this)
        );
        item.dataset.characterId = characterId;
        item.style.textAlign = "left";
        item.style.background = active ? "#72551b" : "#193b55";
        item.style.color = active ? "#fff0b5" : "#d6e6ef";
        list.appendChild(item);
    }

    handleCharacterSelect(event) {
        const characterId = event.currentTarget.dataset.characterId;
        this.mode.selectCharacter(characterId);
        this.refresh();
    }

    handleRemoveCharacter(event) {
        const characterId = event.currentTarget.dataset.characterId;
        this.run(this.mode.removeCharacter.bind(this.mode, characterId));
    }

    refreshCharacters() {
        const list = this.characterList;
        list.replaceChildren();

        for (const character of this.mode.characters.values()) {
            this.appendCharacterRosterRow(list, character);
        }

        if (!this.mode.characters.size) {
            list.textContent = "No characters created.";
        }

        this.refreshCharacterDetails();
    }

    appendCharacterRosterRow(list, character) {
        const item = document.createElement("div");
        Object.assign(item.style, {
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "space-between",
            gap: "5px",
            padding: "7px",
            margin: "4px 0",
            background: character.id === this.mode.selectedCharacter?.id ? "#72551b" : "#193b55",
            borderRadius: "4px"
        });
        const select = this.createButton(character.name, this.handleCharacterSelect.bind(this));
        select.dataset.characterId = character.id;
        select.style.flex = "1";
        const remove = this.createButton("×", this.handleRemoveCharacter.bind(this));
        remove.dataset.characterId = character.id;
        remove.style.width = "40px";
        remove.style.background = "#713b40";
        item.append(select, remove);
        list.appendChild(item);
    }

    refreshCharacterDetails() {
        const parent = this.characterDetails;
        parent.replaceChildren();
        const character = this.mode.selectedCharacter;
        if (!character) {
            parent.textContent = "Select a character to view details.";
            return;
        }
        this.appendCharacterSummary(parent, character);
        this.appendCharacterAbilityScores(parent, character);
    }

    appendCharacterSummary(parent, character) {
        this.createTextRow(parent, "Name", character.name);
        this.createTextRow(parent, "Race", character.race);
        this.createTextRow(parent, "Class", character.className);
        this.createTextRow(parent, "Level", character.level);
        this.createTextRow(parent, "Hit points", `${character.hitPoints}/${character.maxHitPoints}`);
        this.createTextRow(parent, "Armor class", character.armorClass);
        this.createTextRow(parent, "Initiative", character.initiative);
        this.createTextRow(parent, "Movement", character.movement);
        this.createTextRow(parent, "Altitude above ground", `${character.position.altitude ?? 0} world units`);
        this.createTextRow(parent, "Grid row", character.position.row);
        this.createTextRow(parent, "Grid column", character.position.col);
    }

    appendCharacterAbilityScores(parent, character) {
        this.addSection(parent, "ABILITY SCORES");
        for (const [ability, score] of Object.entries(character.stats)) {
            this.appendAbilityScore(parent, character, ability, score);
        }
    }

    appendAbilityScore(parent, character, ability, score) {
        const modifier = character.getModifier(ability);
        const sign = modifier >= 0 ? "+" : "";
        this.createTextRow(parent, ability.toUpperCase(), `${score} (${sign}${modifier})`);
    }

    refreshHistory() {
        const parent = this.historyList;
        parent.replaceChildren();
        const battle = this.mode.activeBattle;
        if (!battle || !battle.actions.length) {
            parent.textContent = "No recorded battle events.";
            return;
        }
        const events = battle.actions.slice(-30).reverse();
        for (const event of events) this.appendHistoryEntry(parent, event);
    }

    appendHistoryEntry(parent, event) {
        const character = this.mode.characters.get(event.characterId);
        const timestamp = new Date(event.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString();
        const entry = this.createHistoryEntry();
        const heading = this.element("div", `${timestamp} — ${character?.name || "System"}`);
        heading.style.color = "#9dd8ed";
        entry.append(heading, this.element("div", this.describeHistoryEvent(event)));
        parent.appendChild(entry);
    }

    createHistoryEntry() {
        const entry = this.element("div");
        Object.assign(entry.style, {
            padding: "7px",
            margin: "4px 0",
            background: "#193b55",
            borderRadius: "4px",
            overflowWrap: "anywhere"
        });
        return entry;
    }

    describeHistoryEvent(event) {
        if (event.type === "move") return "Movement";
        if (event.type === "action") return `Action: ${event.data.description || "Other"}`;
        if (event.type === "bonus-action") return `Bonus: ${event.data.description || "Other"}`;
        if (event.type === "speech") return `Speech: ${event.data.text}`;
        if (event.type === "other") return `Custom: ${event.data.payload?.type || "Action"}`;
        return event.type;
    }

    refreshBattleStatus() {
        const battle = this.mode.activeBattle;
        const character = this.mode.getActiveCharacter();

        if (!battle?.active) {
            this.battleStatus.textContent =
                "Encounter inactive. Create characters and start a battle.";
            return;
        }

        const resources = battle.currentResources;

        const allowance = (character?.movement ?? 0) * (resources?.dashUsed ? 2 : 1);
        this.battleStatus.textContent = [
            `ROUND ${battle.round}`,
            `ACTIVE: ${character?.name || "Unknown"}`,
            `MOVEMENT: ${resources?.movementUsed || 0}/${allowance}`,
            `ACTION: ${resources?.actionUsed ? "USED" : "AVAILABLE"}`,
            `BONUS: ${resources?.bonusActionUsed ? "USED" : "AVAILABLE"}`,
            `DASH: ${resources?.dashUsed ? "USED" : "AVAILABLE"}`
        ].join("  |  ");
    }

    refresh() {
        if (!this.panel || !this.mode) return;
        this.refreshRegistrationStatus();
        this.refreshBattleControls();
        this.refreshBattleStatus();
        this.refreshInitiative();
        this.refreshCharacters();
        this.refreshHistory();
        this.refreshGridInformation();
        this.refreshAltitudeControl();
    }

    refreshRegistrationStatus() {
        this.status.textContent = `${this.mode.characters.size} characters registered`;
    }

    refreshBattleControls() {
        const battle = this.mode.activeBattle;
        const active = Boolean(battle?.active);
        const resources = battle?.currentResources;
        this.setButtonDisabled(this.startBattleButton, active || !this.mode.characters.size);
        this.setButtonDisabled(this.advanceButton, !active);
        this.setButtonDisabled(this.endBattleButton, !active);
        this.setButtonDisabled(this.actionButton, !active || Boolean(resources?.actionUsed));
        this.setButtonDisabled(this.bonusButton, !active || Boolean(resources?.bonusActionUsed));
        this.previewButton.disabled = !active;
        this.previewButton.style.opacity = active ? "1" : "0.45";
    }

    setButtonDisabled(button, disabled) {
        button.disabled = disabled;
        button.style.opacity = disabled ? "0.45" : "1";
    }

    refreshGridInformation() {
        this.gridInfo.textContent = [
            `Dimensions: ${this.mode.gridRows} rows × ${this.mode.gridColumns} columns`,
            `Cell size: ${this.mode.cellSize} world units`,
            `Total width: ${this.mode.gridColumns * this.mode.cellSize}`,
            `Total depth: ${this.mode.gridRows * this.mode.cellSize}`,
            `Origin: ${this.mode.gridOrigin.x}, ${this.mode.gridOrigin.z}`
        ].join("\n");
        this.gridRowsInput.value = String(this.mode.gridRows);
        this.gridColumnsInput.value = String(this.mode.gridColumns);
        this.cellSizeInput.value = String(this.mode.cellSize);
        this.refreshMovementPreviewLabel();
    }

    refreshMovementPreviewLabel() {
        this.previewButton.textContent = this.mode.movementPreview
            ? "DISABLE MOVEMENT PREVIEW"
            : "ENABLE MOVEMENT PREVIEW";
    }

    refreshAltitudeControl() {
        const selected = this.mode.selectedCharacter;
        this.selectedAltitudeInput.value = String(selected?.position.altitude ?? 0);
        this.selectedAltitudeInput.disabled = !selected;
    }

    showMessage(message) {
        if (!this.message) return;

        this.message.textContent = String(message);

        this.message.style.color = "#ffd27d";
    }

    toggle() {
        this.setVisible(!this.visible);
    }

    setVisible(visible) {
        this.visible = Boolean(visible);

        if (this.panel) {
            this.panel.style.display =
                this.visible ? "block" : "none";
        }

        if (this.toggleButton) {
            this.toggleButton.textContent =
                this.visible ? "× HIDE MENU" : "☰ D&D MENU";
        }
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        window.removeEventListener(
            "keydown",
            this.onKeyDown
        );

        this.toggleButton?.remove();
        this.focusButton?.remove();
        this.panel?.remove();

        this.panel = null;
        this.toggleButton = null;
        this.focusButton = null;

        this.tabContents = {};
        this.tabBar = null;
    }
}

export { DnDTacticalUI };


========================================================================
FILE: src/game/game-manager.js
========================================================================


class GameManager {
    constructor(scene, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.canvas = options.canvas;

        this.flightCamera = options.flightCamera;
        this.thirdPersonCamera = null;
        this.editorCamera = null;

        this.playerController = null;
        this.characterCameraController = null;

        this.mode = "menu";
        this.resumeMode = "flight";

        this.callbacks = new Set();
        this.disposed = false;

        if (!this.flightCamera) {
            throw new Error("Flight camera is required.");
        }

        this.flightCamera.detachControl();

        this.onKeyDown = event => {
            if (event.repeat) return;

            if (event.code === "KeyV") {
                if (
                    this.mode === "flight" ||
                    this.mode === "thirdPerson"
                ) {
                    this.togglePerspective();
                }
            }

            if (event.code === "Escape") {
                if (this.mode === "editor") {
                    this.exitEditor();
                } else if (
                    this.mode === "flight" ||
                    this.mode === "thirdPerson"
                ) {
                    this.pause();
                } else if (this.mode === "paused") {
                    this.resume();
                }
            }
        };

        window.addEventListener("keydown", this.onKeyDown);
    }

    registerThirdPerson(camera, controller, cameraController) {
        this.thirdPersonCamera = camera;
        this.playerController = controller;
        this.characterCameraController = cameraController;

        camera.detachControl();
        this.applyMode();
    }

    registerEditor(camera) {
        this.editorCamera = camera;
        camera.detachControl();
    }

    setMode(mode) {
        const valid = [
            "menu",
            "flight",
            "thirdPerson",
            "paused",
            "editor",
		"dnd"
        ];

        if (!valid.includes(mode)) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid game mode: ${mode}`);
        }

        if (
            mode === "thirdPerson" &&
            (!this.thirdPersonCamera || !this.playerController)
        ) {
            throw new Error("Third-person systems are not registered.");
        }

        if (mode === "editor" && !this.editorCamera) {
            throw new Error("Editor camera is not registered.");
        }

        if (this.mode === mode) return;

        const previous = this.mode;

        if (
            previous === "flight" ||
            previous === "thirdPerson"
        ) {
            this.resumeMode = previous;
        }

        this.mode = mode;
        this.applyMode();

        for (const callback of this.callbacks) {
            callback(mode, previous);
        }
    }

    applyMode() {
        this.flightCamera.detachControl();
        this.thirdPersonCamera?.detachControl();
        this.editorCamera?.detachControl();

        this.playerController?.setEnabled(false);
        this.characterCameraController?.setEnabled(false);

        if (this.mode === "flight") {
            this.scene.activeCamera = this.flightCamera;
            this.flightCamera.attachControl(this.canvas, true);
        }

	if (this.mode === "dnd") {
	    this.scene.activeCamera = this.flightCamera;
	}

        if (this.mode === "thirdPerson") {
            this.scene.activeCamera = this.thirdPersonCamera;
            this.playerController.setEnabled(true);
            this.characterCameraController.setEnabled(true);
        }

        if (this.mode === "editor") {
            this.scene.activeCamera = this.editorCamera;
            this.editorCamera.attachControl(this.canvas, true);
        }

        if (this.mode === "menu" || this.mode === "paused") {
            this.scene.activeCamera = this.flightCamera;
        }
    }

    startFlight() {
        this.setMode("flight");
    }

    startThirdPerson() {
        this.setMode("thirdPerson");
    }

    startEditor() {
        this.setMode("editor");
    }

	startDnD() {
	    this.setMode("dnd");
	}

    exitEditor() {
        if (this.mode !== "editor") return;

        this.setMode(this.resumeMode);
    }

    togglePerspective() {
        if (this.mode === "flight") {
            this.startThirdPerson();
        } else if (this.mode === "thirdPerson") {
            this.startFlight();
        }
    }

    pause() {
        if (
            this.mode !== "flight" &&
            this.mode !== "thirdPerson"
        ) return;

        this.resumeMode = this.mode;
        this.setMode("paused");
    }

    resume() {
        if (this.mode === "paused") {
            this.setMode(this.resumeMode);
        }
    }

    returnToMenu() {
        this.setMode("menu");
    }

    onModeChanged(callback) {
        this.callbacks.add(callback);
        return () => this.callbacks.delete(callback);
    }

    getState() {
        return {
            mode: this.mode,
            resumeMode: this.resumeMode
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        window.removeEventListener("keydown", this.onKeyDown);

        this.flightCamera.detachControl();
        this.thirdPersonCamera?.detachControl();
        this.editorCamera?.detachControl();

        this.callbacks.clear();
    }
}

export { GameManager };


========================================================================
FILE: src/game/player-character.js
========================================================================

class PlayerCharacter {
    constructor(scene, spatialTransform, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        this.worldPosition = {
            x: options.x ?? 0,
            y: 0,
            z: options.z ?? 0
        };

        this.yaw = 0;

        const sample = options.terrain.sampleAt(
            this.worldPosition.x,
            this.worldPosition.z
        );

        if (!sample) {
            throw new Error("Unable to sample initial player terrain.");
        }

        this.worldPosition.y = sample.y;

        this.root = new BABYLON.TransformNode(
            "somniumPlayer",
            scene
        );

        this.body = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateCapsule(
            "playerBody",
            {
                height: 1.8,
                radius: 0.35,
                tessellation: 12
            },
            scene
        );

        this.body.parent = this.root;
        this.body.position.y = 0.9;

        this.head = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
            "playerHead",
            {
                diameter: 0.48,
                segments: 12
            },
            scene
        );

        this.head.parent = this.root;
        this.head.position.y = 1.95;

        this.material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "playerMaterial",
            scene
        );

        this.material.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.18, 0.48, 0.78);

        this.body.material = this.material;

        this.head.material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "playerHeadMaterial",
            scene
        );

        this.head.material.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.82, 0.69, 0.55);

        this.syncRenderPosition();
    }

    syncRenderPosition() {
        const projected = this.transform.worldToRender(
            this.worldPosition
        );

        this.root.position.set(
            projected.x,
            projected.y,
            projected.z
        );

        this.root.rotation.y = this.yaw;
    }

    setWorldPosition(position) {
        this.worldPosition = {
            x: position.x,
            y: position.y,
            z: position.z
        };

        this.syncRenderPosition();
    }

    dispose() {
        this.root.dispose(false, true);
        this.material.dispose();
    }
}

export { PlayerCharacter };


========================================================================
FILE: src/game/player-controller.js
========================================================================

class PlayerController {
    constructor(character, terrain, canvas, options = {}) {
        this.character = character;
        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.canvas = canvas;

        this.walkSpeed = options.walkSpeed ?? 180;
        this.runSpeed = options.runSpeed ?? 360;
        this.turnSpeed = options.turnSpeed ?? 2.2;

        this.keys = new Set();

        this.enabled = true;
        this.disposed = false;

        this.mouseSensitivity = 0.004;
        this.dragging = false;
        this.previousMouseX = 0;

        this.onKeyDown = event => {
            this.keys.add(event.code);

            if (
                ["KeyW", "KeyA", "KeyS", "KeyD", "ShiftLeft"]
                    .includes(event.code)
            ) {
                event.preventDefault();
            }
        };

        this.onKeyUp = event => {
            this.keys.delete(event.code);
        };

        this.onBlur = () => {
            this.keys.clear();
            this.dragging = false;
        };

        this.onPointerDown = event => {
            if (event.button !== 0) return;

            this.dragging = true;
            this.previousMouseX = event.clientX;
        };

        this.onPointerMove = event => {
            if (!this.dragging || !this.enabled) return;

            const dx = event.clientX - this.previousMouseX;

            this.previousMouseX = event.clientX;

            this.character.yaw +=
                dx * this.mouseSensitivity;
        };

        this.onPointerUp = () => {
            this.dragging = false;
        };

        window.addEventListener("keydown", this.onKeyDown);
        window.addEventListener("keyup", this.onKeyUp);
        window.addEventListener("blur", this.onBlur);

        canvas.addEventListener(
            "pointerdown",
            this.onPointerDown
        );

        window.addEventListener(
            "pointermove",
            this.onPointerMove
        );

        window.addEventListener(
            "pointerup",
            this.onPointerUp
        );
    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
        this.keys.clear();
        this.dragging = false;
    }

    update(deltaSeconds) {
        if (!this.enabled || this.disposed) return;

        const dt = Math.min(deltaSeconds, 0.05);

        if (dt <= 0) return;

        const forward =
            (this.keys.has("KeyW") ? 1 : 0) -
            (this.keys.has("KeyS") ? 1 : 0);

        const turn =
            (this.keys.has("KeyA") ? 1 : 0) -
            (this.keys.has("KeyD") ? 1 : 0);

        this.character.yaw +=
            turn * this.turnSpeed * dt;

        const speed = this.keys.has("ShiftLeft")
            ? this.runSpeed
            : this.walkSpeed;

        const yaw = this.character.yaw;

        const dx = Math.sin(yaw) * forward * speed * dt;
        const dz = Math.cos(yaw) * forward * speed * dt;

        if (dx === 0 && dz === 0) {
            return;
        }

        const current = this.character.worldPosition;

        const nextX = current.x + dx;
        const nextZ = current.z + dz;

        // Respect the authoritative world bounds.
        if (
            nextX < this.terrain.min.x ||
            nextX > this.terrain.max.x ||
            nextZ < this.terrain.min.z ||
            nextZ > this.terrain.max.z
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const sample = this.terrain.sampleAt(
            nextX,
            nextZ
        );

        if (!sample) return;

        // Initial terrain-following implementation.
        // Steep-slope collision will be added separately.
        this.character.setWorldPosition({
            x: nextX,
            y: sample.y,
            z: nextZ
        });
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        window.removeEventListener(
            "keydown",
            this.onKeyDown
        );

        window.removeEventListener(
            "keyup",
            this.onKeyUp
        );

        window.removeEventListener(
            "blur",
            this.onBlur
        );

        this.canvas.removeEventListener(
            "pointerdown",
            this.onPointerDown
        );

        window.removeEventListener(
            "pointermove",
            this.onPointerMove
        );

        window.removeEventListener(
            "pointerup",
            this.onPointerUp
        );

        this.keys.clear();
    }
}

export { PlayerController };

import { fractalNoise2D } from "../core/noise.js";

    granite: {
        density: 2.70,
        hardness: 0.98,
        cohesion: 0.95,
        porosity: 0.02,
        moistureCapacity: 0.12,

const clamp = (value, min = 0, max = 1) =>
    Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));

class GeologicalField {
    constructor(terrain, seed = 91827) {
        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.seed = seed;

        this.regionFrequency = 0.0025;
        this.detailFrequency = 0.009;
        this.slopeSamplingDistance = 4;
    }

    noise(x, z, salt, frequency, octaves = 3) {
        return fractalNoise2D(
            x * frequency,
            z * frequency,
            this.seed + salt,
            octaves
        );
    }

    sample(x, z) {
        const profile = this.terrain.sampleProfileAt(
            x,
            z,
            this.slopeSamplingDistance
        );

        if (!profile) return null;

        const elevation = profile.elevation;
        const slope = profile.slopeDegrees;

        const moistureNoise = this.noise(
            x, z, 1000, this.regionFrequency
        );

        const geologyNoise = this.noise(
            x, z, 2000, this.regionFrequency * 1.5
        );

        const detail = this.noise(
            x, z, 3000, this.detailFrequency
        );

        const strata = this.noise(
            x, z, 4000, 0.004, 4
        );

        const surfaceVariation = this.noise(
            x, z, 5000, 0.025
        );

        const anomalyNoise = this.noise(
            x, z, 6000, 0.0015
        );

        const moisture = clamp(
            moistureNoise * 0.7 + detail * 0.3
        );

        // Determine the underlying geological formation.
        let bedrockType;

        if (anomalyNoise > 0.985) {
            bedrockType = "anomaly";
        } else if (geologyNoise < 0.28) {
            bedrockType = "limestone";
        } else if (geologyNoise < 0.48) {
            bedrockType = "sandstone";
        } else if (geologyNoise > 0.84) {
            bedrockType = "basalt";
        } else {
            bedrockType = "granite";
        }

        if (elevation > 0.70 && geologyNoise > 0.65) {
            bedrockType = "granite";
        }

        // Bedrock exposure increases on steep slopes.
        const exposure = clamp(
            slope / 42 * 0.78 +
            elevation * 0.12 +
            (1 - moisture) * 0.10
        );

	// -----------------------------------------
// TOPOGRAPHIC SOIL DISTRIBUTION
// -----------------------------------------

const mapValue = this.terrain.sampleMapValue(x, z);

let surfaceSoil;

if (mapValue === null) {
    surfaceSoil = "loam";
} else if (mapValue < 0.18) {
    surfaceSoil = "clay";
} else if (mapValue < 0.38) {
    surfaceSoil = "sand";
} else if (mapValue < 0.58) {
    surfaceSoil = "fertileSoil";
} else if (mapValue < 0.76) {
    surfaceSoil = "loam";
} else if (mapValue < 0.90) {
    surfaceSoil = "organic";
} else {
    surfaceSoil = "scree";
}

// Local geology still controls exposed bedrock.
if (exposure > 0.88 || (slope > 32 && exposure > 0.65)) {
    surfaceSoil = "scree";
}

        const surface = MATERIALS[surfaceSoil];
        const bedrock = MATERIALS[bedrockType];

        // Variable soil-horizon depths.
        const soilDepth = Math.max(
            0.08,
            (1 - exposure) *
            (0.8 + moisture * 2.2) *
            (0.75 + surfaceVariation * 0.5)
        );

        const organicThickness = soilDepth *
            (surfaceSoil === "organic" ? 0.22 : 0.06);

        const topsoilThickness = soilDepth * 0.42;
        const subsoilThickness = soilDepth * 0.34;
        const parentThickness = soilDepth -
            organicThickness -
            topsoilThickness -
            subsoilThickness;

        const horizons = {
            O: {
                material: "organic",
                thickness: organicThickness
            },
            A: {
                material: surfaceSoil,
                thickness: topsoilThickness
            },
            B: {
                material: geologyNoise > 0.58 ? "clay" : "silt",
                thickness: subsoilThickness
            },
            C: {
                material: bedrockType,
                thickness: parentThickness
            }
        };

        const weathering = clamp(
            moisture * 0.45 +
            surfaceVariation * 0.25 +
            (1 - bedrock.hardness) * 0.30
        );

        return {
            position: profile.position,
            normal: profile.normal,
            elevation,
            slope,
            slopeDegrees: slope,

            speciesName: exposure > 0.82
                ? bedrockType
                : surfaceSoil,

            bedrockType,
            surfaceSoil,
            horizons,

            soilDepth,
            exposure,
            weathering,
            moisture,
            geology: geologyNoise,
            strata,
            surfaceVariation,

            density: surface.density,
            hardness: surface.hardness,
            cohesion: surface.cohesion,
            porosity: surface.porosity,
            moistureCapacity: surface.moistureCapacity,
            infiltration: surface.infiltration,
            drainage: surface.drainage,
            fertility: surface.fertility,
            roughness: surface.roughness,

            bedrockProperties: { ...bedrock },
            soilProperties: { ...surface },
            material: { ...surface },
            color: surface.color
        };
    }

    getMaterialAt(x, z) {
        return this.sample(x, z);
    }

    getSoilProfileAt(x, z) {
        const sample = this.sample(x, z);

        if (!sample) return null;

        return {
            horizons: sample.horizons,
            soilDepth: sample.soilDepth,
            surfaceSoil: sample.surfaceSoil,
            bedrockType: sample.bedrockType,
            fertility: sample.fertility,
            moistureCapacity: sample.moistureCapacity,
            infiltration: sample.infiltration,
            drainage: sample.drainage,
            exposure: sample.exposure
        };
    }

    getMaterialProperties(x, z) {
        const sample = this.sample(x, z);

        if (!sample) return null;

        return {
            density: sample.density,
            hardness: sample.hardness,
            cohesion: sample.cohesion,
            porosity: sample.porosity,
            moistureCapacity: sample.moistureCapacity,
            infiltration: sample.infiltration,
            drainage: sample.drainage,
            fertility: sample.fertility,
            roughness: sample.roughness
        };
    }
}

export { GeologicalField, MATERIALS };


========================================================================
FILE: src/geology/geological-manager.js
========================================================================

import { GeologicalField } from "./geological-field.js";
import { SurfaceDepositSystem } from "./surface-deposit-system.js";
import { RockSpecies } from "./rock-species.js";
import { ThinInstanceRenderer } from "../rendering/thin-instance-renderer.js";
import { CreateSeededRandom } from "../core/random.js";
import { RockGenerator } from "../geology/rock-generator.js";

let nextFormationId = 0;

class GeologicalManager {

    constructor(scene, terrain, shadowGenerator) {

        this.scene = scene;

        this.terrain = terrain;

        this.shadowGenerator = shadowGenerator;

        this.regionSize = 250;

        this.seed = 91827;

        this.field = new GeologicalField(
            terrain,
            this.seed
        );

	this.surfaceDeposits =
    new SurfaceDepositSystem(
        scene,
        terrain,
        this.field
    );

        this.regions = new Map();

        this.formations = [];

        this.maxFormations = 15000;

        this.minFragmentScale = 0.35;

        this.maxFragmentDepth = 2;

        this.renderDirty = true;

        this.simulationSteps = 0;

        this.renderEverySteps = 5;

        this.renderers = {};


        // One independent rendering batch per material.
        for (const speciesName in RockSpecies) {

            const config = RockSpecies[speciesName];

            const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
                "geology_" + speciesName,
                {
                    diameter: 1,
                    segments: 2
                },
                scene
            );

            const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
                "geologyMaterial_" + speciesName,
                scene
            );

            material.diffuseColor =
                config.color.clone();

            material.specularColor =
                new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.06,
                    0.06,
                    0.06
                );

            if (speciesName === "anomaly") {

                material.emissiveColor =
                    config.color.scale(0.35);

            }

            mesh.material = material;

            this.renderers[speciesName] =
                new ThinInstanceRenderer(
                    scene,
                    "geology_" + speciesName,
                    mesh,
                    shadowGenerator
                );

        }

    }


    getRegionKey(x, z) {

        return `${x},${z}`;

    }


    createFormation(
        speciesName,
        position,
        scale,
        seed,
        fragmentDepth = 0
    ) {

        if (
            !position ||
            this.formations.length >= this.maxFormations
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const formation = {

            id: nextFormationId++,

            speciesName,

            position: position.clone(),

            scale,

            age: 0,

            integrity: 1.0,

            erosionProgress: 0,

            erosionEvents: 0,

            fragmentDepth,

            seed: seed >>> 0,

            alive: true

        };

        this.formations.push(formation);

        return formation;

    }


    createRegion(regionX, regionZ) {

        const key = this.getRegionKey(
            regionX,
            regionZ
        );

        if (this.regions.has(key)) {

            return this.regions.get(key);

        }

        const seed = (
            Math.imul(regionX, 73856093) ^
            Math.imul(regionZ, 19349663) ^
            this.seed
        ) >>> 0;

        const centerX =
            (regionX + 0.5) * this.regionSize;

        const centerZ =
            (regionZ + 0.5) * this.regionSize;

        const centerSample =
            this.field.sample(centerX, centerZ);

        const region = {

            x: regionX,

            z: regionZ,

            seed,

            dominantSpecies:
                centerSample
                    ? centerSample.speciesName
                    : "granite",

            generated: false,

            formationIds: []

        };

        this.regions.set(key, region);

        return region;

    }


    generateRegion(regionX, regionZ) {

        const region = this.createRegion(
            regionX,
            regionZ
        );

        if (region.generated) return;

        const random =
            CreateSeededRandom(region.seed);

        const originX =
            regionX * this.regionSize;

        const originZ =
            regionZ * this.regionSize;


        const samplePosition = () => {

            const x = originX +
                random() * this.regionSize;

            const z = originZ +
                random() * this.regionSize;

            return this.field.sample(x, z);

        };


        const addFormation = (
            sample,
            scale,
            depth,
            salt
        ) => {

            if (!sample) return;
		// These materials have their own surface renderer.
    if (
        sample.speciesName === "sand" ||
        sample.speciesName === "clay" ||
        sample.speciesName === "fertileSoil"
    ) {
        return;
    }

            const speciesName =
                sample.speciesName;

            const config =
                RockSpecies[speciesName];

            // Small local variations.
            const variation =
                0.8 + random() * 0.4;

            const formation = this.createFormation(

                speciesName,

                sample.position,

                scale * variation,

                region.seed ^ salt,

                depth

            );

            if (formation) {

                region.formationIds.push(
                    formation.id
                );

            }

        };


        // PRIMARY GEOLOGICAL FORMATIONS
        // Large formations establish the region.

        const primaryCount = 7;

        for (let i = 0; i < primaryCount; i++) {

            const sample = samplePosition();

            if (!sample) continue;

            const config =
                RockSpecies[sample.speciesName];

            addFormation(

                sample,

                config.scale * 4.0,

                Math.min(2, config.recursionDepth),

                i + 1000

            );

        }


        // SECONDARY FORMATIONS
        // Medium-scale geological structures.

        const secondaryCount = 7;

        for (let i = 0; i < secondaryCount; i++) {

            const sample = samplePosition();

            if (!sample) continue;

            const config =
                RockSpecies[sample.speciesName];

            addFormation(

                sample,

                config.scale * 1.7,

                Math.min(2, config.recursionDepth),

                i + 2000

            );

        }


        // FINE GEOLOGICAL DETAIL

        const detailCount = 32;

        for (let i = 0; i < detailCount; i++) {

            const sample = samplePosition();

            if (!sample) continue;

            const config =
                RockSpecies[sample.speciesName];

            addFormation(

                sample,

                config.scale * (
                    0.35 + random() * 0.4
                ),

                Math.min(1, config.recursionDepth),

                i + 3000

            );

        }


        region.generated = true;

        this.renderDirty = true;

    }


    generateAll() {

        const minX = Math.floor(
            this.terrain.min.x / this.regionSize
        );

        const maxX = Math.floor(
            this.terrain.max.x / this.regionSize
        );

        const minZ = Math.floor(
            this.terrain.min.z / this.regionSize
        );

        const maxZ = Math.floor(
            this.terrain.max.z / this.regionSize
        );

        for (let x = minX; x <= maxX; x++) {

            for (let z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z++) {

                this.generateRegion(x, z);

            }

        }

	this.surfaceDeposits.generate();

this.rebuildRendering();


    }


    // ---------------------------------
    // GEOLOGICAL EROSION
    // ---------------------------------

    erodeFormation(formation, deltaYears) {

        if (!formation.alive) return false;

        const config =
            RockSpecies[formation.speciesName];

        formation.age += deltaYears;

        // Hardness and cohesion both resist erosion.
        const resistance =
            0.5 + config.hardness * 0.5;

        const cohesionResistance =
            0.5 + config.cohesion * 0.5;

        const erosion =
            config.erosionRate *
            deltaYears /
            (resistance * cohesionResistance);

        formation.erosionProgress += erosion;

        if (formation.erosionProgress < 1.0) {

            return false;

        }

        formation.erosionProgress -= 1.0;

        this.fragmentFormation(formation);

        return true;

    }


    fragmentFormation(parent) {

        const config =
            RockSpecies[parent.speciesName];

        parent.erosionEvents++;

        // Smaller parent after fracturing.
        parent.scale *= 0.72;

        parent.integrity = 1.0;

        const canFragment =

            parent.fragmentDepth <
                this.maxFragmentDepth &&

            parent.scale >
                this.minFragmentScale &&

            parent.erosionEvents <= 3 &&

            this.formations.length <
                this.maxFormations;


        if (!canFragment) {

            if (
                parent.erosionEvents >= 3 ||
                parent.scale <= this.minFragmentScale
            ) {

                parent.alive = false;

            }

            return;

        }


        const random = CreateSeededRandom(
            parent.seed ^
            Math.imul(parent.erosionEvents, 7919)
        );


        const fragmentCount = Math.max(

            2,

            Math.round(
                2 + config.fragmentation * 3
            )

        );


        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < fragmentCount;
            i++
        ) {

            const angle =
                random() * Math.PI * 2;

            const distance =
                parent.scale *
                (1.0 + random() * 2.0) *
                config.fragmentation;

            const x =
                parent.position.x +
                Math.cos(angle) * distance;

            const z =
                parent.position.z +
                Math.sin(angle) * distance;

            const position =
                this.terrain.sampleAt(x, z);

            if (!position) continue;


            const fragmentScale =
                parent.scale *
                (0.25 + random() * 0.18);


            const fragmentSeed =
                (
                    parent.seed ^
                    Math.imul(i + 1, 2654435761) ^
                    parent.erosionEvents
                ) >>> 0;


            this.createFormation(

                parent.speciesName,

                position,

                fragmentScale,

                fragmentSeed,

                parent.fragmentDepth + 1

            );

        }


        // Fine sediment is gradually incorporated
        // into the surrounding ground.
        if (
            parent.fragmentDepth >=
                this.maxFragmentDepth ||
            parent.scale < this.minFragmentScale * 2
        ) {

            parent.alive = false;

        }

    }


    advance(deltaYears) {

        let changed = false;

        // Only process formations that existed
        // at the beginning of this simulation step.
        const initialCount =
            this.formations.length;

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < initialCount;
            i++
        ) {

            const formation =
                this.formations[i];

            if (!formation.alive) continue;

            if (
                this.erodeFormation(
                    formation,
                    deltaYears
                )
            ) {

                changed = true;

            }

        }


        if (changed) {

            this.renderDirty = true;

        }


        this.simulationSteps++;

        if (
            this.renderDirty ||
            this.simulationSteps >=
                this.renderEverySteps
        ) {

            this.rebuildRendering();

            this.simulationSteps = 0;

        }

    }


    // ---------------------------------
    // RENDERING
    // ---------------------------------

    rebuildRendering() {

        for (const speciesName in this.renderers) {

            this.renderers[speciesName].clear();

        }


        for (const formation of this.formations) {

            if (!formation.alive) continue;

            const config =
                RockSpecies[formation.speciesName];

            const generator =
                new RockGenerator(
                    config,
                    formation.seed
                );

            const matrices = generator.generate(

                formation.position,

                formation.scale,

                formation.fragmentDepth === 0
                    ? config.recursionDepth
                    : Math.max(
                        0,
                        config.recursionDepth -
                        formation.fragmentDepth
                    )

            );


            this.renderers[
                formation.speciesName
            ].addMatrices(matrices);

        }


        for (const speciesName in this.renderers) {

            this.renderers[speciesName].update();

        }

        this.renderDirty = false;

    }


    getLivingFormationCount() {

        return this.formations.reduce(

            (count, formation) =>

                count + (formation.alive ? 1 : 0),

            0

        );

    }

	getMaterialAt(position) {

    return this.field.sample(
        position.x,
        position.z
    );

}


getSoilQualityAt(position) {

    const sample = this.getMaterialAt(position);

    if (!sample) return 0.5;

    const quality = {

        fertileSoil: 1.0,

        clay: 0.55,

        limestone: 0.45,

        granite: 0.20,

        sand: 0.12,

        anomaly: 0.65

    };

    return quality[sample.speciesName] ?? 0.5;

}

}
export { GeologicalManager }


========================================================================
FILE: src/geology/rock-generator.js
========================================================================


import { CreateSeededRandom } from "../core/random.js";

class RecursiveObjectGenerator {
    constructor(config) {
        this.config = config;
        this.matrices = [];
    }

    generate(position, scale, depth) {
        this.matrices = [];

        this.generateRecursive(
            position,
            scale,
            depth,
            0
        );

        return this.matrices;
    }

    generateRecursive() {
        throw new Error(
            "generateRecursive must be implemented"
        );
    }
}

class RockGenerator extends RecursiveObjectGenerator {
    constructor(config, seed = 1) {
        super(config);
        this.random = CreateSeededRandom(seed);
    }

    generateRecursive(
        position,
        scale,
        maxDepth,
        currentDepth
    ) {
        const c = this.config;
        const random = this.random;

        const irregularity = c.irregularity ?? 0.5;

        // Angular, asymmetric rock body.
        const sx = scale * (
            0.75 + random() * irregularity
        );

        const sy = scale * (
            0.65 + random() * irregularity
        ) * (c.verticalScale ?? 1);

        const sz = scale * (
            0.75 + random() * irregularity
        );

        // Fractured formations tend to have tilted faces.
        const rotation =
            BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationYawPitchRoll(
                random() * Math.PI * 2,
                (random() - 0.5) * 0.8,
                (random() - 0.5) * 0.8
            );

        const buriedFraction = currentDepth === 0
            ? 0.30
            : 0.05;

        const center = position.add(
            new BABYLON.Vector3(
                0,
                sy * (0.5 - buriedFraction),
                0
            )
        );

        this.matrices.push(
            BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
                new BABYLON.Vector3(sx, sy, sz),
                rotation,
                center
            )
        );

        if (currentDepth >= maxDepth) {
            return;
        }

        const count = Math.max(
            2,
            Math.round(
                c.branchCount *
                (1 - currentDepth * 0.10)
            )
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const phi = random() * Math.PI * 2;

            // Bias protrusions toward the upper surface.
            const vertical = random() * 1.4 - 0.15;

            const radial = Math.sqrt(
                Math.max(0, 1 - vertical * vertical)
            );

            const direction = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                Math.cos(phi) * radial,
                vertical,
                Math.sin(phi) * radial
            ).normalize();

            const displacement =
                (c.displacement ?? 0.8) *
                (0.65 + random() * 0.55);

            const childPosition = position.add(
                direction.scale(scale * displacement)
            );

            const childScale = scale *
                (c.scaleDecay ?? 0.6) *
                (0.75 + random() * 0.35);

            this.generateRecursive(
                childPosition,
                childScale,
                maxDepth,
                currentDepth + 1
            );
        }
    }
}

export {
    RockGenerator,
    RecursiveObjectGenerator
};

========================================================================
FILE: src/geology/surface-deposit-system.js
