import { SurfaceDepositRenderer } from "../rendering/surface-deposit-renderer.js"
import { CreateSeededRandom } from "../core/random.js";

class SurfaceDepositSystem {

    constructor(scene, terrain, geologicalField) {

        this.scene = scene;

        this.terrain = terrain;

        this.field = geologicalField;

        this.spacing = 28;

        this.seed = 48391;

        this.renderers = {

            sand: new SurfaceDepositRenderer(
                scene,
                "sand",
                new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.76,
                    0.65,
                    0.43
                )
            ),

            clay: new SurfaceDepositRenderer(
                scene,
                "clay",
                new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.48,
                    0.25,
                    0.17
                )
            ),

            fertileSoil: new SurfaceDepositRenderer(
                scene,
                "fertileSoil",
                new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.22,
                    0.16,
                    0.10
                )
            )

        };

        this.generated = false;

    }


    generate() {

        if (this.generated) return;

        for (const speciesName in this.renderers) {

            this.renderers[speciesName].clear();

        }


        const minX = this.terrain.min.x;
        const maxX = this.terrain.max.x;

        const minZ = this.terrain.min.z;
        const maxZ = this.terrain.max.z;


        const startX =
            Math.floor(minX / this.spacing) *
            this.spacing;

        const startZ =
            Math.floor(minZ / this.spacing) *
            this.spacing;


        for (
            let x = startX;
            x <= maxX;
            x += this.spacing
        ) {

            for (
                let z = startZ;
                z <= maxZ;
                z += this.spacing
            ) {

                const cellX = Math.floor(
                    x / this.spacing
                );

                const cellZ = Math.floor(
                    z / this.spacing
                );

                const seed = (

                    this.seed ^

                    Math.imul(cellX, 73856093) ^

                    Math.imul(cellZ, 19349663)

                ) >>> 0;

                const random =
                    CreateSeededRandom(seed);


                // Small deterministic displacement.
                const sampleX =
                    x + (random() - 0.5) *
                    this.spacing * 0.5;

                const sampleZ =
                    z + (random() - 0.5) *
                    this.spacing * 0.5;


                const sample = this.field.sample(
                    sampleX,
                    sampleZ
                );

                if (!sample) continue;


                const speciesName =
                    sample.speciesName;

const renderer =
    this.renderers[speciesName];

if (!renderer) continue;

// Surface deposits should not cover steep terrain.
if (sample.slopeDegrees > 12) {
    continue;
}

// Avoid large deposits at high elevations.
if (
    sample.elevation > 0.72 &&
    speciesName !== "clay"
) {
    continue;
}

const baseRadius =
    this.spacing *
    (0.85 + random() * 0.45);

                // Slightly flatten and vary each deposit.
                const radiusX =
                    baseRadius *
                    (0.85 + random() * 0.35);

                const radiusZ =
                    baseRadius *
                    (0.85 + random() * 0.35);

                const rotation =
                    random() * Math.PI * 2;


                renderer.addPatch(

                    sample.position,

                    radiusX,

                    radiusZ,

                    rotation,

                    0.025 + random() * 0.025,
			sample.normal

                );


                // Secondary overlapping patches
                // create less regular boundaries.
                if (random() > 0.35) {

                    const offsetX =
                        (random() - 0.5) *
                        this.spacing * 0.65;

                    const offsetZ =
                        (random() - 0.5) *
                        this.spacing * 0.65;

                    const secondaryPosition =
                        this.terrain.sampleAt(
                            sampleX + offsetX,
                            sampleZ + offsetZ
                        );

			const secondaryProfile =
    this.terrain.sampleProfileAt(
        sampleX + offsetX,
        sampleZ + offsetZ
    );

if (
    secondaryProfile &&
    secondaryProfile.slopeDegrees < 12
) {

    renderer.addPatch(
        secondaryProfile.position,
        radiusX * 0.5,
        radiusZ * 0.5,
        random() * Math.PI * 2,
        0.02,
        secondaryProfile.normal
    );

}

                }

            }

        }


        this.update();

        this.generated = true;

    }


    update() {

        for (const speciesName in this.renderers) {

            this.renderers[speciesName].update();

        }

    }


}
export { SurfaceDepositSystem }


========================================================================

import createScene from "./scene/create-scene.js";

async function main() {
    console.log("[Main] Initializing Somnium...");

    const canvas = document.getElementById("renderCanvas");

    if (!canvas) {
        throw new Error("Render canvas not found.");
    }

    const engine = new BABYLON.Engine(
        canvas,
        true,
        {
            useLargeWorldRendering: true
        }
    );

    let scene = null;

    try {
        console.log("[Main] Engine initialized.");

        scene = await createScene(engine, canvas);

        console.log("[Main] Scene initialized.");

        engine.runRenderLoop(() => {
            if (scene && !scene.isDisposed) {
                scene.render();
            }
        });

        const handleResize = () => {
            engine.resize();
        };

        window.addEventListener("resize", handleResize);

        window.addEventListener("beforeunload", () => {
            window.removeEventListener("resize", handleResize);

            scene?.dispose();
            engine.dispose();
        }, { once: true });

    } catch (error) {
        scene?.dispose();
        engine.dispose();
        throw error;
    }
}

main().catch(error => {
    console.error(
        "[Main] Application initialization failed:",
        error
    );
});


========================================================================
FILE: src/rendering/adaptive-graphics-controller.js
========================================================================


export class AdaptiveGraphicsController {
    constructor(engine, camera, world, options = {}) {
        this.engine = engine;
        this.camera = camera;
        this.world = world;

        this.targetFPS = options.targetFPS ?? 55;
        this.minimumFPS = options.minimumFPS ?? 35;

        this.profiles = {
            low: {
                radius: 7,
                detailRadius: 1,
                generationBudget: 3,
                hardwareScale: 1.5
            },

            medium: {
                radius: 14,
                detailRadius: 2,
                generationBudget: 6,
                hardwareScale: 1.0
            },

            high: {
                radius: 18,
                detailRadius: 2,
                generationBudget: 8,
                hardwareScale: 0.85
            }
        };

        this.profileName = this.detectInitialProfile();

        this.fpsHistory = [];
        this.historyLimit = 30;

        this.elapsed = 0;
        this.lowFPSDuration = 0;
        this.highFPSDuration = 0;

        this.setProfile(this.profileName);

        console.log(
            "[Graphics] Initialized:",
            this.getStats()
        );
    }

    detectInitialProfile() {
        const cores = navigator.hardwareConcurrency || 4;
        const memory = navigator.deviceMemory || 4;

        const mobile =
            /Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod/i.test(
                navigator.userAgent
            );

        if (cores <= 2 || memory <= 2) {
            return "low";
        }

        if (
            cores >= 8 &&
            memory >= 8 &&
            !mobile
        ) {
            return "high";
        }

        return "medium";
    }

    setProfile(name) {
        const profile = this.profiles[name];

        if (!profile) return;

        this.profileName = name;

        this.world.renderRadius = profile.radius;

        this.world.highDetailRadius =
            profile.detailRadius;

        this.world.maxChunksPerUpdate =
            profile.generationBudget;

        this.camera.maxZ =
            this.world.chunkSize *
            (profile.radius + 3) * 2;

        this.engine.setHardwareScalingLevel(
            profile.hardwareScale
        );

        console.log("[Graphics] Profile:", name);
    }

    getAverageFPS() {
        if (!this.fpsHistory.length) {
            return this.engine.getFps();
        }

        return this.fpsHistory.reduce(
            (sum, fps) => sum + fps,
            0
        ) / this.fpsHistory.length;
    }

    update(deltaSeconds) {
        this.elapsed += deltaSeconds;

        if (this.elapsed < 2) return;

        const elapsed = this.elapsed;
        this.elapsed = 0;

        const fps = this.engine.getFps();

        if (Number.isFinite(fps) && fps > 0) {
            this.fpsHistory.push(fps);

            if (this.fpsHistory.length > this.historyLimit) {
                this.fpsHistory.shift();
            }
        }

        const average = this.getAverageFPS();

        if (average < this.minimumFPS) {
            this.lowFPSDuration += elapsed;
            this.highFPSDuration = 0;
        } else if (average > this.targetFPS) {
            this.highFPSDuration += elapsed;
            this.lowFPSDuration = 0;
        } else {
            this.lowFPSDuration = Math.max(
                0,
                this.lowFPSDuration - elapsed
            );

            this.highFPSDuration = 0;
        }

        if (this.lowFPSDuration >= 6) {
            if (this.profileName === "high") {
                this.setProfile("medium");
            } else if (this.profileName === "medium") {
                this.setProfile("low");
            }

            this.lowFPSDuration = 0;
            this.highFPSDuration = 0;
        }

        if (this.highFPSDuration >= 25) {
            if (this.profileName === "low") {
                this.setProfile("medium");
            } else if (this.profileName === "medium") {
                this.setProfile("high");
            }

            this.highFPSDuration = 0;
            this.lowFPSDuration = 0;
        }
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            profile: this.profileName,
            renderRadius: this.world.renderRadius,
            averageFPS: Math.round(this.getAverageFPS()),
            targetFPS: this.targetFPS,
            minimumFPS: this.minimumFPS
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        this.fpsHistory.length = 0;
    }
}

FILE: src/rendering/cloud-renderer.js

import { ThinInstanceRenderer } from "./thin-instance-renderer.js";

class CloudRenderer extends ThinInstanceRenderer {
    constructor(scene) {
        const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
            "cloudPrototype",
            {
                diameter: 1,
                segments: 10
            },
            scene
        );

        const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "cloudMaterial",
            scene
        );

        material.diffuseColor =
            BABYLON.Color3.White();

        material.emissiveColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(
                0.035,
                0.035,
                0.045
            );

        material.specularColor =
            BABYLON.Color3.Black();

        material.useVertexColor = true;
        material.useVertexAlpha = false;

        material.backFaceCulling = false;

        mesh.material = material;
        mesh.isPickable = false;

        super(scene, "clouds", mesh, null);

        this.instanceColors = [];

        this.cloudCount = 0;
    }

    clear() {
        super.clear();

        this.instanceColors.length = 0;
        this.cloudCount = 0;
    }

    getCloudColor(
        speciesName,
        moistureRatio = 0,
        relativeHeight = 0.5
    ) {
        const moisture = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            moistureRatio,
            0,
            1
        );

        let color;

        switch (speciesName) {
            case "cumulonimbus":
                color = BABYLON.Color3.Lerp(
                    new BABYLON.Color3(
                        0.24, 0.29, 0.39
                    ),
                    new BABYLON.Color3(
                        0.89, 0.92, 0.98
                    ),
                    BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                        relativeHeight,
                        0,
                        1
                    )
                );
                break;

            case "towering":
                color = new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.82, 0.87, 0.95
                );
                break;

            default:
                color = new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.98, 0.99, 1.0
                );
                break;
        }

        // Saturated clouds become darker.
        const brightness =
            1.0 - moisture * 0.30;

        color.scaleInPlace(brightness);

        return color;
    }

    addMatrix(
        matrix,
        speciesName = "cumulus",
        moistureRatio = 0,
        relativeHeight = 0.5
    ) {
        super.addMatrix(matrix);

        const color = this.getCloudColor(
            speciesName,
            moistureRatio,
            relativeHeight
        );

        this.instanceColors.push(
            color.r,
            color.g,
            color.b,
            1.0
        );

        this.cloudCount++;
    }

    addCloud(
        matrices,
        moistureRatio = 0,
        speciesName = "cumulus",
        cloudPosition = null
    ) {
        for (const matrix of matrices) {
            const position = matrix.getTranslation();

            const relativeHeight = cloudPosition
                ? BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                    (position.y - cloudPosition.y) /
                    Math.max(1, 1500),
                    0,
                    1
                )
                : 0.5;

            this.addMatrix(
                matrix,
                speciesName,
                moistureRatio,
                relativeHeight
            );
        }
    }

    addMatrices(matrices) {
        this.addCloud(matrices);
    }

    update() {
        // Transform and upload instance matrices.
        super.update();

        const expectedColorLength =
            this.matrices.length * 4;

        if (this.instanceColors.length !== expectedColorLength) {
            console.warn(
                "[CloudRenderer] Instance color buffer mismatch:",
                {
                    matrices: this.matrices.length,
                    colors: this.instanceColors.length / 4
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        const colors = new Float32Array(
            this.instanceColors
        );

        this.mesh.thinInstanceSetBuffer(
            "color",
            colors,
            4,
            true
        );

        this.mesh.thinInstanceRefreshBoundingInfo(true);
    }
}

export { CloudRenderer };

import { DirectionRotation } from "../ecology/tree-generator.js";

class SurfaceDepositRenderer {

    constructor(scene, speciesName, color) {

        this.scene = scene;

        this.speciesName = speciesName;

        this.mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateCylinder(
            "surface_" + speciesName,
            {
                height: 1,
                diameter: 2,
                tessellation: 16
            },
            scene
        );

        this.mesh.position.y = -10000;

        this.mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);

        this.inverseMeshWorld =
            this.mesh.getWorldMatrix().clone();

        this.inverseMeshWorld.invert();


        const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "surfaceMaterial_" + speciesName,
            scene
        );

        material.diffuseColor = color.clone();

        material.specularColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0, 0, 0);

        material.roughness = 1;

        this.mesh.material = material;

        this.mesh.receiveShadows = true;

        this.matrices = [];

        this.buffer = null;

    }


    clear() {

        this.matrices.length = 0;

    }


addPatch(
    position,
    radiusX,
    radiusZ,
    rotation = 0,
    thickness = 0.04,
    normal = BABYLON.Axis.Y
) {
        const scale = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            radiusX,
            thickness,
            radiusZ
        );

const up = normal.normalize();

const yaw = BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationAxis(
    up,
    rotation
);

	const align = DirectionRotation(normal);


const quaternion = align.multiply(yaw);

	const center = position.add(
		normal.normalize().scale(thickness * 0.5)
	);

        const matrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
            scale,
            quaternion,
            center
        );

        this.matrices.push(matrix);

    }


    update() {

        const requiredSize =
            this.matrices.length * 16;

        if (
            !this.buffer ||
            this.buffer.length < requiredSize
        ) {

            this.buffer = new Float32Array(
                Math.max(
                    requiredSize,
                    this.buffer
                        ? this.buffer.length * 2
                        : 1024
                )
            );

        }


        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < this.matrices.length;
            i++
        ) {

            const localMatrix =
                this.matrices[i].multiply(
                    this.inverseMeshWorld
                );

            localMatrix.copyToArray(
                this.buffer,
                i * 16
            );

        }


        this.mesh.thinInstanceSetBuffer(
            "matrix",
            this.buffer.subarray(
                0,
                requiredSize
            ),
            16,
            true
        );

        this.mesh.thinInstanceRefreshBoundingInfo(true);

    }

}
export { SurfaceDepositRenderer }


========================================================================
FILE: src/rendering/thin-instance-renderer.js
========================================================================


class ThinInstanceRenderer {
    constructor(scene, name, mesh, shadowGenerator) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.mesh = mesh;
        this.matrices = [];
        this.buffer = null;

        this.localMatrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Identity();

        this.mesh.position.y = -10000;
        this.mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);

        this.inverseMeshWorld =
            this.mesh.getWorldMatrix().clone();

        this.inverseMeshWorld.invert();

        this.mesh.isPickable = false;

        if (shadowGenerator) {
            shadowGenerator.addShadowCaster(
                this.mesh,
                true
            );
        }
    }

    clear() {
        this.matrices.length = 0;
    }

    addMatrix(matrix) {
        this.matrices.push(matrix);
    }

    addMatrices(matrices) {
        this.matrices.push(...matrices);
    }

    add(
        position,
        scale,
        rotation = BABYLON.Quaternion.Identity()
    ) {
        const matrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
            new BABYLON.Vector3(scale, scale, scale),
            rotation,
            position
        );

        this.matrices.push(matrix);
    }

    update() {
        const geometryRenderer =
            this.scene.metadata?.geometryRenderer;

        const capacity = this.matrices.length * 16;

        // Retain buffer capacity between updates.
        if (
            !this.buffer ||
            this.buffer.length < capacity
        ) {
            this.buffer = new Float32Array(
                Math.max(1024, capacity)
            );
        }

        let writeIndex = 0;

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < this.matrices.length;
            i++
        ) {
            const sourceMatrix = this.matrices[i];

            if (!sourceMatrix || !sourceMatrix.m) {
                continue;
            }

            // Validate directly without allocating an array.
            const values = sourceMatrix.m;
            let valid = true;

            for (let j = 0; j < 16; j++) {
                if (!Number.isFinite(values[j])) {
                    valid = false;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (!valid) continue;

            const transformedMatrix =
                geometryRenderer
                    ? geometryRenderer.transformInstanceMatrix(
                        sourceMatrix
                    )
                    : sourceMatrix;

            if (!transformedMatrix) continue;

            transformedMatrix.multiplyToRef(
                this.inverseMeshWorld,
                this.localMatrix
            );

            this.localMatrix.copyToArray(
                this.buffer,
                writeIndex * 16
            );

            writeIndex++;
        }

        const requiredSize = writeIndex * 16;

        this.mesh.thinInstanceSetBuffer(
            "matrix",
            this.buffer.subarray(0, requiredSize),
            16,
            true
        );

        this.mesh.thinInstanceRefreshBoundingInfo(true);
    }
}

export { ThinInstanceRenderer };

import { HeightmapTerrain } from "../environment/heightmap-terrain.js";
import { WorldManager } from "../world/world-manager.js";
import { ChunkEcologySystem } from "../world/chunk-ecology-system.js";
import { ChunkGeologySystem } from "../world/chunk-geology-system.js";
import { AdaptiveGraphicsController } from "../rendering/adaptive-graphics-controller.js";
import { WaterSystem } from "../water/water-system.js";
import { Hydrology } from "../water/hydrology.js";
import { RainSystem } from "../water/rain-system.js";
import { ChunkCloudSystem } from "../world/chunk-cloud-system.js";

import { SpatialTransform } from "../world/fields/geometry/spatial-transform.js";
import { GeometryRenderer } from "../world/fields/geometry/geometry-renderer.js";
import { GeometryShaderManager } from "../world/fields/geometry/geometry-shader-manager.js";
import { HyperbolicCameraController } from "../world/fields/geometry/hyperbolic-camera-controller.js";

import { GameManager } from "../game/game-manager.js";
import { PlayerCharacter } from "../game/player-character.js";
import { PlayerController } from "../game/player-controller.js";
import { CharacterCameraController } from "../game/camera-controller.js";
import { MainMenu } from "../ui/main-menu.js";
import { CelestialSystem } from "../environment/celestial-system.js";
import { WorldPersistence } from "../world/persistence/world-persistence.js";
import { WorldSession } from "../world/persistence/world-session.js";
import { WorldLibraryUI } from "../world/persistence/world-library-ui.js";
import { BuildingSystem } from "../buildings/building-system.js";
import { BuildingEditor } from "../editor/building-editor.js";
import { DnDMode } from "../game/dnd-mode.js";

async function createScene(engine, canvas) {
    console.log("[Scene] Initializing Somnium...");

    const scene = new BABYLON.Scene(engine);

	scene.clearColor = new BABYLON.Color4(
		0.003, 0.006, 0.022, 1
	);

    const WORLD_SIZE = 40000;
    const CHUNK_SIZE = 2000;

    // -----------------------------------------
    // LIGHTING
    // -----------------------------------------


	// -----------------------------------------
// CELESTIAL LIGHTING
// -----------------------------------------

const sunDirection = new BABYLON.Vector3(
    -0.5, -1, -0.3
).normalize();

const sun = new BABYLON.DirectionalLight(
    "sun",
    sunDirection.clone(),
    scene
);

const ambient = new BABYLON.HemisphericLight(
    "ambient",
    new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 1, 0),
    scene
);

const celestial = new CelestialSystem(
    scene,
    {
        sunLight: sun,
        ambientLight: ambient,
        dayLength: 900,
        orbitDistance: 45000,
        skyRadius: 90000,
        sunSize: 1800,
        moonSize: 1300
    }
);

scene.metadata = {
    ...(scene.metadata || {}),
    celestial
};
    // -----------------------------------------
    // HEIGHTMAP
    // -----------------------------------------

    const terrain = new HeightmapTerrain({
        url: "./assets/0_somnium_map_topographic.png",
        worldSize: WORLD_SIZE,
        minHeight: -2000,
        maxHeight: 1000,
        heightChannel: "average"
    });

    await terrain.load();

    // -----------------------------------------
    // SPATIAL TRANSFORM
    // -----------------------------------------

    const spatialTransform = new SpatialTransform({
        geometry: "poincare",
        renderScale: 10000,

        bounds: {
            minX: terrain.min.x,
            maxX: terrain.max.x,
            minY: -WORLD_SIZE / 2,
            maxY: WORLD_SIZE / 2,
            minZ: terrain.min.z,
            maxZ: terrain.max.z,
            characteristicLength: 0.25
        }
    });

    // -----------------------------------------
    // GEOMETRY RENDERER
    // -----------------------------------------

    const geometryRenderer = new GeometryRenderer(
        scene,
        spatialTransform
    );

const buildingSystem = new BuildingSystem(
    scene,
    geometryRenderer,
    { terrain }
);

const buildingEditor = new BuildingEditor(
    scene,
    buildingSystem,
    spatialTransform
);

scene.metadata = {
    ...(scene.metadata || {}),
    buildingSystem,
    buildingEditor
};

    const geometryShaderManager =
        new GeometryShaderManager(
            scene,
            spatialTransform,
            {
                enabled: true,
                renderScale: spatialTransform.renderScale
            }
        );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // FLIGHT CAMERA
    // -----------------------------------------

    const camera = new BABYLON.FreeCamera(
        "FlightCamera",
        BABYLON.Vector3.Zero(),
        scene
    );

    camera.minZ = 1;
    camera.maxZ = 100000;
    camera.speed = 500;
    camera.angularSensibility = 3000;

    const initialCameraPosition =
        spatialTransform.worldToRender({
            x: 0,
            y: 1800,
            z: -3000
        });

    camera.position.set(
        initialCameraPosition.x,
        initialCameraPosition.y,
        initialCameraPosition.z
    );

    camera.setTarget(BABYLON.Vector3.Zero());

    // -----------------------------------------
    // HYPERBOLIC FLIGHT CAMERA
    // -----------------------------------------

    const hyperbolicCamera =
        new HyperbolicCameraController(
            camera,
            spatialTransform,
            {
                maxHyperbolicSpeed: 2.5,
                boundaryMargin: 1e-7
            }
        );

    // Synchronize after initial camera positioning.
    hyperbolicCamera.synchronize();

    // -----------------------------------------
    // THIRD-PERSON CAMERA
    // -----------------------------------------

    const thirdPersonCamera = new BABYLON.FreeCamera(
        "ThirdPersonCamera",
        BABYLON.Vector3.Zero(),
        scene
    );

    thirdPersonCamera.minZ = 0.5;
    thirdPersonCamera.maxZ = 100000;

    // -----------------------------------------
    // PLAYER CHARACTER
    // -----------------------------------------

    const playerCharacter = new PlayerCharacter(
        scene,
        spatialTransform,
        {
            x: 0,
            z: 0,
            terrain
        }
    );

    const playerController = new PlayerController(
        playerCharacter,
        terrain,
        canvas,
        {
            walkSpeed: 180,
            runSpeed: 360,
            turnSpeed: 2.2
        }
    );

	const characterCameraController =
    new CharacterCameraController(
        scene,
        thirdPersonCamera,
        playerCharacter,
        spatialTransform,
        {
            distance: 24,
            height: 8,

            minDistance: 6,
            maxDistance: 120,

            zoomSensitivity: 0.001,
            smoothing: 8
        }
    );
    // -----------------------------------------
    // GAME MANAGER
    // -----------------------------------------

    const gameManager = new GameManager(
        scene,
        {
            canvas,
            flightCamera: camera
        }
    );

	buildingEditor.setGameManager(gameManager);

    gameManager.registerThirdPerson(
        thirdPersonCamera,
        playerController,
        characterCameraController
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // MAIN MENU
    // -----------------------------------------
let dndMode = null;

const mainMenu = new MainMenu(
    gameManager,
    () => {
        if (dndMode) {
            dndMode.dispose();
        }

        gameManager.startDnD();

        dndMode = new DnDMode(
            scene,
            terrain,
            spatialTransform
        );
    }
);

scene.metadata = {
    ...(scene.metadata || {}),
    celestial,
    spatialTransform,
    geometryRenderer,
    geometryShaderManager,
    hyperbolicCamera,
    gameManager,
    mainMenu,
    playerCharacter,
    playerController,
    characterCameraController
};
    // -----------------------------------------
    // HEIGHTMAP DIAGNOSTICS
    // -----------------------------------------

    const heightmapDiagnostics =
        terrain.getDiagnostics(64);

    console.log(
        "[Scene] Heightmap diagnostics:",
        heightmapDiagnostics
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TERRAIN STREAMING
    // -----------------------------------------

    const world = new WorldManager(
        scene,
        terrain,
        {
            chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
            renderRadius: 3,
            highDetailRadius: 2,
            maxChunksPerUpdate: 4
        }
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // ADAPTIVE GRAPHICS
    // -----------------------------------------

    const graphics = new AdaptiveGraphicsController(
        engine,
        camera,
        world,
        {
            targetFPS: 55,
            minimumFPS: 35
        }
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CHUNK ECOLOGY
    // -----------------------------------------

    const ecology = new ChunkEcologySystem(
        scene,
        terrain,
        {
            worldSeed: 91827,
            chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
            renderRadius: 3,
            treesPerChunk: 60,
            seaLevel: -100,
            sunDirection
        }
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CHUNK GEOLOGY
    // -----------------------------------------

    const geology = new ChunkGeologySystem(
        scene,
        terrain,
        {
            worldSeed: 91827,
            chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
            renderRadius: 3,
            rocksPerChunk: 10,
            seaLevel: -100
        }
    );

    console.log("[Scene] Chunk geology connected.");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // WATER
    // -----------------------------------------

    const water = new WaterSystem(
        scene,
        terrain,
        -100,
        {
            chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
            renderRadius: 3
        }
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // HYDROLOGY
    // -----------------------------------------

    const hydrology = new Hydrology(
        terrain,
        water,
        20
    );
// -----------------------------------------
// SHARED GEOLOGICAL AND ECOLOGICAL FIELDS
// -----------------------------------------

// One authoritative geological field is shared
// by hydrology and the active chunk ecology.
hydrology.geologicalField = geology.field;

ecology.geologicalField = geology.field;
ecology.hydrology = hydrology;

console.log(
    "[Scene] Geological soil systems connected."
);

    // -----------------------------------------
    // RAIN
    // -----------------------------------------

    const rain = new RainSystem(scene);

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CLOUDS
    // -----------------------------------------

    const clouds = new ChunkCloudSystem(
        scene,
        terrain,
        water,
        hydrology,
        rain,
        {
            chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
            renderRadius: 4,
            cloudsPerChunk: 8,
            maxChunksPerUpdate: 2,
            worldSeed: 91827,
            renderInterval: 0.5,
            simulationInterval: 0.25
        }
    );

// -----------------------------------------
// PERSISTENT WORLD LIBRARY
// -----------------------------------------

const worldPersistence = new WorldPersistence();

await worldPersistence.open();

const worldSession = new WorldSession(ecology);

let activeWorld = null;
let simulationElapsed = 0;

const worldLibrary = new WorldLibraryUI(
    worldPersistence,
    {

onLoad: async world => {
    const result = worldSession.restore(world);

    buildingSystem.restoreWorld(world);

	
    // Reset stale editor references after rebuilding
    // the authoritative building registry.
    buildingEditor.resetAfterWorldLoad();

    activeWorld = world;
    simulationElapsed = result.elapsedTime;

    geology.worldSeed = result.worldSeed;
    clouds.worldSeed = result.worldSeed;
    geology.field.seed = result.worldSeed;

    geology.records.clear();
    geology.activeChunks.clear();

    for (const renderer of Object.values(geology.renderers)) {
        renderer.clear();
        renderer.update();
    }

    clouds.records.clear();
    clouds.activeChunks.clear();
    clouds.renderer.clear();
    clouds.renderer.update();

    ecology.renderDirty = true;

    console.log("[World] Loaded:", world.metadata.name, result);
},

onCapture: async world => {
    worldSession.capture(world, simulationElapsed);
    buildingSystem.captureWorld(world);
}


    }
);

scene.metadata = {
    ...(scene.metadata || {}),
    worldPersistence,
    worldSession,
    worldLibrary
};

    console.log("[Scene] Environmental systems connected.");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // UPDATE LOOP
    // -----------------------------------------

    let ecologyAccumulator = 0;

    scene.onBeforeRenderObservable.add(() => {
        const deltaSeconds = Math.min(
            engine.getDeltaTime() / 1000,
            0.1
        );

	if (activeWorld) {
		simulationElapsed += deltaSeconds;
	}
	
	celestial.update(deltaSeconds);

        graphics.update(deltaSeconds);

        let position;
        let forward;

        if (gameManager.mode === "flight") {
            // Only the flight camera receives hyperbolic
            // camera updates.
            hyperbolicCamera.update(deltaSeconds);

            position =
                hyperbolicCamera.getWorldPosition();

            forward =
                camera.getForwardRay().direction;

        } else if (gameManager.mode === "thirdPerson") {
            // Character movement operates in original
            // authoritative world coordinates.
            playerController.update(deltaSeconds);

            characterCameraController.update(
                deltaSeconds
            );

            position = {
                ...playerCharacter.worldPosition
            };

            const yaw = playerCharacter.yaw;

            forward = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                Math.sin(yaw),
                0,
                Math.cos(yaw)
            );

        } else {
            // Menu and pause: preserve the world at its
            // current camera position without advancing
            // either navigation controller.
            position = hyperbolicCamera.getWorldPosition();
            forward = camera.getForwardRay().direction;
        }

        // -------------------------------------
        // WORLD STREAMING
        // -------------------------------------

        world.update(position, forward);

        // -------------------------------------
        // GEOLOGY
        // -------------------------------------

        geology.update(position);

        // -------------------------------------
        // REGIONAL WATER
        // -------------------------------------

        water.update(position, world.renderRadius);

        // -------------------------------------
        // ATMOSPHERE
        // -------------------------------------

        clouds.update(position, deltaSeconds);

        // -------------------------------------
        // HYDROLOGY
        // -------------------------------------

        const deltaYears = deltaSeconds * 0.05;

        hydrology.update(deltaYears);
        rain.update();

        // -------------------------------------
        // ECOLOGY
        // -------------------------------------

        ecologyAccumulator += deltaSeconds;

        if (ecologyAccumulator >= 0.25) {
            const elapsed = ecologyAccumulator;
            ecologyAccumulator = 0;

            ecology.update(position, elapsed);
        }
    });

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DIAGNOSTICS
    // -----------------------------------------

    scene.metadata = {
        ...(scene.metadata || {}),

        worldSize: terrain.worldSize,

        spatialTransform,
        geometryRenderer,
        geometryShaderManager,

        getGeometryStats: () =>
            geometryRenderer.getStats(),

	getCelestialStats: () => celestial.getStats(),

        getWorldStats: () =>
            world.getStats(),

        getEcologyStats: () =>
            ecology.getStats(),

        getGeologyStats: () =>
            geology.getStats(),

        getGraphicsStats: () =>
            graphics.getStats(),

        getHeightmapStats: () =>
            terrain.getDiagnostics(64),

        getWaterStats: () =>
            water.getStats(),

        getCloudStats: () =>
            clouds.getStats(),

        getGeometryShaderStats: () =>
            geometryShaderManager.getStats(),

        getHydrologyStats: () =>
            hydrology.getStatistics(),

        getGameStats: () =>
            gameManager.getState()
    };

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CLEANUP
    // -----------------------------------------

    scene.onDisposeObservable.add(() => {
        console.log("[Scene] Disposing Somnium systems.");

        mainMenu.dispose();
        gameManager.dispose();

        playerController.dispose();
        characterCameraController.dispose();
        playerCharacter.dispose();

        geometryRenderer.dispose(world);

        graphics.dispose();
        ecology.dispose();
        geology.dispose();
        world.dispose();

        terrain.dispose();
        rain.dispose();
        water.dispose();
        clouds.dispose();

        geometryShaderManager.dispose();
        hyperbolicCamera.dispose();
	celestial.dispose();


	buildingEditor.dispose();
	buildingSystem.dispose();


	worldLibrary.dispose();
	worldPersistence.close();
    });

    // -----------------------------------------
    // INITIALIZATION COMPLETE
    // -----------------------------------------

    console.log("[Scene] Somnium initialized.");

    console.log("[Scene] Configuration:", {
        worldSize: WORLD_SIZE,
        chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
        chunksAcross: WORLD_SIZE / CHUNK_SIZE,
        initialPerspective: gameManager.mode,
        treesPerChunk: ecology.treesPerChunk,
        rocksPerChunk: geology.rocksPerChunk
    });

    return scene;
}

export default createScene;

class MainMenu {

        mode => this.update(mode)
    );

    this.update(gameManager.mode);

                            <strong>FLIGHT SIMULATOR</strong>
                            <small>Explore freely from above</small>
                        </button>

                        <button id="somnium-play-editor">

                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

            mode === "menu" ? "flex" : "none";

        this.pausePanel.style.display =
            mode === "paused" ? "flex" : "none";

        this.hud.style.display =
            mode === "flight" || mode === "thirdPerson"
                ? "flex"
                : "none";
    }

    dispose() {
        this.unsubscribe?.();
        this.container?.remove();
    }
}

========================================================================
FILE: src/water/hydrology.js

class Hydrology {

    constructor(terrain, waterSystem, cellSize = 20) {

        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.waterSystem = waterSystem;

        this.cellSize = cellSize;

        this.cells = new Map();

        this.defaultMoisture = 0.35;

        this.evaporationRate = 0.015;

        this.rainfallRetention = 1.0;
	this.geologicalField = null;

    }


    getCellCoordinates(x, z) {

        return {

            x: Math.floor(x / this.cellSize),

            z: Math.floor(z / this.cellSize)

        };

    }


    getCellKey(x, z) {

        return `${x},${z}`;

    }


	getCell(x, z) {
    const coordinates = this.getCellCoordinates(x, z);

    const key = this.getCellKey(
        coordinates.x,
        coordinates.z
    );

    if (!this.cells.has(key)) {
        const worldX = (coordinates.x + 0.5) * this.cellSize;
        const worldZ = (coordinates.z + 0.5) * this.cellSize;

        const soil = this.geologicalField
            ? this.geologicalField.sample(worldX, worldZ)
            : null;

        const capacity = soil
            ? 0.25 + soil.moistureCapacity * 0.75
            : 0.75;

        this.cells.set(key, {
            moisture: this.defaultMoisture * capacity,
            capacity,
            infiltration: soil?.infiltration ?? 0.65,
            drainage: soil?.drainage ?? 0.5,
            fertility: soil?.fertility ?? 0.5,
            soilType: soil?.surfaceSoil ?? "loam",
            rainfall: 0,
            evaporation: 0
        });
    }

    return this.cells.get(key);
}

sampleSoil(position) {
    if (!this.geologicalField || !position) {
        return null;
    }

    return this.geologicalField.sample(
        position.x,
        position.z
    );
}


sampleMoisture(position) {
    if (this.waterSystem.isUnderwater(position)) {
        return 1.0;
    }

    const cell = this.getCell(
        position.x,
        position.z
    );

    return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        cell.moisture / Math.max(cell.capacity, 0.001),
        0,
        1
    );
}


    addRain(position, radius, amount) {

        const minX = Math.floor(
            (position.x - radius) / this.cellSize
        );

        const maxX = Math.floor(
            (position.x + radius) / this.cellSize
        );

        const minZ = Math.floor(
            (position.z - radius) / this.cellSize
        );

        const maxZ = Math.floor(
            (position.z + radius) / this.cellSize
        );


        for (let x = minX; x <= maxX; x++) {

            for (let z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z++) {

                const worldX =
                    (x + 0.5) * this.cellSize;

                const worldZ =
                    (z + 0.5) * this.cellSize;

                const dx = worldX - position.x;
                const dz = worldZ - position.z;

                const distance = Math.sqrt(
                    dx * dx + dz * dz
                );

                if (distance > radius) continue;


                const terrainPosition =
                    this.terrain.sampleAt(
                        worldX,
                        worldZ
                    );

                if (!terrainPosition) continue;


                // Rain falling into the ocean
                // does not increase soil moisture.

                if (
                    this.waterSystem.isUnderwater(
                        terrainPosition
                    )
                ) {
                    continue;
                }


                const falloff =
                    1 - distance / radius;

                const cell = this.getCell(
                    worldX,
                    worldZ
                );

const depositedWater =
    amount *
    falloff *
    this.rainfallRetention *
    cell.infiltration;

const availableCapacity = Math.max(
    0,
    cell.capacity - cell.moisture
);

const storedWater = Math.min(
    depositedWater,
    availableCapacity
);

cell.moisture += storedWater;

// Water that cannot infiltrate or be stored
// becomes runoff rather than disappearing
// into the soil moisture reservoir.
const runoff = depositedWater - storedWater;

cell.rainfall += storedWater;
cell.runoff = (cell.runoff ?? 0) + runoff;
                cell.rainfall += depositedWater;

            }

        }

    }


update(deltaYears) {
    for (const cell of this.cells.values()) {
        const evaporation =
            this.evaporationRate *
            (0.35 + cell.drainage * 0.65) *
            deltaYears;

        const drainage =
            cell.drainage *
            0.025 *
            deltaYears;

        cell.moisture = Math.max(
            0,
            cell.moisture - evaporation - drainage
        );

        cell.evaporation += evaporation;

        cell.rainfall = Math.max(
            0,
            cell.rainfall - deltaYears * 0.1
        );

        cell.runoff = Math.max(
            0,
            (cell.runoff ?? 0) - deltaYears * 0.1
        );
    }
}

    getStatistics() {

        let totalMoisture = 0;

        let count = 0;

        for (const cell of this.cells.values()) {

            totalMoisture += cell.moisture;

            count++;

        }

        return {

            cells: count,

            averageMoisture:
                count > 0
                    ? totalMoisture / count
                    : this.defaultMoisture

        };

    }

}

export { Hydrology };

class RainSystem {

    constructor(scene) {

        this.scene = scene;

        this.active = false;

        this.particles = new BABYLON.ParticleSystem(
            "rainParticles",
            2000,
            scene
        );


        // Generate a tiny procedural particle texture.

        const texture = new BABYLON.DynamicTexture(
            "rainTexture",
            {
                width: 8,
                height: 16
            },
            scene,
            false
        );

        const context = texture.getContext();

        context.clearRect(0, 0, 8, 16);

        context.fillStyle = "#c5e5ff";

        context.fillRect(3, 0, 2, 16);

        texture.update();

        this.particles.particleTexture = texture;


        this.particles.emitter =
            BABYLON.Vector3.Zero();

        this.particles.minEmitBox =
            new BABYLON.Vector3(-25, 0, -25);

        this.particles.maxEmitBox =
            new BABYLON.Vector3(25, 0, 25);


        this.particles.color1 =
            new BABYLON.Color4(0.65, 0.8, 1, 0.65);

        this.particles.color2 =
            new BABYLON.Color4(0.8, 0.9, 1, 0.8);

        this.particles.colorDead =
            new BABYLON.Color4(0.5, 0.65, 0.8, 0);


        this.particles.minSize = 0.08;

        this.particles.maxSize = 0.14;

        this.particles.minLifeTime = 0.5;

        this.particles.maxLifeTime = 1.2;


        this.particles.emitRate = 1200;

        this.particles.minEmitPower = 20;

        this.particles.maxEmitPower = 35;

        this.particles.updateSpeed = 0.015;


        this.particles.direction1 =
            new BABYLON.Vector3(-1, -30, -1);

        this.particles.direction2 =
            new BABYLON.Vector3(1, -30, 1);


        this.particles.gravity =
            new BABYLON.Vector3(0, -9.81, 0);


        this.particles.blendMode =
            BABYLON.ParticleSystem.BLENDMODE_STANDARD;


        this.particles.minAngularSpeed = 0;

        this.particles.maxAngularSpeed = 0;

    }


    start(position, intensity = 1) {

        this.particles.emitter.copyFrom(position);

        this.particles.emitRate =
            1200 * BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                intensity,
                0.2,
                2
            );

        if (!this.active) {

            this.particles.start();

            this.active = true;

        }

    }


    stop() {

        if (!this.active) return;

        this.particles.stop();

        this.active = false;

    }


    update() {

        // Babylon.js updates the particle system
        // automatically during scene rendering.

    }


    dispose() {

        this.particles.dispose();

    }

}

export { RainSystem };


========================================================================
FILE: src/water/water-system.js
========================================================================


export class WaterSystem {
    constructor(scene, terrain, seaLevel = -100, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.seaLevel = seaLevel;

        this.chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 2000;
        this.renderRadius = options.renderRadius ?? 3;

        this.evaporationRate = 0.12;

        this.tiles = new Map();
        this.disposed = false;

        this.material = this.createWaterMaterial();

        console.log("[Water] Regional water system initialized.");
    }

    createWaterMaterial() {
        const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "regionalOceanMaterial",
            this.scene
        );

        material.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.025, 0.19, 0.30);

        material.specularColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.35, 0.45, 0.55);

        material.specularPower = 64;

        material.emissiveColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.005, 0.025, 0.04);

        material.backFaceCulling = false;

        return material;
    }

    getChunkKey(x, z) {
        return `${x},${z}`;
    }

    createOceanTile(chunkX, chunkZ) {
        const minX = Math.max(
            chunkX * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.min.x
        );

        const maxX = Math.min(
            (chunkX + 1) * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.max.x
        );

        const minZ = Math.max(
            chunkZ * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.min.z
        );

        const maxZ = Math.min(
            (chunkZ + 1) * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.max.z
        );

        const width = maxX - minX;
        const depth = maxZ - minZ;

        if (width <= 0 || depth <= 0) return null;

        const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateGround(
            `ocean_${chunkX}_${chunkZ}`,
            {
                width,
                height: depth,
                subdivisions: 1
            },
            this.scene
        );

        mesh.position.set(
            (minX + maxX) * 0.5,
            this.seaLevel,
            (minZ + maxZ) * 0.5
        );

        mesh.material = this.material;
        mesh.isPickable = false;
        mesh.receiveShadows = false;

        mesh.metadata = {
            waterType: "ocean",
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        };

        this.tiles.set(
            this.getChunkKey(chunkX, chunkZ),
            mesh
        );

        return mesh;
    }

    removeTile(key) {
        const mesh = this.tiles.get(key);

        if (!mesh) return;

        mesh.dispose();
        this.tiles.delete(key);
    }

    update(cameraPosition, renderRadius = this.renderRadius) {
        if (this.disposed || !cameraPosition) return;

        const centerX = Math.floor(
            cameraPosition.x / this.chunkSize
        );

        const centerZ = Math.floor(
            cameraPosition.z / this.chunkSize
        );

        const desired = new Set();

        for (
            let x = centerX - renderRadius;
            x <= centerX + renderRadius;
            x++
        ) {
            for (
                let z = centerZ - renderRadius;
                z <= centerZ + renderRadius;
                z++
            ) {
                const minX = x * this.chunkSize;
                const minZ = z * this.chunkSize;

                const maxX = minX + this.chunkSize;
                const maxZ = minZ + this.chunkSize;

                // Ignore chunks outside the terrain.
                if (
                    maxX <= this.terrain.min.x ||
                    minX >= this.terrain.max.x ||
                    maxZ <= this.terrain.min.z ||
                    minZ >= this.terrain.max.z
                ) {
                    continue;
                }

                const key = this.getChunkKey(x, z);

                desired.add(key);

                if (!this.tiles.has(key)) {
                    this.createOceanTile(x, z);
                }
            }
        }

        // Remove distant water meshes.
        for (const key of this.tiles.keys()) {
            if (!desired.has(key)) {
                this.removeTile(key);
            }
        }
    }

    isUnderwater(position) {
        if (!position) return false;

        return position.y < this.seaLevel;
    }

    getTerrainWaterDepth(position) {
        if (!position) return 0;

        return Math.max(
            0,
            this.seaLevel - position.y
        );
    }

    getEvaporationPotential(position) {
        const terrainPosition = this.terrain.sampleAt(
            position.x,
            position.z
        );

        if (!terrainPosition) return 0.2;

        const distanceToSeaLevel = Math.abs(
            terrainPosition.y - this.seaLevel
        );

        const coastalInfluence =
            1 - BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                distanceToSeaLevel / 300,
                0,
                1
            );

        const oceanInfluence =
            terrainPosition.y < this.seaLevel
                ? 1
                : coastalInfluence;

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            0.2 + oceanInfluence * 0.8,
            0.2,
            1
        );
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            activeOceanTiles: this.tiles.size,
            chunkSize: this.chunkSize,
            seaLevel: this.seaLevel,
            renderRadius: this.renderRadius
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        for (const key of Array.from(this.tiles.keys())) {
            this.removeTile(key);
        }

        this.material.dispose();
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/chunk-cloud-system.js
========================================================================

import { Cloud } from "../atmosphere/cloud.js";
import { CloudSpecies } from "../atmosphere/cloud-species.js";

export class ChunkCloudSystem {
    constructor(
        scene,
        terrain,
        waterSystem,
        hydrology,
        rainSystem,
        options = {}
    ) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.waterSystem = waterSystem;
        this.hydrology = hydrology;
        this.rainSystem = rainSystem;

	this.windTime = 0;
this.windUpdateInterval = 2.0;
this.windAccumulator = 0;

        // Chunk configuration.
        this.chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 2000;
        this.renderRadius = options.renderRadius ?? 4;
        this.cloudsPerChunk = options.cloudsPerChunk ?? 8;
        this.maxChunksPerUpdate = options.maxChunksPerUpdate ?? 2;
        this.worldSeed = options.worldSeed ?? 91827;

        // Update intervals.
        this.renderInterval = options.renderInterval ?? 0.5;
        this.simulationInterval = options.simulationInterval ?? 0.25;

        // Storage.
        this.records = new Map();
        this.activeChunks = new Set();

        // Rendering.
        this.renderer = new CloudRenderer(scene);

        // Timing.
        this.renderAccumulator = 0;
        this.simulationAccumulator = 0;

        // Diagnostics.
        this.visibleCloudCount = 0;
        this.totalGeneratedClouds = 0;
        this.totalGeneratedStorms = 0;

        // State.
        this.renderDirty = true;
        this.disposed = false;
        this.rainingCloud = null;

        console.log(
            "[ChunkCloudSystem] Initialized with cumulonimbus support."
        );
    }

    getChunkCoordinates(position) {
        return {
            x: Math.floor(position.x / this.chunkSize),
            z: Math.floor(position.z / this.chunkSize)
        };
    }

    getChunkKey(x, z) {
        return `${x},${z}`;
    }

    getChunkSeed(x, z) {
        return (
            this.worldSeed ^
            Math.imul(x, 73856093) ^
            Math.imul(z, 19349663)
        ) >>> 0;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CLOUD SPECIES DISTRIBUTION
    // ------------------------------------------------

selectSpecies(random) {
    const value = random();

    if (value < 0.12 && CloudSpecies.cumulonimbus) {
        return "cumulonimbus";
    }

    if (value < 0.32 && CloudSpecies.towering) {
        return "towering";
    }

    if (CloudSpecies.cumulus) {
        return "cumulus";
    }

    // Fall back to whichever species exists.
    const availableSpecies =
        Object.keys(CloudSpecies);

    if (availableSpecies.length === 0) {
        throw new Error(
            "[ChunkCloudSystem] No cloud species configured."
        );
    }

    return availableSpecies[0];
}







    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CHUNK GENERATION
    // ------------------------------------------------

    generateChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {
        const key = this.getChunkKey(
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        );

        if (this.records.has(key)) {
            return this.records.get(key);
        }

        const seed = this.getChunkSeed(
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        );

        const random = CreateSeededRandom(seed);

        const minX = Math.max(
            chunkX * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.min.x
        );

        const maxX = Math.min(
            (chunkX + 1) * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.max.x
        );

        const minZ = Math.max(
            chunkZ * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.min.z
        );

        const maxZ = Math.min(
            (chunkZ + 1) * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.max.z
        );

        const clouds = [];

        if (minX >= maxX || minZ >= maxZ) {
            const record = {
                key,
                chunkX,
                chunkZ,
                seed,
                clouds
            };

            this.records.set(key, record);

            return record;
        }

        let attempts = 0;

        const attemptsLimit =
            this.cloudsPerChunk * 6;

        while (
            clouds.length < this.cloudsPerChunk &&
            attempts < attemptsLimit
        ) {
            attempts++;

            const x =
                minX + random() * (maxX - minX);

            const z =
                minZ + random() * (maxZ - minZ);

            const terrainPosition =
                this.terrain.sampleAt(x, z);

            if (!terrainPosition) {
                continue;
            }

		let speciesName =
    this.selectSpecies(random);

let config =
    CloudSpecies[speciesName];

if (!config) {

        "[ChunkCloudSystem] Missing cloud species:",
        speciesName,

        Object.keys(CloudSpecies)
    );

    speciesName = "cumulus";
    config = CloudSpecies.cumulus;
}

if (!config) {
    throw new Error(
        "[ChunkCloudSystem] Cumulus configuration is missing."
    );
}

// Give every cloud a substantially different size.
//
// Storm systems have a narrower variation range so
// their already enormous dimensions remain controlled.

const sizeVariation =
    speciesName === "cumulonimbus"
        ? 0.78 + random() * 0.62
        : 0.62 + random() * 1.08;

const scale = config.scale * (0.75 + random() * 0.55);

const altitude = speciesName === "cumulonimbus"
    ? 4200 + random() * 1800
    : speciesName === "towering"
        ? 3400 + random() * 1500
        : 3000 + random() * 1400;
            const position =
                terrainPosition.add(
                    new BABYLON.Vector3(
                        0,
                        altitude,
