                    )
                );

            const cloud = new Cloud(
                position,
                speciesName,
                scale,
                config
            );

	
const wind = this.sampleWind(position, 0);

cloud.velocity.copyFrom(wind);
cloud.windTarget.copyFrom(wind);
cloud.waterContent =
    random() * cloud.maxWaterCapacity * 0.25;


            cloud.waterContent =
                random() *
                cloud.maxWaterCapacity *
                0.25;

            cloud.baseScale = scale;

            cloud.geometrySeed =
                (seed ^ Math.imul(
                    clouds.length + 1,
                    2654435761
                )) >>> 0;

            cloud.moistureAtLastRender = -1;

            clouds.push(cloud);

            if (speciesName === "cumulonimbus") {
                this.totalGeneratedStorms++;
            }
        }

        const record = {
            key,
            chunkX,
            chunkZ,
            seed,
            clouds
        };

        this.records.set(key, record);

        this.totalGeneratedClouds += clouds.length;

        this.renderDirty = true;

        console.log(
            `[ChunkCloudSystem] Generated ${clouds.length} clouds in ${key}`,
            {
                storms: clouds.filter(
                    cloud => cloud.isStormCloud
                ).length
            }
        );

        return record;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CHUNK STREAMING
    // ------------------------------------------------

    updateStreaming(cameraPosition) {
        const center =
            this.getChunkCoordinates(cameraPosition);

        const desiredChunks = new Set();
        const candidates = [];

        for (
            let x = center.x - this.renderRadius;
            x <= center.x + this.renderRadius;
            x++
        ) {
            for (
                let z = center.z - this.renderRadius;
                z <= center.z + this.renderRadius;
                z++
            ) {
                const dx = x - center.x;
                const dz = z - center.z;

                if (
                    dx * dx + dz * dz >
                    this.renderRadius * this.renderRadius
                ) {
                    continue;
                }

                const minX = x * this.chunkSize;
                const minZ = z * this.chunkSize;

                const maxX = minX + this.chunkSize;
                const maxZ = minZ + this.chunkSize;

                if (
                    maxX <= this.terrain.min.x ||
                    minX >= this.terrain.max.x ||
                    maxZ <= this.terrain.min.z ||
                    minZ >= this.terrain.max.z
                ) {
                    continue;
                }

                const key = this.getChunkKey(x, z);

                desiredChunks.add(key);

                if (!this.records.has(key)) {
                    candidates.push({
                        x,
                        z,
                        distance: dx * dx + dz * dz
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        candidates.sort(
            (a, b) => a.distance - b.distance
        );

        let generated = 0;

        for (const candidate of candidates) {
            if (
                generated >= this.maxChunksPerUpdate
            ) {
                break;
            }

            this.generateChunk(
                candidate.x,
                candidate.z
            );

            generated++;
        }

        const nextActive = new Set();

        for (const key of desiredChunks) {
            if (this.records.has(key)) {
                nextActive.add(key);
            }
        }

        const changed =
            nextActive.size !== this.activeChunks.size ||
            [...nextActive].some(
                key => !this.activeChunks.has(key)
            );

        if (changed) {
            this.activeChunks = nextActive;
            this.renderDirty = true;
        }
    }

	    // ------------------------------------------------
    // ATMOSPHERIC MOISTURE
    // ------------------------------------------------

    getLocalMoisture(cloud) {
        if (
            !this.hydrology ||
            typeof this.hydrology.sampleMoisture !== "function"
        ) {
            return 0.35;
        }

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            this.hydrology.sampleMoisture(
                cloud.position
            ),
            0,
            1
        );
    }

    getEvaporationPotential(cloud) {
        if (
            !this.waterSystem ||
            typeof this.waterSystem.getEvaporationPotential !== "function"
        ) {
            return 0.2;
        }

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            this.waterSystem.getEvaporationPotential(
                cloud.position
            ),
            0,
            1
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CLOUD SIMULATION
    // ------------------------------------------------

updateCloud(cloud, deltaSeconds) {
    cloud.position.addInPlace(
        cloud.velocity.scale(deltaSeconds * 0.5)
    );

    // Environmental sampling is more expensive than
    // the simple cloud-water simulation.
    // Refresh it once per second instead of every tick.

    cloud.environmentSampleTimer =
        (cloud.environmentSampleTimer ?? 0) -
        deltaSeconds;

    if (cloud.environmentSampleTimer <= 0) {
        cloud.localMoisture =
            this.getLocalMoisture(cloud);

        cloud.evaporationPotential =
            this.getEvaporationPotential(cloud);

        cloud.environmentSampleTimer = 1.0;
    }

    const moistureAvailability =
        BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            cloud.localMoisture * 0.65 +
            cloud.evaporationPotential * 0.35,
            0,
            1
        );

    if (!cloud.isRaining) {
        cloud.waterContent +=
            cloud.absorptionRate *
            moistureAvailability *
            deltaSeconds *
            0.05;
    }

    cloud.waterContent = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        cloud.waterContent,
        0,
        cloud.maxWaterCapacity
    );

    const moistureRatio = cloud.moistureRatio;

    if (
        !cloud.isRaining &&
        moistureRatio >= 0.95
    ) {
        this.startRain(cloud);
    }

    if (cloud.isRaining) {
        cloud.rainRemaining -= deltaSeconds;

        if (cloud.rainRemaining <= 0) {
            cloud.isRaining = false;
            cloud.rainIntensity = 0;
            cloud.rainRemaining = 0;
            this.renderDirty = true;
        }
    }

    const previous = cloud.moistureAtLastRender;

    if (
        previous < 0 ||
        Math.abs(moistureRatio - previous) >= 0.08
    ) {
        this.renderDirty = true;
    }
}
    // ------------------------------------------------
    // PRECIPITATION
    // ------------------------------------------------

    startRain(cloud) {
        cloud.isRaining = true;

        cloud.rainRemaining =
            cloud.rainDuration;

        const moistureRatio =
            cloud.moistureRatio;

        const stormMultiplier =
            cloud.isStormCloud ? 2.0 : 1.0;

        cloud.rainIntensity =
            (0.5 + moistureRatio * 1.5) *
            stormMultiplier;

        const releasedWater =
            cloud.maxWaterCapacity * 0.45;

        cloud.waterContent = Math.max(
            0,
            cloud.waterContent - releasedWater
        );

        if (
            this.hydrology &&
            typeof this.hydrology.addRain === "function"
        ) {
            const rainfallAmount =
                0.25 * cloud.rainIntensity;

            const rainfallRadius =
                cloud.scale *
                (cloud.isStormCloud ? 5 : 4);

            this.hydrology.addRain(
                cloud.position,
                rainfallRadius,
                rainfallAmount
            );
        }

        this.renderDirty = true;

        console.log(
            "[ChunkCloudSystem] Precipitation initiated:",
            {
                species: cloud.speciesName,
                position: cloud.position.asArray(),
                moistureRatio,
                intensity: cloud.rainIntensity
            }
        );
    }


updateSimulation(deltaSeconds) {
    let strongestRainCloud = null;

    // Only update currently active chunks.
    for (const key of this.activeChunks) {
        const record = this.records.get(key);

        if (!record) continue;

        for (const cloud of record.clouds) {
		this.windTime += deltaSeconds;

for (const key of this.activeChunks) {
    const record = this.records.get(key);
    if (!record) continue;

    for (const cloud of record.clouds) {
        const wind = this.sampleWind(
            cloud.position,
            this.windTime
        );

        cloud.updateWind(wind, deltaSeconds);

        this.updateCloudEnvironment(cloud, deltaSeconds);
    }
}
this.renderDirty = true;

            if (
                cloud.isRaining &&
                (
                    !strongestRainCloud ||
                    cloud.rainIntensity >
                    strongestRainCloud.rainIntensity
                )
            ) {
                strongestRainCloud = cloud;
            }
        }
    }

    this.rainingCloud = strongestRainCloud;

    if (strongestRainCloud) {
        const rainPosition =
            strongestRainCloud.position.clone();

        rainPosition.y -=
            strongestRainCloud.isStormCloud
                ? 300
                : 10;

        this.rainSystem.start(
            rainPosition,
            strongestRainCloud.rainIntensity
        );
    } else {
        this.rainSystem.stop();
    }
}

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CLOUD DIMENSIONS
    // ------------------------------------------------

getCloudRenderScale(cloud) {
    const moisture = cloud.moistureRatio;

    const expansion = 1.0 + moisture * 0.15;

    const verticalExpansion =
        cloud.isStormCloud
            ? 1.0 + moisture * 0.40
            : cloud.speciesName === "towering"
                ? 1.0 + moisture * 0.30
                : 1.0 + moisture * 0.10;

    return {
        horizontal: cloud.baseScale * expansion,
        vertical:
            cloud.baseScale *
            expansion *
            verticalExpansion
    };
}

	    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CLOUD RENDERING
    // ------------------------------------------------

    rebuildRendering() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.renderer.clear();

        let visibleCount = 0;

        for (const key of this.activeChunks) {
            const record = this.records.get(key);

            if (!record) continue;

            for (const cloud of record.clouds) {
                const config =
                    CloudSpecies[cloud.speciesName];

                if (!config) continue;

                // Generate persistent geometry once.
                if (!cloud.baseGeometryMatrices) {
                    const generator =
                        new CloudGenerator(config);

                    cloud.baseGeometryMatrices =
                        generator.generate(
                            cloud.position,
                            cloud.baseScale,
                            config.recursionDepth,
                            cloud.geometrySeed
                        );

                    cloud.baseGeometryPosition =
                        cloud.position.clone();
                }

                const dimensions =
                    this.getCloudRenderScale(cloud);

                const horizontalScale =
                    dimensions.horizontal /
                    cloud.baseScale;

                const verticalScale =
                    dimensions.vertical /
                    cloud.baseScale;

                const offset =
                    cloud.position.subtract(
                        cloud.baseGeometryPosition
                    );

                for (
                    const matrix of cloud.baseGeometryMatrices
                ) {
                    const scaling =
                        new BABYLON.Vector3();

                    const rotation =
                        new BABYLON.Quaternion();

                    const translation =
                        new BABYLON.Vector3();

                    matrix.decompose(
                        scaling,
                        rotation,
                        translation
                    );

                    scaling.x *= horizontalScale;
                    scaling.z *= horizontalScale;
                    scaling.y *= verticalScale;

                    translation.addInPlace(offset);

                    const adjustedMatrix =
                        BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
                            scaling,
                            rotation,
                            translation
                        );

                    this.renderer.addMatrix(
                        adjustedMatrix,
                        cloud.speciesName,
                        cloud.moistureRatio,
                        cloud.position
                    );
                }

                cloud.moistureAtLastRender =
                    cloud.moistureRatio;

                visibleCount++;
            }
        }

        this.renderer.update();

        this.visibleCloudCount = visibleCount;
        this.renderDirty = false;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // MAIN UPDATE
    // ------------------------------------------------

    update(cameraPosition, deltaSeconds) {
        if (this.disposed || !cameraPosition) {
            return;
        }

        const elapsed = Math.min(
            Math.max(deltaSeconds, 0),
            1
        );

        this.updateStreaming(cameraPosition);

        this.simulationAccumulator += elapsed;

        while (
            this.simulationAccumulator >=
            this.simulationInterval
        ) {
            this.updateSimulation(
                this.simulationInterval
            );

            this.simulationAccumulator -=
                this.simulationInterval;
        }

        this.renderAccumulator += elapsed;

        if (
            this.renderDirty ||
            this.renderAccumulator >=
            this.renderInterval
        ) {
            this.rebuildRendering();

            this.renderAccumulator = 0;
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // DIAGNOSTICS
    // ------------------------------------------------

    getStats() {
        let totalClouds = 0;
        let rainingClouds = 0;
        let stormClouds = 0;
        let averageMoisture = 0;
        let moistureSamples = 0;

        for (const record of this.records.values()) {
            for (const cloud of record.clouds) {
                totalClouds++;

                if (cloud.isRaining) {
                    rainingClouds++;
                }

                if (cloud.isStormCloud) {
                    stormClouds++;
                }

                averageMoisture +=
                    cloud.moistureRatio;

                moistureSamples++;
            }
        }

        return {
            generatedChunks: this.records.size,
            activeChunks: this.activeChunks.size,

            retainedChunks: this.records.size,

            totalClouds,
            visibleClouds: this.visibleCloudCount,

            stormClouds,
            totalGeneratedStorms:
                this.totalGeneratedStorms,

            rainingClouds,

            averageMoisture:
                moistureSamples > 0
                    ? averageMoisture /
                      moistureSamples
                    : 0,

            renderRadius: this.renderRadius,
            chunkSize: this.chunkSize,

            simulationInterval:
                this.simulationInterval,

            renderInterval:
                this.renderInterval,

            renderDirty: this.renderDirty
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // DISPOSAL
    // ------------------------------------------------

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        if (this.rainSystem) {
            this.rainSystem.stop();
        }

        if (this.renderer) {
            this.renderer.mesh.dispose(
                false,
                true
            );
        }

        this.records.clear();
        this.activeChunks.clear();

        this.rainingCloud = null;

        this.visibleCloudCount = 0;
        this.totalGeneratedClouds = 0;
        this.totalGeneratedStorms = 0;

        console.log(
            "[ChunkCloudSystem] Disposed."
        );
    }


sampleWind(position, time) {
    const x = position.x;
    const z = position.z;

    // Large-scale atmospheric circulation.
    const broad = Math.sin(
        x * 0.000035 + z * 0.000018 + time * 0.018
    );

    const secondary = Math.cos(
        z * 0.000045 - x * 0.000012 + time * 0.012
    );

    const direction = new BABYLON.Vector3(
        0.75 + broad * 0.20,
        0,
        0.30 + secondary * 0.25
    ).normalize();

    const speed = 4 + 9 * (
        0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(
            x * 0.00002 + z * 0.000025 + time * 0.01
        )
    );

    return direction.scale(speed);
}

updateCloudEnvironment(cloud, deltaSeconds) {
    // Keep the existing moisture and precipitation simulation.
    // These values should be sampled periodically, not every frame.

    cloud.environmentSampleTimer =
        (cloud.environmentSampleTimer || 0) + deltaSeconds;

    const sampleInterval = 2.0;

    if (cloud.environmentSampleTimer >= sampleInterval) {
        cloud.environmentSampleTimer = 0;

        const position = cloud.position;

        // Use the existing environmental sampling functions
        // from your original updateCloud implementation.
        if (this.moistureField) {
            cloud.localMoisture =
                this.moistureField.sample(
                    position.x,
                    position.z
                );
        }
    }

    // Preserve cloud water capacity.
    cloud.waterContent = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        cloud.waterContent,
        0,
        cloud.maxWaterCapacity
    );

    // Existing precipitation lifecycle.
    if (cloud.isRaining) {
        cloud.rainRemaining -= deltaSeconds;

        if (cloud.rainRemaining <= 0) {
            cloud.isRaining = false;
            cloud.rainIntensity = 0;
            cloud.rainRemaining = 0;
        }
    }
}

}


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/chunk-ecology-system.js
========================================================================


import { ChunkPopulationManager } from "./chunk-population-manager.js";
import { CreateSeededRandom } from "../core/random.js";
import { Tree } from "../ecology/tree.js";
import { Genetics } from "../core/genetics.js";
import { Environment } from "../environment/environment.js";
import { Species } from "../core/species.js";

import {
    TreeRenderer,
    FoliageRenderer
} from "../rendering/tree-renderers.js";

export class ChunkEcologySystem {
    constructor(scene, terrain, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.terrain = terrain;


	this.renderAccumulator = 0;
	this.renderInterval = 0.75;
	
	this.hydrology = options.hydrology ?? null;
	this.geologicalField = options.geologicalField ?? null;

        this.chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 2000;
        this.renderRadius = options.renderRadius ?? 3;
        this.treesPerChunk = options.treesPerChunk ?? 24;
        this.seaLevel = options.seaLevel ?? -100;

        this.worldSeed = options.worldSeed ?? 91827;

        this.environment = new Environment(
            options.sunDirection ??
            new BABYLON.Vector3(-0.5, -1, -0.3)
        );

        this.activeRecords = new Set();

        this.trunkRenderers = {
            oak: new TreeRenderer(
                scene, "chunkOakTrunks", null
            ),
            pine: new TreeRenderer(
                scene, "chunkPineTrunks", null
            )
        };

        this.foliageRenderers = {
            oak: new FoliageRenderer(
                scene,
                "chunkOakFoliage",
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.18, 0.32, 0.13),
                null
            ),
            pine: new FoliageRenderer(
                scene,
                "chunkPineFoliage",
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.10, 0.27, 0.17),
                null
            )
        };

        this.trunkRenderers.oak.mesh.material =
            this.createTrunkMaterial(
                "chunkOakMaterial",
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.30, 0.16, 0.07)
            );

        this.trunkRenderers.pine.mesh.material =
            this.createTrunkMaterial(
                "chunkPineMaterial",
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.40, 0.27, 0.13)
            );

        this.populationManager =
            new ChunkPopulationManager({
                worldSeed: this.worldSeed,
                chunkSize: this.chunkSize,
                maxGenerationPerUpdate: 2,
                maxSimulationPerUpdate: 8,

                adapter: {
                    generate: context =>
                        this.generatePopulation(context),

activate: record => {
    this.activeRecords.add(record);
    this.renderDirty = true;
},

deactivate: record => {
    this.activeRecords.delete(record);
    this.renderDirty = true;
},

                    simulate: (record, deltaYears) =>
                        this.simulatePopulation(
                            record, deltaYears
                        ),

                    serialize: record =>
                        this.serializePopulation(record),

                    deserialize: data =>
                        this.deserializePopulation(data)
                }
            });

        this.renderDirty = true;
        this.disposed = false;
    }

    createTrunkMaterial(name, color) {
        const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            name,
            this.scene
        );

        material.diffuseColor = color;
        material.specularColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.03, 0.03, 0.03);

        return material;
    }

    generatePopulation(context) {
        const random = context.random;

        const trees = [];

        const minX = Math.max(
            context.bounds.minX,
            this.terrain.min.x
        );

        const maxX = Math.min(
            context.bounds.maxX,
            this.terrain.max.x
        );

        const minZ = Math.max(
            context.bounds.minZ,
            this.terrain.min.z
        );

        const maxZ = Math.min(
            context.bounds.maxZ,
            this.terrain.max.z
        );

        if (minX >= maxX || minZ >= maxZ) {
            return { trees };
        }

        const attemptsLimit = this.treesPerChunk * 12;

        let attempts = 0;

        while (
            trees.length < this.treesPerChunk &&
            attempts < attemptsLimit
        ) {
            attempts++;

            const x = minX + random() * (maxX - minX);
            const z = minZ + random() * (maxZ - minZ);

            const profile = this.terrain.sampleProfileAt(
                x, z, 4
            );

            if (!profile) continue;

            if (profile.position.y <= this.seaLevel) {
                continue;
            }

            if (profile.slopeDegrees > 28) {
                continue;
            }

	const soil = this.geologicalField
    ? this.geologicalField.sample(x, z)
    : null;

const speciesName = this.chooseSpeciesForSoil(
    soil,
    random
);

            const genome = Genetics.createGenome();

            // Reproducible genetic variation.
            for (const gene of Object.keys(genome)) {
                genome[gene] *= 0.85 + random() * 0.30;
            }

            const initialAge = random() * 20;

            const tree = new Tree(
                speciesName,
                profile.position,
                genome,
                null,
                initialAge
            );

            tree.soilMoisture =
                0.25 + random() * 0.40;

            trees.push(tree);
        }

        return {
            trees,
            seed: context.seed,
            generatedCount: trees.length
        };
    }

simulatePopulation(record, deltaYears) {
    for (const tree of record.population.trees) {
        if (!tree.alive) continue;

        tree.update(
            deltaYears,
            this.environment,
            this.hydrology
        );
    }

    this.renderDirty = true;
}

    serializePopulation(record) {
        return {
            trees: record.population.trees.map(tree => ({
                speciesName: tree.speciesName,
                position: {
                    x: tree.position.x,
                    y: tree.position.y,
                    z: tree.position.z
                },
                genome: { ...tree.genome },
                parentId: tree.parentId,
                age: tree.age,
                seedTimer: tree.seedTimer,
                soilMoisture: tree.soilMoisture,
                alive: tree.alive,
                droughtYears: tree.droughtYears,
                waterloggingYears: tree.waterloggingYears,
                lowLightYears: tree.lowLightYears
            }))
        };
    }

    deserializePopulation(data) {
        const trees = [];

        for (const item of data.trees ?? []) {
            const tree = new Tree(
                item.speciesName,
                new BABYLON.Vector3(
                    item.position.x,
                    item.position.y,
                    item.position.z
                ),
                { ...item.genome },
                item.parentId,
                item.age
            );

            tree.seedTimer = item.seedTimer ?? 0;
            tree.soilMoisture = item.soilMoisture ?? 0.35;
            tree.alive = item.alive ?? true;

            tree.droughtYears = item.droughtYears ?? 0;
            tree.waterloggingYears =
                item.waterloggingYears ?? 0;
            tree.lowLightYears = item.lowLightYears ?? 0;

            trees.push(tree);
        }

        return { trees };
    }

    rebuildRendering() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.trunkRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.clear();
        }

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.foliageRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.clear();
        }

        const allTrees = [];

        for (const record of this.activeRecords) {
            if (!record.active) continue;

            for (const tree of record.population.trees) {
                if (tree.alive) allTrees.push(tree);
            }
        }

        this.environment.rebuildCanopyGrid(allTrees);

        for (const tree of allTrees) {
            const species = tree.species;
            const age = tree.ageFactor;

            const growth =
                age * age * (3 - 2 * age);

            const height =
                species.trunkLength *
                growth *
                tree.genome.maxHeight;

            const radius =
                species.trunkRadius *
                Math.sqrt(Math.max(age, 0.001)) *
                tree.genome.trunkThickness;

            if (height <= 0.05) continue;

            const trunkMatrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
                new BABYLON.Vector3(
                    radius,
                    height,
                    radius
                ),
                BABYLON.Quaternion.Identity(),
                new BABYLON.Vector3(
                    tree.position.x,
                    tree.position.y + height * 0.5,
                    tree.position.z
                )
            );

            this.trunkRenderers[
                tree.speciesName
            ].addMatrix(trunkMatrix);

            const canopy = species.canopy;

            const canopyHeight = height * canopy.height;

            const canopyWidth =
                height *
                canopy.width *
                tree.genome.canopyWidth;

            const canopyCenter = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                tree.position.x,
                tree.position.y +
                    height * (
                        canopy.startHeight +
                        canopy.height * 0.5
                    ),
                tree.position.z
            );

            const foliageScale = Math.max(
                0.08,
                canopyWidth * 0.42
            );

            const foliageMatrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
                new BABYLON.Vector3(
                    foliageScale * 1.6,
                    Math.max(0.1, canopyHeight * 0.45),
                    foliageScale * 1.6
                ),
                BABYLON.Quaternion.Identity(),
                canopyCenter
            );

            this.foliageRenderers[
                tree.speciesName
            ].addMatrix(foliageMatrix);
        }

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.trunkRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.update();
        }

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.foliageRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.update();
        }

        this.renderDirty = false;
    }


update(cameraPosition, deltaSeconds = 0) {
    if (this.disposed) return;

    // Streaming and simulation continue normally.
    this.populationManager.updateVisibleChunks(
        cameraPosition,
        this.renderRadius
    );

    const deltaYears = deltaSeconds * 0.05;

    if (deltaYears > 0) {
        this.populationManager.updateSimulation(
            deltaYears
        );
    }

    // Batch expensive rendering work.
    this.renderAccumulator += Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(deltaSeconds, 0.25)
    );

    if (
        this.renderDirty &&
        (
            this.renderAccumulator >= this.renderInterval
        )
    ) {
        this.rebuildRendering();
        this.renderAccumulator = 0;
    }
}


    getStats() {
        return {
            ...this.populationManager.getStats(),

            activeTrees: Array.from(
                this.activeRecords
            ).reduce(
                (total, record) =>
                    total + record.population.trees.filter(
                        tree => tree.alive
                    ).length,
                0
            )
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        this.populationManager.dispose();

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.trunkRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.mesh.dispose();
        }

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.foliageRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.mesh.dispose();
        }

        this.activeRecords.clear();
    }


chooseSpeciesForSoil(soil, random) {
    const fallback = random() < 0.55 ? "oak" : "pine";

    if (!soil) {
        return fallback;
    }

    const calculateScore = (speciesName) => {
        const species = Species[speciesName];

        // Some species definitions may not yet have
        // geological affinity data.
        if (!species || !species.soilAffinity) {
            return 0.5;
        }

        const affinity = species.soilAffinity;

        const preferred = Array.isArray(affinity.preferred)
            ? affinity.preferred
            : [];

        const texture = preferred.includes(soil.surfaceSoil)
            ? 1.0
            : (affinity.tolerance ?? 0.5);

        const fertility = 1 - Math.abs(
            (soil.fertility ?? 0.5) -
            (affinity.fertility ?? 0.5)
        );

        const drainage = 1 - Math.abs(
            (soil.drainage ?? 0.5) -
            (affinity.drainage ?? 0.5)
        );

        const moisture = 1 - Math.abs(
            (soil.moisture ?? 0.5) -
            (affinity.moisture ?? 0.5)
        );

        return (
            texture * 0.45 +
            fertility * 0.20 +
            drainage * 0.20 +
            moisture * 0.15
        );
    };

    const oakScore = calculateScore("oak");
    const pineScore = calculateScore("pine");

    const total = oakScore + pineScore;

    if (total <= 0) {
        return fallback;
    }

    return random() < oakScore / total
        ? "oak"
        : "pine";
}

}


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/chunk-geology-system.js
========================================================================


import { GeologicalField } from "../geology/geological-field.js";
import { RockSpecies } from "../geology/rock-species.js";
import { RockGenerator } from "../geology/rock-generator.js";
import { CreateSeededRandom } from "../core/random.js";
import { ThinInstanceRenderer } from "../rendering/thin-instance-renderer.js";

export class ChunkGeologySystem {
    constructor(scene, terrain, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.terrain = terrain;

        this.chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 2000;
        this.renderRadius = options.renderRadius ?? 3;
        this.rocksPerChunk = options.rocksPerChunk ?? 10;
        this.seaLevel = options.seaLevel ?? -100;
        this.worldSeed = options.worldSeed ?? 91827;

        this.field = new GeologicalField(
            terrain,
            this.worldSeed
        );

        // Persistent geological records.
        this.records = new Map();

        // Currently rendered chunks.
        this.activeChunks = new Set();

        this.renderers = {};
        this.disposed = false;
        this.renderDirty = true;

        this.createRenderers();

        console.log(
            "[Geology] Chunk geology initialized."
        );
    }

    createRenderers() {
        for (const speciesName of Object.keys(RockSpecies)) {
            const config = RockSpecies[speciesName];

            // Surface materials are handled separately.
            if (
                speciesName === "sand" ||
                speciesName === "clay" ||
                speciesName === "fertileSoil"
            ) {
                continue;
            }

		const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateBox(
    "chunkRock_" + speciesName,
    {
        size: 1,
        faceUV: [
            new BABYLON.Vector4(0, 0, 1, 1),
            new BABYLON.Vector4(0, 0, 1, 1),
            new BABYLON.Vector4(0, 0, 1, 1),
            new BABYLON.Vector4(0, 0, 1, 1),
            new BABYLON.Vector4(0, 0, 1, 1),
            new BABYLON.Vector4(0, 0, 1, 1)
        ]
    },
    this.scene
);

            const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
                "chunkRockMaterial_" + speciesName,
                this.scene
            );

            material.diffuseColor = config.color.clone();

            material.specularColor =
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.06, 0.06, 0.06);

            if (speciesName === "anomaly") {
                material.emissiveColor =
                    config.color.scale(0.35);
            }

            mesh.material = material;

            this.renderers[speciesName] =
                new ThinInstanceRenderer(
                    this.scene,
                    "chunkRock_" + speciesName,
                    mesh,
                    null
                );
        }
    }

    getChunkKey(x, z) {
        return `${x},${z}`;
    }

    getChunkSeed(chunkX, chunkZ) {
        return (
            this.worldSeed ^
            Math.imul(chunkX, 73856093) ^
            Math.imul(chunkZ, 19349663)
        ) >>> 0;
    }

    generateChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {
        const key = this.getChunkKey(chunkX, chunkZ);

        if (this.records.has(key)) {
            return this.records.get(key);
        }

        const seed = this.getChunkSeed(
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        );

        const random = CreateSeededRandom(seed);

        const minX = Math.max(
            chunkX * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.min.x
        );

        const maxX = Math.min(
            (chunkX + 1) * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.max.x
        );

        const minZ = Math.max(
            chunkZ * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.min.z
        );

        const maxZ = Math.min(
            (chunkZ + 1) * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.max.z
        );

        const formations = [];

        if (minX >= maxX || minZ >= maxZ) {
            const emptyRecord = {
                key,
                chunkX,
                chunkZ,
                seed,
                formations
            };

            this.records.set(key, emptyRecord);
            return emptyRecord;
        }

        const attemptsLimit = this.rocksPerChunk * 12;

        let attempts = 0;

        while (
            formations.length < this.rocksPerChunk &&
            attempts < attemptsLimit
        ) {
            attempts++;

            const x = minX + random() * (maxX - minX);
            const z = minZ + random() * (maxZ - minZ);

            const sample = this.field.sample(x, z);

            if (!sample) continue;

            if (sample.position.y <= this.seaLevel) {
                continue;
            }

		// Formations come from underlying bedrock,
// not the current surface-soil classification.
if (sample.exposure < 0.45) {
    continue;
}

const speciesName = sample.bedrockType;
const config = RockSpecies[speciesName];

            // Only generate actual geological formations.
            if (!config) continue;

            if (
                speciesName === "sand" ||
                speciesName === "clay" ||
                speciesName === "fertileSoil"
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            const variation = 0.85 + random() * 0.65;

            const formationSeed = (
                seed ^
                Math.imul(formations.length + 1, 2654435761)
            ) >>> 0;

            formations.push({
                id: `${key}:${formations.length}`,
                speciesName,
                position: sample.position.clone(),
                scale: config.scale * variation * 2.0,
                seed: formationSeed,
                age: 0,
                integrity: 1,
                erosionProgress: 0,
                alive: true
            });
        }

        const record = {
            key,
            chunkX,
            chunkZ,
            seed,
            formations
        };

        this.records.set(key, record);

        console.log(
            `[Geology] Generated ${formations.length} formations in ${key}`
        );

        return record;
    }

    update(cameraPosition) {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        const centerX = Math.floor(
            cameraPosition.x / this.chunkSize
        );

        const centerZ = Math.floor(
            cameraPosition.z / this.chunkSize
        );

        const desiredChunks = new Set();

        for (
            let x = centerX - this.renderRadius;
            x <= centerX + this.renderRadius;
            x++
        ) {
            for (
                let z = centerZ - this.renderRadius;
                z <= centerZ + this.renderRadius;
                z++
            ) {
                const minX = x * this.chunkSize;
                const minZ = z * this.chunkSize;

                const maxX = minX + this.chunkSize;
                const maxZ = minZ + this.chunkSize;

                if (
                    maxX <= this.terrain.min.x ||
                    minX >= this.terrain.max.x ||
                    maxZ <= this.terrain.min.z ||
                    minZ >= this.terrain.max.z
                ) {
                    continue;
                }

                const key = this.getChunkKey(x, z);

                desiredChunks.add(key);

                // Generate once, preserve thereafter.
                this.generateChunk(x, z);
            }
        }

        let changed = false;

        for (const key of this.activeChunks) {
            if (!desiredChunks.has(key)) {
                this.activeChunks.delete(key);
                changed = true;
            }
        }

        for (const key of desiredChunks) {
            if (!this.activeChunks.has(key)) {
                this.activeChunks.add(key);
                changed = true;
            }
        }

        if (changed || this.renderDirty) {
            this.rebuildRendering();
        }
    }

    rebuildRendering() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        for (const renderer of Object.values(this.renderers)) {
            renderer.clear();
        }

        let visibleFormationCount = 0;

        for (const key of this.activeChunks) {
            const record = this.records.get(key);

            if (!record) continue;

            for (const formation of record.formations) {
                if (!formation.alive) continue;

                const config =
                    RockSpecies[formation.speciesName];

                const renderer =
                    this.renderers[formation.speciesName];

                if (!config || !renderer) continue;

                const generator = new RockGenerator(
                    config,
                    formation.seed
                );

                // Conservative initial detail level.
                // Higher recursive detail can be introduced
                // after performance validation.
                const depth = Math.min(
                    1,
                    config.recursionDepth
                );

                const matrices = generator.generate(
                    formation.position,
                    formation.scale,
                    depth
                );

                renderer.addMatrices(matrices);

                visibleFormationCount++;
            }
        }

        for (const renderer of Object.values(this.renderers)) {
            renderer.update();
        }

        this.visibleFormationCount =
            visibleFormationCount;

        this.renderDirty = false;
    }

    getStats() {
        let totalFormations = 0;

        for (const record of this.records.values()) {
            totalFormations += record.formations.filter(
                formation => formation.alive
            ).length;
        }

        return {
            generatedChunks: this.records.size,
            activeChunks: this.activeChunks.size,
            totalFormations,
            visibleFormations: this.visibleFormationCount ?? 0
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        for (const renderer of Object.values(this.renderers)) {
            renderer.mesh.dispose();
        }

        this.renderers = {};
        this.activeChunks.clear();
        this.records.clear();
    }
}


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/chunk-population-manager.js
========================================================================


import { CreateSeededRandom } from "../core/random.js";

export class ChunkPopulationManager {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.worldSeed = options.worldSeed ?? 91827;
        this.chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 2000;

        this.maxGenerationPerUpdate =
            options.maxGenerationPerUpdate ?? 2;

        this.maxSimulationPerUpdate =
            options.maxSimulationPerUpdate ?? 8;

        this.adapter = options.adapter ?? {};

        this.records = new Map();
        this.pending = [];
        this.activeKeys = new Set();

        this.simulationCursor = 0;

        this.statistics = {
            generated: 0,
            activated: 0,
            deactivated: 0,
            simulated: 0
        };
    }

    getKey(x, z) {
        return `${x},${z}`;
    }

    getSeed(x, z) {
        let seed = this.worldSeed >>> 0;

        seed ^= Math.imul(x | 0, 73856093);
        seed ^= Math.imul(z | 0, 19349663);

        seed = Math.imul(
            seed ^ (seed >>> 16),
            2246822519
        );

        seed = Math.imul(
            seed ^ (seed >>> 13),
            3266489917
        );

        return (seed ^ (seed >>> 16)) >>> 0;
    }

    getChunkCoordinates(position) {
        return {
            x: Math.floor(position.x / this.chunkSize),
            z: Math.floor(position.z / this.chunkSize)
        };
    }

    getRecord(x, z) {
        return this.records.get(this.getKey(x, z)) ?? null;
    }

    requestChunk(x, z, priority = 0) {
        const key = this.getKey(x, z);

        const existing = this.records.get(key);

        if (existing) {
            existing.lastRequested = Date.now();

            this.activateRecord(existing);

            return existing;
        }

        const pending = this.pending.find(
            item => item.key === key
        );

        if (pending) {
            pending.priority = Math.max(
                pending.priority,
                priority
            );

            return null;
        }

        this.pending.push({
            x, z, key, priority
        });

        this.pending.sort(
            (a, b) => b.priority - a.priority
        );

        return null;
    }

    generateRecord(x, z) {
        const key = this.getKey(x, z);

        if (this.records.has(key)) {
            return this.records.get(key);
        }

        const seed = this.getSeed(x, z);

        const random = CreateSeededRandom(seed);

        const bounds = {
            minX: x * this.chunkSize,
            maxX: (x + 1) * this.chunkSize,
            minZ: z * this.chunkSize,
            maxZ: (z + 1) * this.chunkSize
        };

        const context = {
            x, z, key, seed, random, bounds
        };

        const population =
            this.adapter.generate?.(context) ?? {};

        const record = {
            x,
            z,
            key,
            seed,
            bounds,
            population,
            active: false,
            generated: true,
            simulationTime: 0,
            lastRequested: Date.now(),
            metadata: { version: 1 }
        };

        this.records.set(key, record);

        this.statistics.generated++;

        this.activateRecord(record);

        return record;
    }

    activateRecord(record) {
        if (record.active) return;

        this.adapter.activate?.(record);

        record.active = true;

        this.activeKeys.add(record.key);

        this.statistics.activated++;
    }

    deactivateRecord(record) {
        if (!record.active) return;

        this.adapter.deactivate?.(record);

        record.active = false;

        this.activeKeys.delete(record.key);

        this.statistics.deactivated++;
    }

    updateGeneration() {
        let generated = 0;

        while (
            this.pending.length > 0 &&
            generated < this.maxGenerationPerUpdate
        ) {
            const item = this.pending.shift();

            if (this.records.has(item.key)) continue;

            this.generateRecord(item.x, item.z);

            generated++;
        }

        return generated;
    }

    updateSimulation(deltaYears) {
        if (
            !Number.isFinite(deltaYears) ||
            deltaYears < 0
        ) {
            throw new RangeError(
                "deltaYears must be non-negative."
            );
        }

        const active = Array.from(this.activeKeys);

        if (active.length === 0) {
            this.simulationCursor = 0;
            return;
        }

        const count = Math.min(
            active.length,
            this.maxSimulationPerUpdate
        );

        const start =
            this.simulationCursor % active.length;

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const index = (start + i) % active.length;
            const record = this.records.get(active[index]);

            if (!record || !record.active) continue;

            this.adapter.simulate?.(
                record,
                deltaYears
            );

            record.simulationTime += deltaYears;

            this.statistics.simulated++;
        }

        this.simulationCursor =
            (start + count) % active.length;
    }

    updateVisibleChunks(cameraPosition, radius = 3) {
        const center = this.getChunkCoordinates(
            cameraPosition
        );

        const required = new Set();

        for (
            let x = center.x - radius;
            x <= center.x + radius;
            x++
        ) {
            for (
                let z = center.z - radius;
                z <= center.z + radius;
                z++
            ) {
                const dx = x - center.x;
                const dz = z - center.z;

                if (
                    dx * dx + dz * dz >
                    radius * radius
                ) continue;

                const key = this.getKey(x, z);

                required.add(key);

                const distance = Math.sqrt(
                    dx * dx + dz * dz
                );

                this.requestChunk(
                    x, z, 1000 - distance
                );
            }
        }

        // Remove obsolete requests before generating them.
        this.pending = this.pending.filter(
            item => required.has(item.key)
        );

        for (const key of Array.from(this.activeKeys)) {
            if (required.has(key)) continue;

            const record = this.records.get(key);

            if (record) {
                this.deactivateRecord(record);
            }
        }

        this.updateGeneration();
    }

    getPopulation(x, z) {
        return this.getRecord(x, z)?.population ?? null;
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            ...this.statistics,
            totalRecords: this.records.size,
            activeRecords: this.activeKeys.size,
            pendingRecords: this.pending.length
        };
    }

    exportRecord(x, z) {
        const record = this.getRecord(x, z);

        if (!record) return null;

        return {
            version: 1,
            coordinates: { x, z },
            seed: record.seed,
            bounds: { ...record.bounds },
            simulationTime: record.simulationTime,
            population: this.adapter.serialize
                ? this.adapter.serialize(record)
                : structuredClone(record.population)
        };
    }

    importRecord(data) {
        if (!data || data.version !== 1) {
            throw new Error(
                "Unsupported population record version."
            );
        }

        const { x, z } = data.coordinates;
        const key = this.getKey(x, z);

        const previous = this.records.get(key);

        if (previous?.active) {
            this.deactivateRecord(previous);
        }

        const record = {
            x,
            z,
            key,
            seed: data.seed,
            bounds: { ...data.bounds },

            population: this.adapter.deserialize
                ? this.adapter.deserialize(data.population)
                : structuredClone(data.population),

            active: false,
            generated: true,
            simulationTime: data.simulationTime ?? 0,
            lastRequested: Date.now(),
            metadata: { version: 1 }
        };

        this.records.set(key, record);

        return record;
    }

    dispose() {
        for (const record of this.records.values()) {
            if (record.active) {
                this.deactivateRecord(record);
            }

            this.adapter.dispose?.(record);
        }

        this.records.clear();
        this.pending.length = 0;
        this.activeKeys.clear();

        this.simulationCursor = 0;
    }
}

class FieldRegistry {

    constructor() {

        this.fields = new Map();

    }

    register(field) {

        if (!field || !field.name || typeof field.sample !== "function") {

            throw new TypeError("Invalid world field.");

        }

        if (this.fields.has(field.name)) {

            throw new Error(
                `Field already registered: ${field.name}`
            );

        }

        this.fields.set(field.name, field);

        return field;

    }

    unregister(name) {

        return this.fields.delete(name);

    }

    get(name) {

        return this.fields.get(name) || null;

    }

    has(name) {

        return this.fields.has(name);

    }

    getAll() {

        return Array.from(this.fields.values());

    }

    clear() {

        this.fields.clear();

    }

}

export { FieldRegistry };


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/fields/field-sampler.js

class FieldSampler {

    constructor(registry) {

        this.registry = registry;

    }

    sample(name, position, time = 0) {

        const field = this.registry.get(name);

        if (!field) return null;

        return field.sample(position, time);

    }

    sampleAll(position, time = 0) {

        const result = {};

        for (const field of this.registry.getAll()) {

            if (!field.enabled) continue;

            result[field.name] =
                field.sample(position, time);

        }

        return result;

    }

}

export { FieldSampler };

import { GeometryModel } from "./geometry-model.js";
import { MetricTensor } from "../metric-tensor.js";

class EuclideanGeometry extends GeometryModel {

    constructor() {

        super("euclidean");

        this.metric = MetricTensor.identity();

    }

    metricAt() {

        return this.metric;

    }

    distance(a, b) {

        return Math.hypot(
            b.x - a.x,
            b.y - a.y,
            b.z - a.z
        );

    }

}

export { EuclideanGeometry };


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/fields/geometry/geodesic-solver.js
========================================================================


class GeodesicSolver {

    constructor(geometry, options = {}) {

        this.geometry = geometry;

        this.iterations =
            options.iterations ?? 80;

        this.relaxation =
            options.relaxation ?? 0.15;

        this.segments =
            options.segments ?? 24;

    }

    solve(start, end) {

        const points = [];

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i <= this.segments;
            i++
        ) {

            const t = i / this.segments;

            points.push({

                x: start.x + (end.x - start.x) * t,

                y: start.y + (end.y - start.y) * t,

                z: start.z + (end.z - start.z) * t

            });

        }

        const step = this.relaxation;

        for (
            let iteration = 0;
            iteration < this.iterations;
            iteration++
        ) {

            for (
                let i = 1;
                i < points.length - 1;
                i++
            ) {

                const previous = points[i - 1];

                const current = points[i];

                const next = points[i + 1];

                const midpoint = {

                    x: (previous.x + next.x) * 0.5,

                    y: (previous.y + next.y) * 0.5,

                    z: (previous.z + next.z) * 0.5

                };

                const candidate = {

                    x: current.x +
                        (midpoint.x - current.x) * step,

                    y: current.y +
                        (midpoint.y - current.y) * step,

                    z: current.z +
                        (midpoint.z - current.z) * step

                };

                // Reject candidates outside bounded geometries.
                if (
                    typeof this.geometry.isInside === "function" &&
                    !this.geometry.isInside(candidate)
                ) {
                    continue;
                }

                points[i] = candidate;

            }

        }

        return {

            points,

            length: this.calculateLength(points)

        };

    }

    calculateLength(points) {

        let length = 0;

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < points.length - 1;
            i++
        ) {

            length += this.geometry.distance(
                points[i],
                points[i + 1]
            );

        }

        return length;

    }

}

export { GeodesicSolver };
