class WorldLibraryUI {
    constructor(persistence, callbacks = {}) {
        this.persistence = persistence;
        this.callbacks = callbacks;
        this.currentWorldId = null;
        this.container = null;
        this.create();
    }

    create() {
        this.container = document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(this.container.style, {
            position: "fixed",
            inset: "0",
            zIndex: "2000",
            display: "none",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.82)",
            color: "white",
            fontFamily: "Arial, sans-serif"
        });

        this.container.innerHTML = `
            <section style="
                width:min(620px,90vw);
                max-height:85vh;
                overflow:auto;
                padding:28px;
                border-radius:14px;
                background:#18202b;
                box-shadow:0 20px 80px #000;
            ">
                <h2>SOMNIUM WORLDS</h2>

                <p style="color:#aab5c4">
                    Create and manage persistent worlds.
                </p>

                <div style="display:flex;gap:8px">
                    <input
                        id="world-name"
                        placeholder="New world name"
                        style="flex:1;padding:12px"
                    />

                    <button id="world-create">
                        CREATE
                    </button>
                </div>

                <hr style="margin:22px 0">

                <div id="world-list"></div>

                <hr style="margin:22px 0">

                <div style="display:flex;gap:8px">
                    <button id="world-save">
                        SAVE CURRENT WORLD
                    </button>

                    <button id="world-close">
                        CLOSE
                    </button>
                </div>

                <p
                    id="world-status"
                    style="color:#aab5c4;font-size:12px"
                ></p>
            </section>
        `;

        document.body.appendChild(this.container);


	this.launcher = document.createElement("button");

this.launcher.textContent = "WORLD LIBRARY";

Object.assign(this.launcher.style, {
    position: "fixed",
    top: "76px",
    right: "18px",
    zIndex: "1500",
    padding: "12px 18px",
    background: "#263b50",
    color: "white",
    border: "1px solid #71869b",
    borderRadius: "6px",
    cursor: "pointer"
});

this.launcher.onclick = () => this.open();

document.body.appendChild(this.launcher);

        this.list = this.container.querySelector("#world-list");
        this.nameInput = this.container.querySelector("#world-name");
        this.status = this.container.querySelector("#world-status");

        this.container.querySelector("#world-create").onclick =
            () => this.createWorld();

        this.container.querySelector("#world-save").onclick =
            () => this.saveCurrent();

        this.container.querySelector("#world-close").onclick =
            () => this.close();
    }

    setStatus(message) {
        this.status.textContent = message;
    }

    open() {
        this.container.style.display = "flex";
        this.refresh();
    }

    close() {
        this.container.style.display = "none";
    }

    async refresh() {
        try {
            const worlds = await this.persistence.listWorlds();

            this.list.replaceChildren();

            if (worlds.length === 0) {
                const empty = document.createElement("p");
                empty.textContent = "No saved worlds yet.";
                this.list.appendChild(empty);
                return;
            }

            for (const world of worlds) {
                const row = document.createElement("div");

                Object.assign(row.style, {
                    display: "flex",
                    alignItems: "center",
                    gap: "8px",
                    padding: "12px 0",
                    borderBottom: "1px solid #354252"
                });

                const information = document.createElement("div");
                information.style.flex = "1";

                const title = document.createElement("strong");
                title.textContent = world.name;

                const details = document.createElement("small");
                details.style.display = "block";
                details.style.color = "#aab5c4";
                details.textContent =
                    `Seed: ${world.worldSeed} · Revision: ${world.revision}`;

                information.append(title, details);

                const load = document.createElement("button");
                load.textContent = "LOAD";
                load.onclick = () => this.loadWorld(world.id);

                const rename = document.createElement("button");
                rename.textContent = "RENAME";
                rename.onclick = () => this.renameWorld(world);

                const remove = document.createElement("button");
                remove.textContent = "DELETE";
                remove.onclick = () => this.deleteWorld(world);

                row.append(information, load, rename, remove);
                this.list.appendChild(row);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

    async createWorld() {
        try {
            const name = this.nameInput.value.trim();

            const world = await this.persistence.createWorld(
                name,
                {
                    worldSeed: Math.floor(
                        Math.random() * 2147483647
                    )
                }
            );

            this.currentWorldId = world.metadata.id;

            if (this.callbacks.onLoad) {
                await this.callbacks.onLoad(world);
            }

            this.nameInput.value = "";
            this.setStatus(`Created ${name}.`);
            await this.refresh();
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

    async loadWorld(id) {
        try {
            const world = await this.persistence.loadWorld(id);

            if (this.callbacks.onLoad) {
                await this.callbacks.onLoad(world);
            }

            this.currentWorldId = id;
            this.setStatus(`Loaded ${world.metadata.name}.`);
            await this.refresh();
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

    async saveCurrent() {
        try {
            if (!this.currentWorldId) {
                throw new Error("Select or create a world first.");
            }

            const world = await this.persistence.loadWorld(
                this.currentWorldId
            );

            if (this.callbacks.onCapture) {
                await this.callbacks.onCapture(world);
            }

            await this.persistence.saveWorld(world);

            this.setStatus(
                `Saved ${world.metadata.name} successfully.`
            );

            await this.refresh();
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

    async renameWorld(world) {
        const name = prompt(
            "Enter a new world name:",
            world.name
        );

        if (name === null) return;

        try {
            await this.persistence.renameWorld(
                world.id,
                name
            );

            await this.refresh();
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

    async deleteWorld(world) {
        if (!confirm(`Delete "${world.name}" permanently?`)) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            await this.persistence.deleteWorld(world.id);

            if (this.currentWorldId === world.id) {
                this.currentWorldId = null;
            }

            this.setStatus(`Deleted ${world.name}.`);
            await this.refresh();
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

dispose() {
    this.launcher?.remove();
    this.container?.remove();

    this.launcher = null;
    this.container = null;
}

}

export { WorldLibraryUI };

FILE: src/world/persistence/world-persistence.js

import { WorldState } from "./world-state.js";

const DATABASE_NAME = "somnium-worlds";
const DATABASE_VERSION = 1;
const STORE_NAME = "worlds";

class WorldPersistence {
    constructor() {
        this.database = null;
        this.opening = null;
    }

    async open() {
        if (this.database) {
            return this.database;
        }

        if (this.opening) {
            return this.opening;
        }

        if (!globalThis.indexedDB) {
            throw new Error(
                "IndexedDB is unavailable in this browser."
            );
        }

        this.opening = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const request = indexedDB.open(
                DATABASE_NAME,
                DATABASE_VERSION
            );

            request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
                const database = request.result;

                if (!database.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_NAME)) {
                    database.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME, {
                        keyPath: "metadata.id"
                    });
                }
            };

            request.onsuccess = () => {
                this.database = request.result;

                this.database.onversionchange = () => {
                    this.database.close();
                    this.database = null;
                };

                resolve(this.database);
            };

            request.onerror = () => {
                reject(request.error);
            };

            request.onblocked = () => {
                console.warn(
                    "[WorldPersistence] Database upgrade blocked."
                );
            };
        });

        try {
            return await this.opening;
        } finally {
            this.opening = null;
        }
    }

    async transaction(mode, operation) {
        const database = await this.open();

        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const tx = database.transaction(STORE_NAME, mode);
            const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);

            let result;

            try {
                result = operation(store, tx);
            } catch (error) {
                tx.abort();
                reject(error);
                return;
            }

            tx.oncomplete = () => resolve(result);
            tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
            tx.onabort = () => reject(
                tx.error ?? new Error("Transaction aborted.")
            );
        });
    }

    async createWorld(name, options = {}) {
        const cleanName = String(name ?? "").trim();

        if (!cleanName) {
            throw new Error("Please provide a world name.");
        }

        const world = new WorldState({
            ...options,
            name: cleanName
        });

        world.validate();

        await this.transaction("readwrite", store => {
            store.add(world.serialize());
        });

        console.log(
            "[WorldPersistence] Created world:",
            world.metadata.name
        );

        return world;
    }

    async listWorlds() {
        const records = await new Promise(async (resolve, reject) => {
            try {
                const database = await this.open();
                const tx = database.transaction(STORE_NAME, "readonly");
                const request = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME).getAll();

                request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
                request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
            } catch (error) {
                reject(error);
            }
        });

        return records
            .map(record => ({
                id: record.metadata.id,
                name: record.metadata.name,
                createdAt: record.metadata.createdAt,
                updatedAt: record.metadata.updatedAt,
                revision: record.metadata.revision,
                worldSeed: record.generation.worldSeed
            }))
            .sort((a, b) =>
                b.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.updatedAt)
            );
    }

    async loadWorld(id) {
        const record = await new Promise(async (resolve, reject) => {
            try {
                const database = await this.open();
                const tx = database.transaction(STORE_NAME, "readonly");
                const request = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME).get(id);

                request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
                request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
            } catch (error) {
                reject(error);
            }
        });

        if (!record) {
            throw new Error(`World not found: ${id}`);
        }

        return WorldState.deserialize(record);
    }

    async saveWorld(world, options = {}) {
        if (!(world instanceof WorldState)) {
            throw new TypeError("Expected a WorldState instance.");
        }

        world.validate();

        const serialized = world.serialize();

        await this.transaction("readwrite", (store, tx) => {
            const request = store.get(world.metadata.id);

            request.onsuccess = () => {
                const existing = request.result;

                if (!existing) {
                    if (!options.allowCreate) {
                        tx.abort();
                        return;
                    }

                    world.touch();
                    store.add(world.serialize());
                    return;
                }

                // Prevent overwriting changes from another tab.
                if (
                    existing.metadata.revision !==
                    world.metadata.revision
                ) {
                    tx.abort();
                    return;
                }

                world.touch();
                store.put(world.serialize());
            };
        });

        return world;
    }

    async renameWorld(id, newName) {
        const world = await this.loadWorld(id);
        const cleanName = String(newName ?? "").trim();

        if (!cleanName) {
            throw new Error("World name cannot be empty.");
        }

        world.metadata.name = cleanName;

        return this.saveWorld(world);
    }

    async deleteWorld(id) {
        await this.transaction("readwrite", store => {
            store.delete(id);
        });

        console.log("[WorldPersistence] Deleted world:", id);
    }

    close() {
        this.database?.close();
        this.database = null;
    }
}

export { WorldPersistence };

FILE: src/world/persistence/world-session.js

import { WorldState } from "./world-state.js";

class WorldSession {
    constructor(ecology) {
        if (!ecology?.populationManager) {
            throw new Error(
                "WorldSession requires ChunkEcologySystem."
            );
        }

        this.ecology = ecology;
    }

    capture(world, elapsedTime = 0) {
        if (!(world instanceof WorldState)) {
            throw new TypeError("Expected WorldState.");
        }

        const manager = this.ecology.populationManager;

        const chunkRecords = [];

        for (const record of manager.records.values()) {
            const exported = manager.exportRecord(
                record.x,
                record.z
            );

            if (exported) {
                chunkRecords.push(exported);
            }
        }

        world.generation.worldSeed = manager.worldSeed;

        world.simulation.elapsedTime = elapsedTime;

        world.ecology.chunkRecords = chunkRecords;


        return world;
    }

    restore(world) {
        if (!(world instanceof WorldState)) {
            throw new TypeError("Expected WorldState.");
        }

        const ecology = this.ecology;
        const manager = ecology.populationManager;

        // Deactivate currently rendered populations.
        for (const record of manager.records.values()) {
            if (record.active) {
                manager.deactivateRecord(record);
            }
        }

        manager.records.clear();
        manager.pending.length = 0;
        manager.activeKeys.clear();
        manager.simulationCursor = 0;

        ecology.worldSeed = world.generation.worldSeed;
        manager.worldSeed = world.generation.worldSeed;

        // Restore all previously generated populations.
        for (const record of world.ecology.chunkRecords) {
            manager.importRecord(record);
        }

        ecology.renderDirty = true;

        return {
            elapsedTime: world.simulation.elapsedTime,
            worldSeed: world.generation.worldSeed,
            restoredChunks: world.ecology.chunkRecords.length
        };
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            worldSeed: this.ecology.populationManager.worldSeed,
            generatedChunks:
                this.ecology.populationManager.records.size
        };
    }
}

export { WorldSession };

FILE: src/world/persistence/world-state.js
========================================================================


const WORLD_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;

function createId() {
    if (globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID) {
        return globalThis.crypto.randomUUID();
    }

    return `world-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()
        .toString(36).slice(2)}`;
}

function cloneData(value) {
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
}

class WorldState {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        const now = new Date().toISOString();

        this.schemaVersion = WORLD_SCHEMA_VERSION;

        this.metadata = {
            id: options.id ?? createId(),
            name: options.name ?? "Untitled World",
            createdAt: options.createdAt ?? now,
            updatedAt: now,
            revision: options.revision ?? 0
        };

        this.generation = {
            worldSeed: options.worldSeed ?? 91827,
            generatorVersion: options.generatorVersion ?? 1,
            configuration: options.configuration ?? {}
        };

        this.simulation = {
            elapsedTime: options.elapsedTime ?? 0,
            simulationRate: options.simulationRate ?? 1,
            paused: options.paused ?? false
        };

        this.ecology = {
            chunkRecords: options.chunkRecords ?? []
        };

        this.entities = {
            manual: options.manualEntities ?? [],
            overrides: options.entityOverrides ?? [],
            removedProceduralIds: options.removedProceduralIds ?? []
        };

        this.environment = {
            state: options.environmentState ?? {}
        };
    }

    validate() {
        if (!this.metadata.id || !this.metadata.name.trim()) {
            throw new Error("World requires an ID and name.");
        }

        if (!Number.isInteger(this.generation.worldSeed)) {
            throw new Error("Invalid world seed.");
        }

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(this.simulation.elapsedTime) ||
            this.simulation.elapsedTime < 0
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid simulation time.");
        }

        if (!Array.isArray(this.ecology.chunkRecords)) {
            throw new Error("Invalid ecology records.");
        }

        if (
            !Array.isArray(this.entities.manual) ||
            !Array.isArray(this.entities.overrides) ||
            !Array.isArray(this.entities.removedProceduralIds)
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid entity collections.");
        }

        return true;
    }

    touch() {
        this.metadata.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        this.metadata.revision++;
    }

    serialize() {
        this.validate();

        return cloneData(this);
    }

    static deserialize(data) {
        if (!data || typeof data !== "object") {
            throw new Error("Invalid world data.");
        }

        if (data.schemaVersion !== WORLD_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported world schema: ${data.schemaVersion}`
            );
        }

        const state = new WorldState({
            id: data.metadata?.id,
            name: data.metadata?.name,
            createdAt: data.metadata?.createdAt,
            revision: data.metadata?.revision,
            worldSeed: data.generation?.worldSeed,
            generatorVersion: data.generation?.generatorVersion,
            configuration: data.generation?.configuration,
            elapsedTime: data.simulation?.elapsedTime,
            simulationRate: data.simulation?.simulationRate,
            paused: data.simulation?.paused,
            chunkRecords: data.ecology?.chunkRecords,
            manualEntities: data.entities?.manual,
            entityOverrides: data.entities?.overrides,
            removedProceduralIds:
                data.entities?.removedProceduralIds,
            environmentState: data.environment?.state
        });

        state.metadata.updatedAt =
            data.metadata.updatedAt ?? state.metadata.updatedAt;

        state.validate();

        return state;
    }
}

export {
    WorldState,
    WORLD_SCHEMA_VERSION
};


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/terrain-mesh-worker.js
========================================================================


let terrain = null;

function clamp(value, min, max) {
    return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
}

function lerp(a, b, t) {
    return a + (b - a) * t;
}


function hashNoise2D(x, z, seed = 1) {
    let n =
        Math.imul(x | 0, 374761393) +
        Math.imul(z | 0, 668265263) +
        Math.imul(seed | 0, 1442695041);

    n = Math.imul(
        n ^ (n >>> 13),
        1274126177
    );

    n ^= n >>> 16;

    return (n >>> 0) / 4294967295;
}

function smoothNoise2D(x, z, seed = 1) {
    const ix = Math.floor(x);
    const iz = Math.floor(z);

    const fx = x - ix;
    const fz = z - iz;

    const u = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx);
    const v = fz * fz * (3 - 2 * fz);

    const a = hashNoise2D(ix, iz, seed);
    const b = hashNoise2D(ix + 1, iz, seed);
    const c = hashNoise2D(ix, iz + 1, seed);
    const d = hashNoise2D(ix + 1, iz + 1, seed);

    return lerp(
        lerp(a, b, u),
        lerp(c, d, u),
        v
    );
}

function fractalNoise2D(x, z, seed, octaves = 3) {
    let value = 0;
    let amplitude = 1;
    let frequency = 1;
    let totalAmplitude = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        value += smoothNoise2D(
            x * frequency,
            z * frequency,
            seed + i * 1013
        ) * amplitude;

        totalAmplitude += amplitude;
        amplitude *= 0.5;
        frequency *= 2;
    }

    return value / totalAmplitude;
}

function mixColor(a, b, t) {
    return [
        lerp(a[0], b[0], t),
        lerp(a[1], b[1], t),
        lerp(a[2], b[2], t)
    ];
}


function pixelValue(x, z) {
    const t = terrain;

    x = clamp(x, 0, t.width - 1);
    z = clamp(z, 0, t.height - 1);

    const index = (Math.floor(z) * t.width + Math.floor(x)) * 4;
    const p = t.pixels;

    const r = p[index];
    const g = p[index + 1];
    const b = p[index + 2];

    switch (t.heightChannel) {
        case "red": return r / 255;
        case "green": return g / 255;
        case "blue": return b / 255;
        case "luminance":
            return (
                0.2126 * r +
                0.7152 * g +
                0.0722 * b
            ) / 255;
        case "average":
        default:
            return (r + g + b) / 765;
    }
}

function heightAt(x, z) {
    const u = clamp(
        (x - terrain.minX) / terrain.worldSize,
        0, 1
    );

    const v = clamp(
        (z - terrain.minZ) / terrain.worldSize,
        0, 1
    );

    const px = u * (terrain.width - 1);
    const pz = v * (terrain.height - 1);

    const x0 = Math.floor(px);
    const z0 = Math.floor(pz);

    const x1 = Math.min(x0 + 1, terrain.width - 1);
    const z1 = Math.min(z0 + 1, terrain.height - 1);

    const tx = px - x0;
    const tz = pz - z0;

    const a = lerp(
        pixelValue(x0, z0),
        pixelValue(x1, z0),
        tx
    );

    const b = lerp(
        pixelValue(x0, z1),
        pixelValue(x1, z1),
        tx
    );

    return lerp(
        terrain.minHeight,
        terrain.maxHeight,
        lerp(a, b, tz)
    );
}


function terrainColor(height, slope, worldX, worldZ) {
    const t = terrain;

    const elevation = clamp(
        (height - t.minHeight) /
        (t.maxHeight - t.minHeight),
        0,
        1
    );

    const slopeDegrees =
        Math.atan(slope) * 180 / Math.PI;

    const seed = 91827;

    const moisture = fractalNoise2D(
        worldX * 0.0025,
        worldZ * 0.0025,
        seed + 1000,
        3
    ) * 0.7 + fractalNoise2D(
        worldX * 0.009,
        worldZ * 0.009,
        seed + 3000,
        3
    ) * 0.3;

    const geology = fractalNoise2D(
        worldX * 0.00375,
        worldZ * 0.00375,
        seed + 2000,
        3
    );

    const variation = fractalNoise2D(
        worldX * 0.025,
        worldZ * 0.025,
        seed + 5000,
        3
    );

    const exposure = clamp(
        slopeDegrees / 42 * 0.78 +
        elevation * 0.12 +
        (1 - moisture) * 0.10,
        0,
        1
    );

    const soilType = soilTypeAt(worldX, worldZ);

    const soilColors = {
        clay:        [0.48, 0.29, 0.21],
        sand:        [0.92, 0.73, 0.39],
        fertileSoil: [0.43, 0.29, 0.18],
        loam:        [0.35, 0.25, 0.16],
        organic:     [0.19, 0.13, 0.085],
        scree:       [0.43, 0.41, 0.37]
    };

    const soil = soilColors[soilType];
    let bedrock;

    if (geology < 0.28) {
        bedrock = [0.63, 0.60, 0.51];
    } else if (geology < 0.48) {
        bedrock = [0.65, 0.43, 0.29];
    } else if (geology > 0.84) {
        bedrock = [0.24, 0.23, 0.22];
    } else {
        bedrock = [0.39, 0.37, 0.34];
    }

    if (elevation > 0.70 && geology > 0.65) {
        bedrock = [0.39, 0.37, 0.34];
    }

    // Blend surface material into exposed bedrock.
    let color = mixColor(
        soil,
        bedrock,
        clamp((exposure - 0.25) / 0.65)
    );

    // Small-scale geological surface variation.
    const texture = 0.92 + variation * 0.16;

    color = color.map(value =>
        clamp(value * texture, 0, 1)
    );

    // Snow and frost at the highest elevations.
    if (elevation > 0.92) {
        const snow = [0.82, 0.84, 0.82];

        color = mixColor(
            color,
            snow,
            clamp((elevation - 0.92) / 0.08)
        );
    }

    return color;
}





function generateMesh(job) {
    const {
        x,
        z,
        size,
        lod,
        neighborLODs
    } = job;

    const resolution = [32, 16, 8, 4][
        clamp(lod, 0, 3)
    ];

    const stride = size / resolution;

    const count = (resolution + 1) ** 2;

    const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
    const normals = new Float32Array(count * 3);
    const colors = new Float32Array(count * 4);

    const indices = new Uint32Array(
        resolution * resolution * 6
    );

    const originX = x * size;
    const originZ = z * size;

    const edgeHeight = (edge, index) => {
        const otherLOD = neighborLODs[edge];

        const worldDistance = index * stride;

        if (otherLOD <= lod) {
            return edge === "north" || edge === "south"
                ? heightAt(
                    originX + worldDistance,
                    originZ + (edge === "south" ? size : 0)
                )
                : heightAt(
                    originX + (edge === "east" ? size : 0),
                    originZ + worldDistance
                );
        }

        const coarseResolution = [32, 16, 8, 4][
            clamp(otherLOD, 0, 3)
        ];

        const coarseStride = size / coarseResolution;

        const lower =
            Math.floor(worldDistance / coarseStride) *
            coarseStride;

        const upper = Math.min(
            lower + coarseStride,
            size
        );

        const t = upper === lower
            ? 0
            : (worldDistance - lower) / (upper - lower);

        let ax, az, bx, bz;

        if (edge === "north" || edge === "south") {
            az = bz = originZ +
                (edge === "south" ? size : 0);

            ax = originX + lower;
            bx = originX + upper;
        } else {
            ax = bx = originX +
                (edge === "east" ? size : 0);

            az = originZ + lower;
            bz = originZ + upper;
        }

        return lerp(
            heightAt(ax, az),
            heightAt(bx, bz),
            t
        );
    };

    for (let iz = 0; iz <= resolution; iz++) {
        for (let ix = 0; ix <= resolution; ix++) {
            const worldX = originX + ix * stride;
            const worldZ = originZ + iz * stride;

            let h = heightAt(worldX, worldZ);

            if (iz === 0) h = edgeHeight("north", ix);
            if (iz === resolution) h = edgeHeight("south", ix);
            if (ix === 0) h = edgeHeight("west", iz);
            if (ix === resolution) h = edgeHeight("east", iz);

            const spacing = Math.max(2, stride * 0.25);

            const dx = (
                heightAt(worldX + spacing, worldZ) -
                heightAt(worldX - spacing, worldZ)
            ) / (2 * spacing);

            const dz = (
                heightAt(worldX, worldZ + spacing) -
                heightAt(worldX, worldZ - spacing)
            ) / (2 * spacing);

            const length = Math.hypot(dx, 1, dz);

            const index = iz * (resolution + 1) + ix;
            const offset = index * 3;

            positions[offset] = worldX;
            positions[offset + 1] = h;
            positions[offset + 2] = worldZ;

            normals[offset] = -dx / length;
            normals[offset + 1] = 1 / length;
            normals[offset + 2] = -dz / length;

		const color = terrainColor(
    h,
    Math.hypot(dx, dz),
    worldX,
    worldZ
);

            const colorOffset = index * 4;

            colors[colorOffset] = color[0];
            colors[colorOffset + 1] = color[1];
            colors[colorOffset + 2] = color[2];
            colors[colorOffset + 3] = 1;
        }
    }

    let cursor = 0;

    for (let iz = 0; iz < resolution; iz++) {
        for (let ix = 0; ix < resolution; ix++) {
            const a = iz * (resolution + 1) + ix;
            const b = a + 1;
            const c = a + resolution + 1;
            const d = c + 1;

            indices[cursor++] = a;
            indices[cursor++] = c;
            indices[cursor++] = b;

            indices[cursor++] = b;
            indices[cursor++] = c;
            indices[cursor++] = d;
        }
    }

    return {
        positions,
        normals,
        colors,
        indices
    };
}

self.onmessage = event => {
    const message = event.data;

    if (message.type === "init") {
        terrain = {
            ...message.terrain,
            pixels: new Uint8ClampedArray(message.pixels)
        };

        self.postMessage({ type: "ready" });
        return;
    }

    if (message.type === "generate" && terrain) {
        try {
            const data = generateMesh(message.job);

            self.postMessage({
                type: "complete",
                id: message.id,
                revision: message.revision,
                data: {
                    positions: data.positions,
                    normals: data.normals,
                    colors: data.colors,
                    indices: data.indices
                }
            }, [
                data.positions.buffer,
                data.normals.buffer,
                data.colors.buffer,
                data.indices.buffer
            ]);
        } catch (error) {
            self.postMessage({
                type: "error",
                id: message.id,
                revision: message.revision,
                message: error.message
            });
        }
    }
};

function soilTypeAt(worldX, worldZ) {
    const t = terrain;

    const u = clamp(
        (worldX - t.minX) / t.worldSize,
        0,
        1
    );

    const v = clamp(
        (worldZ - t.minZ) / t.worldSize,
        0,
        1
    );

    const mapValue = pixelValue(
        u * (t.width - 1),
        v * (t.height - 1)
    );

    if (mapValue < 0.18) return "clay";
    if (mapValue < 0.38) return "sand";
    if (mapValue < 0.58) return "fertileSoil";
    if (mapValue < 0.76) return "loam";
    if (mapValue < 0.90) return "organic";

    return "scree";
}

FILE: src/world/world-chunk.js

export class WorldChunk {
    constructor(x, z, size, terrain, scene) {
        this.x = x;
        this.z = z;
        this.size = size;
        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.scene = scene;

        this.mesh = null;
        this.lod = -1;
        this.loaded = false;
        this.disposed = false;

        this.revision = 0;

        this.neighbors = {
            north: null,
            south: null,
            east: null,
            west: null
        };
    }

    get originX() {
        return this.x * this.size;
    }

    get originZ() {
        return this.z * this.size;
    }

    getResolution(lod) {
        return [32, 16, 8, 4][
            BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(lod, 0, 3)
        ];
    }

    setNeighbors(neighbors) {
        this.neighbors = neighbors;
    }

	
applyMeshData(data, lod, material) {
    if (this.disposed) return;

    const vertexData = new BABYLON.VertexData();

    vertexData.positions = Array.from(data.positions);
    vertexData.normals = Array.from(data.normals);
    vertexData.colors = Array.from(data.colors);
    vertexData.indices = Array.from(data.indices);

    if (this.mesh) {
        this.mesh.dispose();
    }

    this.mesh = new BABYLON.Mesh(
        `terrain_chunk_${this.x}_${this.z}`,
        this.scene
    );

    vertexData.applyToMesh(this.mesh, true);

    this.mesh.material = material;

    material.useVertexColors = true;
    material.diffuseColor.set(1, 1, 1);

    this.mesh.useVertexColors = true;
    this.mesh.hasVertexAlpha = false;

    if (
        !this.mesh.isVerticesDataPresent(
            BABYLON.VertexBuffer.ColorKind
        )
    ) {
        console.error(
            "[WorldChunk] Terrain vertex colors missing:",
            this.x,
            this.z
        );
    }

    // IMPORTANT:
    // Terrain must be pickable for architectural editing.

    this.mesh.isPickable = true;

    this.mesh.metadata = {
        chunkX: this.x,
        chunkZ: this.z,
        lod,

        // Explicit identifier for editor terrain picking.
        terrainChunk: true
    };

    // Apply existing spatial transformation.
    this.scene.metadata?.geometryRenderer?.applyMesh(
        this.mesh
    );

    this.lod = lod;
    this.loaded = true;

    console.log(
        "[WorldChunk] Terrain mesh ready for picking:",
        {
            name: this.mesh.name,
            pickable: this.mesh.isPickable,
            terrainChunk: this.mesh.metadata.terrainChunk,
            vertices: this.mesh.getTotalVertices()
        }
    );
}


    generateMesh(lod, material) {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        const resolution = this.getResolution(lod);
        const stride = this.size / resolution;

        const positions = [];
        const indices = [];
        const normals = [];
        const colors = [];

        const minX = this.terrain.min.x;
        const maxX = this.terrain.max.x;
        const minZ = this.terrain.min.z;
        const maxZ = this.terrain.max.z;

        const heightAt = (x, z) =>
            this.terrain.sampleHeight(
                BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(x, minX, maxX),
                BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(z, minZ, maxZ)
            );

        for (let z = 0; z <= resolution; z++) {
            for (let x = 0; x <= resolution; x++) {
                const wx = this.originX + x * stride;
                const wz = this.originZ + z * stride;

                const h = heightAt(wx, wz);

                positions.push(wx, h, wz);

                const slope = this.terrain.sampleSlopeAt(
                    wx, wz, Math.max(2, stride * 0.25)
                );

                const normal = slope?.normal ??
                    new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 1, 0);

                normals.push(
                    normal.x, normal.y, normal.z
                );

                const color = this.getTerrainColor(
                    h,
                    slope?.magnitude ?? 0
                );

                colors.push(
                    color.r, color.g, color.b, 1
                );
            }
        }

        for (let z = 0; z < resolution; z++) {
            for (let x = 0; x < resolution; x++) {
                const a = z * (resolution + 1) + x;
                const b = a + 1;
                const c = a + resolution + 1;
                const d = c + 1;

                indices.push(a, c, b, b, c, d);
            }
        }

        this.applyMeshData({
            positions,
            normals,
            colors,
            indices
        }, lod, material);
    }

    getTerrainColor(height, slope) {
        const elevation = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            (height - this.terrain.minHeight) /
            (this.terrain.maxHeight - this.terrain.minHeight),
            0, 1
        );

        const low = new BABYLON.Color3(0.24, 0.38, 0.19);
        const grass = new BABYLON.Color3(0.42, 0.48, 0.25);
        const mountain = new BABYLON.Color3(0.48, 0.45, 0.39);
        const snow = new BABYLON.Color3(0.82, 0.84, 0.82);

        let color;

        if (elevation < 0.35) {
            color = BABYLON.Color3.Lerp(
                low, grass, elevation / 0.35
            );
        } else if (elevation < 0.78) {
            color = BABYLON.Color3.Lerp(
                grass, mountain, (elevation - 0.35) / 0.43
            );
        } else {
            color = BABYLON.Color3.Lerp(
                mountain, snow, (elevation - 0.78) / 0.22
            );
        }

        return BABYLON.Color3.Lerp(
            color,
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.32, 0.30, 0.28),
            BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(slope / 0.9, 0, 0.45)
        );
    }

    dispose() {
        this.revision++;

        if (this.mesh) {
            this.mesh.dispose();
            this.mesh = null;
        }

        this.loaded = false;
        this.disposed = true;
    }
}


========================================================================

import { WorldChunk } from "./world-chunk.js";

export class WorldManager {
    constructor(scene, terrain, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.terrain = terrain;

	this.fallbackScheduled = false;

        this.chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 2000;
        this.renderRadius = options.renderRadius ?? 14;
        this.highDetailRadius = options.highDetailRadius ?? 2;

        this.maxChunksPerUpdate =
            options.maxChunksPerUpdate ?? 6;

        this.chunks = new Map();

        this.enabled = true;

        this.stats = {
            generated: 0,
            unloaded: 0,
            loaded: 0,
            workerCompleted: 0,
            workerErrors: 0,
            pendingMeshes: 0
        };

        this.minChunkX = Math.floor(
            terrain.min.x / this.chunkSize
        );

        this.maxChunkX = Math.ceil(
            terrain.max.x / this.chunkSize
        ) - 1;

        this.minChunkZ = Math.floor(
            terrain.min.z / this.chunkSize
        );

        this.maxChunkZ = Math.ceil(
            terrain.max.z / this.chunkSize
        ) - 1;

this.material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
    "worldTerrainMaterial",
    scene
);

this.material.diffuseColor = new BABYLON.Color3(
    1.0, 1.0, 1.0
);

this.material.ambientColor = new BABYLON.Color3(
    1, 1, 1
);

// Preserve vertex colors, but allow actual illumination.
this.material.useVertexColors = true;
this.material.disableLighting = false;

// Small nighttime visibility contribution.
this.material.emissiveColor = new BABYLON.Color3(
    0.1, 0.1, 0.1
);

this.material.backFaceCulling = false;

this.material.specularColor = BABYLON.Color3.Black();
this.material.roughness = 1.0;
	
        this.meshQueue = [];
        this.worker = null;
        this.workerReady = false;
        this.workerBusy = false;
        this.nextTaskId = 1;

        this.initializeWorker();
    }

    getKey(x, z) {
        return `${x},${z}`;
    }

    getChunk(x, z) {
        return this.chunks.get(this.getKey(x, z)) ?? null;
    }

    getCoordinates(position) {
        return {
            x: Math.floor(position.x / this.chunkSize),
            z: Math.floor(position.z / this.chunkSize)
        };
    }

    isInsideWorld(x, z) {
        return (
            x >= this.minChunkX &&
            x <= this.maxChunkX &&
            z >= this.minChunkZ &&
            z <= this.maxChunkZ
        );
    }

	initializeWorker() {
    if (typeof Worker === "undefined") {
        console.warn(
            "[WorldManager] Workers unavailable. Using fallback."
        );
        return;
    }

    try {
        this.worker = new Worker(
            new URL("./terrain-mesh-worker.js", import.meta.url),
            { type: "module" }
        );

        this.worker.onmessage = event => {
            const message = event.data;

            if (message.type === "ready") {
                if (this.workerInitTimer) {
                    clearTimeout(this.workerInitTimer);
                    this.workerInitTimer = null;
                }

                this.workerReady = true;

                console.log(
                    "[WorldManager] Terrain worker ready."
                );

                this.pumpWorker();
                return;
            }

            this.handleWorkerMessage(message);
        };

        this.worker.onerror = error => {
            this.failWorker(error);
        };

        this.worker.onmessageerror = error => {
            this.failWorker(error);
        };

        const pixels = this.terrain.pixels.slice();

        this.worker.postMessage({
            type: "init",
            terrain: {
                width: this.terrain.width,
                height: this.terrain.height,
                worldSize: this.terrain.worldSize,
                minX: this.terrain.min.x,
                minZ: this.terrain.min.z,
                minHeight: this.terrain.minHeight,
                maxHeight: this.terrain.maxHeight,
                heightChannel:
                    this.terrain.heightChannel ?? "average"
            },
            pixels: pixels.buffer
        }, [pixels.buffer]);

        this.workerInitTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            if (!this.workerReady && this.worker) {
                this.failWorker(
                    "Worker initialization timed out."
                );
            }
        }, 5000);

    } catch (error) {
        this.failWorker(error);
    }
}

    handleWorkerMessage(message) {
        if (message.type === "ready") {
            this.workerReady = true;
            this.pumpWorker();
            return;
        }

        if (
            message.type === "complete" ||
            message.type === "error"
        ) {
            const task = this.currentTask;

            this.workerBusy = false;
            this.currentTask = null;

            if (
                task &&
                task.id === message.id
            ) {
                const chunk = this.chunks.get(task.key);

                if (
                    chunk &&
                    !chunk.disposed &&
                    chunk.revision === message.revision
                ) {
                    if (message.type === "complete") {
                        chunk.applyMeshData(
                            message.data,
                            task.lod,
                            this.material
                        );

                        this.stats.workerCompleted++;
                    } else {
                        this.stats.workerErrors++;

                        console.error(
                            "[WorldManager] Mesh generation failed:",
                            message.message
                        );

                        chunk.generateMesh(
                            task.lod,
                            this.material
                        );
                    }
                }
            }

            this.pumpWorker();
        }
    }

    scheduleMesh(chunk, lod) {
        if (!chunk || chunk.disposed) return;

        const neighbors = this.getNeighbors(chunk);

        chunk.setNeighbors(neighbors);
        chunk.lod = lod;
        chunk.revision++;

        const job = {
            id: this.nextTaskId++,
            key: this.getKey(chunk.x, chunk.z),
            revision: chunk.revision,
            lod,

            x: chunk.x,
            z: chunk.z,
            size: chunk.size,

            neighborLODs: {
                north: neighbors.north?.lod ?? lod,
                south: neighbors.south?.lod ?? lod,
                east: neighbors.east?.lod ?? lod,
                west: neighbors.west?.lod ?? lod
            }
        };

        this.meshQueue = this.meshQueue.filter(
            item => item.key !== job.key
        );

        this.meshQueue.push(job);

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;

        this.pumpWorker();
    }

    pumpWorker() {
        if (
            !this.worker ||
            !this.workerReady ||
            this.workerBusy ||
            this.meshQueue.length === 0
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const job = this.meshQueue.shift();

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;

        const chunk = this.chunks.get(job.key);

        if (
            !chunk ||
            chunk.disposed ||
            chunk.revision !== job.revision
        ) {
            this.pumpWorker();
            return;
        }

        this.currentTask = job;
        this.workerBusy = true;

        this.worker.postMessage({
            type: "generate",
            id: job.id,
            revision: job.revision,
            job
        });
    }

	processFallbackQueue() {
    if (
        this.fallbackScheduled ||
        this.meshQueue.length === 0 ||
        !this.enabled
    ) {
        return;
    }

    this.fallbackScheduled = true;

    setTimeout(() => {
        this.fallbackScheduled = false;

        const job = this.meshQueue.shift();

        if (job) {
            const chunk = this.chunks.get(job.key);

            if (
                chunk &&
                !chunk.disposed &&
                chunk.revision === job.revision
            ) {
                try {
                    chunk.generateMesh(
                        job.lod,
                        this.material
                    );
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error(
                        "[WorldManager] Synchronous terrain generation failed:",
                        error
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;
        this.processFallbackQueue();

    }, 0);
}

    getNeighbors(chunk) {
        return {
            north: this.getChunk(chunk.x, chunk.z - 1),
            south: this.getChunk(chunk.x, chunk.z + 1),
            east: this.getChunk(chunk.x + 1, chunk.z),
            west: this.getChunk(chunk.x - 1, chunk.z)
        };
    }

    rebuildChunk(chunk) {
        this.scheduleMesh(
            chunk,
            chunk.lod < 0 ? 3 : chunk.lod
        );
    }

    createChunk(x, z, lod) {
        if (!this.isInsideWorld(x, z)) return null;

        const chunk = new WorldChunk(
            x,
            z,
            this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain,
            this.scene
        );

        this.chunks.set(
            this.getKey(x, z),
            chunk
        );

        this.stats.generated++;
        this.stats.loaded++;

        this.scheduleMesh(chunk, lod);

        // Existing neighbors may need new boundary stitching.
        for (const neighbor of Object.values(
            this.getNeighbors(chunk)
        )) {
            if (neighbor) {
                this.rebuildChunk(neighbor);
            }
        }

        return chunk;
    }

    getLOD(x, z, cameraPosition) {
        const centerX = (x + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;
        const centerZ = (z + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;

        const distance = Math.hypot(
            centerX - cameraPosition.x,
            centerZ - cameraPosition.z
        );

if (
    distance <=
    this.chunkSize * (this.highDetailRadius + 0.5)
) return 0;

if (
    distance <=
    this.chunkSize * (this.highDetailRadius + 2)
) return 1;

if (distance <= this.chunkSize * 8) return 2;

        return 3;
    }

    getPriority(x, z, cameraPosition, forward) {
        const centerX = (x + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;
        const centerZ = (z + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;

        const dx = centerX - cameraPosition.x;
        const dz = centerZ - cameraPosition.z;

        const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dz);

        const length = Math.max(distance, 1);

        const directionDot = (
            dx / length * forward.x +
            dz / length * forward.z
        );

        // Lower scores are generated first.
        return distance -
            Math.max(0, directionDot) *
            this.chunkSize * 4;
    }

    update(cameraPosition, forward = null) {
        if (!this.enabled || !this.terrain.ready) return;

        const center = this.getCoordinates(cameraPosition);

        const direction = forward ?? {
            x: 0,
            z: 1
        };

        const required = [];

        for (
            let x = Math.max(
                this.minChunkX,
                center.x - this.renderRadius
            );
            x <= Math.min(
                this.maxChunkX,
                center.x + this.renderRadius
            );
            x++
        ) {
            for (
                let z = Math.max(
                    this.minChunkZ,
                    center.z - this.renderRadius
                );
                z <= Math.min(
                    this.maxChunkZ,
                    center.z + this.renderRadius
                );
                z++
            ) {
                const dx = x - center.x;
                const dz = z - center.z;

                if (
                    dx * dx + dz * dz >
                    this.renderRadius * this.renderRadius
                ) continue;

                required.push({
                    x,
                    z,
                    priority: this.getPriority(
                        x,
                        z,
                        cameraPosition,
                        direction
                    ),
                    lod: this.getLOD(
                        x,
                        z,
                        cameraPosition
                    )
                });
            }
        }

        required.sort(
            (a, b) => a.priority - b.priority
        );

        let scheduled = 0;

        for (const item of required) {
            const chunk = this.getChunk(item.x, item.z);

            if (!chunk) {
                if (scheduled >= this.maxChunksPerUpdate) {
                    continue;
                }

                this.createChunk(
                    item.x,
                    item.z,
                    item.lod
                );

                scheduled++;
                continue;
            }

            if (chunk.lod !== item.lod) {
                if (scheduled >= this.maxChunksPerUpdate) {
                    continue;
                }

                this.scheduleMesh(chunk, item.lod);
                scheduled++;
            }
        }

        const unloadRadius = this.renderRadius + 1;

        for (const [key, chunk] of this.chunks) {
            const dx = chunk.x - center.x;
            const dz = chunk.z - center.z;

            if (
                dx * dx + dz * dz >
                unloadRadius * unloadRadius
            ) {
                chunk.dispose();
                this.chunks.delete(key);

                this.meshQueue = this.meshQueue.filter(
                    task => task.key !== key
                );

                this.stats.unloaded++;
                this.stats.loaded--;
            }
        }

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;

        if (!this.worker) {
            this.processFallbackQueue();
        } else {
            this.pumpWorker();
        }
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            ...this.stats,
            currentChunks: this.chunks.size,
            chunkSize: this.chunkSize,
            renderRadius: this.renderRadius,
            worldSize: this.terrain.worldSize,
            workerActive: !!this.worker,
            workerReady: this.workerReady
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        this.enabled = false;

        if (this.worker) {
            this.worker.terminate();
            this.worker = null;
        }

        for (const chunk of this.chunks.values()) {
            chunk.dispose();
        }

        this.chunks.clear();
        this.meshQueue.length = 0;

        this.material.dispose();

        this.stats.loaded = 0;
        this.stats.pendingMeshes = 0;
    }

failWorker(reason) {
    console.error(
        "[WorldManager] Worker failure:",
        reason
    );

    this.stats.workerErrors++;

    if (this.workerInitTimer) {
        clearTimeout(this.workerInitTimer);
        this.workerInitTimer = null;
    }

    const failedTask = this.currentTask;
    this.currentTask = null;

    if (this.worker) {
        this.worker.terminate();
        this.worker = null;
    }

    this.workerReady = false;
    this.workerBusy = false;

    // Retry the outstanding task synchronously.
    if (failedTask) {
        const chunk = this.chunks.get(failedTask.key);

        if (
            chunk &&
            !chunk.disposed &&
            chunk.revision === failedTask.revision
        ) {
            this.meshQueue.unshift(failedTask);
        }
    }

    this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;
    this.processFallbackQueue();
}
}
